==================== FILE: src/components/organiser/AccountModal.tsx ====================
     1	import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef, useCallback } from 'react';
     2	import { createPortal } from 'react-dom';
     3	import {
     4	  X,
     5	  User,
     6	  Copy,
     7	  Check,
     8	  Trash2,
     9	  RotateCcw,
    10	  CheckCircle2,
    11	  Lock,
    12	  Eye,
    13	  EyeOff,
    14	  LogOut,
    15	  AlertTriangle,
    16	  Pencil,
    17	  Clock,
    18	  XCircle,
    19	  Send,
    20	  Mail,
    21	  MessageCircle,
    22	  Coffee,
    23	  ExternalLink,
    24	  Download,
    25	  Upload,
    26	  ChevronDown,
    27	  ShieldAlert,
    28	} from 'lucide-react';
    29	import { Organization } from '../../lib/supabaseTypes';
    30	import { dbService, supabase } from '../../lib/supabase';
    31	import { eventBus } from '../../lib/eventBus';
    32	import { useToast } from '../common/ToastProvider';
    33	import { BackupSheet } from './backup/BackupSheet';
    34	import { RestoreSheet } from './backup/RestoreSheet';
    35	import { triggerHaptic } from '../../lib/haptics';
    36	import { BrandBadge } from '../shared/BrandBadge';
    37	import { syncNow } from '../../lib/syncEngine';
    38	
    39	interface AccountModalProps {
    40	  isOpen: boolean;
    41	  onClose: () => void;
    42	  organization: Organization;
    43	  onUpdateOrganization: (updated: Organization) => void;
    44	  onLogout: () => void;
    45	  mustChangePassword?: boolean;
    46	  onPasswordChanged?: () => void;
    47	}
    48	
    49	export function AccountModal({
    50	  isOpen,
    51	  onClose,
    52	  organization,
    53	  onUpdateOrganization,
    54	  onLogout,
    55	  mustChangePassword = false,
    56	  onPasswordChanged,
    57	}: AccountModalProps) {
    58	  const [copiedOrgId, setCopiedOrgId] = useState(false);
    59	  const { showToast } = useToast();
    60	
    61	  // Organization Name Inline Edit State
    62	  const [isEditingName, setIsEditingName] = useState(false);
    63	  const [editName, setEditName] = useState(organization.name);
    64	  const [isSavingName, setIsSavingName] = useState(false);
    65	
    66	  // Organization Email Inline Edit State
    67	  const [isEditingEmail, setIsEditingEmail] = useState(false);
    68	  const [editEmail, setEditEmail] = useState(organization.email || '');
    69	  const [isSavingEmail, setIsSavingEmail] = useState(false);
    70	
    71	  // Master Password Update State
    72	  const [isUpdatingPassword, setIsUpdatingPassword] = useState(false);
    73	  const [currentPassword, setCurrentPassword] = useState('');
    74	  const [newPassword, setNewPassword] = useState('');
    75	  const [confirmPassword, setConfirmPassword] = useState('');
    76	  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
    77	  const [isSavingPassword, setIsSavingPassword] = useState(false);
    78	
    79	  // Double-Confirmation Sign Out Modal State (C2)
    80	  const [showLogoutConfirm, setShowLogoutConfirm] = useState(false);
    81	
    82	  // Danger Zone Shutter & Confirmation States (C8)
    83	  const [isDangerShutterOpen, setIsDangerShutterOpen] = useState(false);
    84	  const [showResetConfirm, setShowResetConfirm] = useState(false);
    85	  const [resetInput, setResetInput] = useState('');
    86	  const [resetSuccess, setResetSuccess] = useState(false);
    87	  const [showDeleteConfirm, setShowDeleteConfirm] = useState(false);
    88	  const [deleteInput, setDeleteInput] = useState('');
    89	  const [isProcessingDanger, setIsProcessingDanger] = useState(false);
    90	
    91	  // Backup & Restore Sheet State
    92	  const [showBackupSheet, setShowBackupSheet] = useState(false);
    93	  const [showRestoreSheet, setShowRestoreSheet] = useState(false);
    94	
    95	  const closeBtnRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);
    96	  const previousFocusRef = useRef<HTMLElement | null>(null);
    97	  const wasOpenRef = useRef(false);
    98	
    99	  const verificationStatus = organization.verification_status || 'pending';
   100	
   101	  // Keep internal edit form synchronized with organization prop
   102	  useEffect(() => {
   103	    setEditName(organization.name);
   104	    setEditEmail(organization.email || '');
   105	  }, [organization]);
   106	
   107	  // Expand password reset automatically when temporary password is active
   108	  useEffect(() => {
   109	    if (isOpen && mustChangePassword) {
   110	      setIsUpdatingPassword(true);
   111	    }
   112	  }, [isOpen, mustChangePassword]);
   113	
   114	  // Reset transient shutter & confirmation states whenever modal closes (C8)
   115	  useEffect(() => {
   116	    if (!isOpen) {
   117	      setIsDangerShutterOpen(false);
   118	      setShowLogoutConfirm(false);
   119	      setShowResetConfirm(false);
   120	      setResetSuccess(false);
   121	      setShowDeleteConfirm(false);
   122	      setResetInput('');
   123	      setDeleteInput('');
   124	      setIsEditingName(false);
   125	      setIsEditingEmail(false);
   126	    }
   127	  }, [isOpen]);
   128	
   129	  const handleCloseModal = useCallback(() => {
   130	    setIsDangerShutterOpen(false);
   131	    setShowLogoutConfirm(false);
   132	    setShowResetConfirm(false);
   133	    setResetSuccess(false);
   134	    setShowDeleteConfirm(false);
   135	    onClose();
   136	  }, [onClose]);
   137	
   138	  // Modal Scroll Lock, Focus Restoration & Escape Handling
   139	  useEffect(() => {
   140	    if (!isOpen) {
   141	      if (wasOpenRef.current) {
   142	        wasOpenRef.current = false;
   143	        previousFocusRef.current?.focus();
   144	        previousFocusRef.current = null;
   145	      }
   146	      return;
   147	    }
   148	
   149	    wasOpenRef.current = true;
   150	    previousFocusRef.current =
   151	      typeof document !== 'undefined' ? (document.activeElement as HTMLElement | null) : null;
   152	
   153	    const originalOverflow = document.body.style.overflow;
   154	    document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
   155	
   156	    const focusTimer = window.setTimeout(() => {
   157	      closeBtnRef.current?.focus();
   158	    }, 20);
   159	
   160	    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
   161	      if (e.key !== 'Escape') return;
   162	      // Let BackupSheet / RestoreSheet handle their own Escape when open
   163	      if (showBackupSheet || showRestoreSheet) return;
   164	
   165	      e.preventDefault();
   166	      if (showDeleteConfirm) {
   167	        setShowDeleteConfirm(false);
   168	        setDeleteInput('');
   169	        return;
   170	      }
   171	      if (showResetConfirm) {
   172	        setShowResetConfirm(false);
   173	        setResetSuccess(false);
   174	        setResetInput('');
   175	        return;
   176	      }
   177	      if (showLogoutConfirm) {
   178	        setShowLogoutConfirm(false);
   179	        return;
   180	      }
   181	      handleCloseModal();
   182	    };
   183	
   184	    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
   185	    return () => {
   186	      window.clearTimeout(focusTimer);
   187	      window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
   188	      document.body.style.overflow = originalOverflow;
   189	    };
   190	  }, [
   191	    isOpen,
   192	    showBackupSheet,
   193	    showRestoreSheet,
   194	    showDeleteConfirm,
   195	    showResetConfirm,
   196	    showLogoutConfirm,
   197	    handleCloseModal,
   198	  ]);
   199	
   200	  if (!isOpen) return null;
   201	
   202	  const handleCopyOrgId = async () => {
   203	    triggerHaptic('tick');
   204	    try {
   205	      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(organization.org_id);
   206	    } catch {
   207	      try {
   208	        const el = document.createElement('textarea');
   209	        el.value = organization.org_id;
   210	        el.style.position = 'fixed';
   211	        el.style.opacity = '0';
   212	        document.body.appendChild(el);
   213	        el.select();
   214	        document.execCommand('copy');
   215	        document.body.removeChild(el);
   216	      } catch {
   217	        showToast('Could not copy Org ID.');
   218	        return;
   219	      }
   220	    }
   221	    setCopiedOrgId(true);
   222	    showToast('Org ID copied.');
   223	    setTimeout(() => setCopiedOrgId(false), 1500);
   224	  };
   225	
   226	  // 1. Save Organization Name
   227	  const handleSaveName = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
   228	    e.preventDefault();
   229	    const trimmed = editName.trim();
   230	    if (!trimmed) {
   231	      showToast('Organization name cannot be empty.');
   232	      return;
   233	    }
   234	    setIsSavingName(true);
   235	    try {
   236	      const updated = await dbService.updateOrganization(organization.org_id, {
   237	        name: trimmed,
   238	      });
   239	      onUpdateOrganization(updated);
   240	      setIsEditingName(false);
   241	      showToast('Organization name updated.');
   242	    } catch (err: any) {
   243	      showToast(`Update failed: ${err.message || 'Unknown error'}`);
   244	    } finally {
   245	      setIsSavingName(false);
   246	    }
   247	  };
   248	
   249	  // 2. Save Organization Email (resets verification_status to 'pending' if changed)
   250	  const handleSaveEmail = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
   251	    e.preventDefault();
   252	    const trimmed = editEmail.trim();
   253	    if (trimmed && !/^\S+@\S+\.\S+$/.test(trimmed)) {
   254	      showToast('Please enter a valid email address.');
   255	      return;
   256	    }
   257	    setIsSavingEmail(true);
   258	    try {
   259	      const emailChanged = trimmed.toLowerCase() !== (organization.email || '').toLowerCase();
   260	      const updated = await dbService.updateOrganization(organization.org_id, {
   261	        email: trimmed || undefined,
   262	        ...(emailChanged ? { verification_status: 'pending' } : {}),
   263	      });
   264	      onUpdateOrganization(updated);
   265	      setIsEditingEmail(false);
   266	      showToast(emailChanged ? 'Email updated. Sent for verification.' : 'Contact email saved.');
   267	    } catch (err: any) {
   268	      showToast(`Update failed: ${err.message || 'Unknown error'}`);
   269	    } finally {
   270	      setIsSavingEmail(false);
   271	    }
   272	  };
   273	
   274	  // 3. Save Master Password
   275	  const handleSavePassword = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
   276	    e.preventDefault();
   277	    if (typeof navigator !== 'undefined' && !navigator.onLine) {
   278	      showToast('Needs internet to update password.');
   279	      return;
   280	    }
   281	    if (!currentPassword) {
   282	      showToast('Current password is required.');
   283	      return;
   284	    }
   285	    if (!newPassword || newPassword.length < 6) {
   286	      showToast('Min 6 characters required.');
   287	      return;
   288	    }
   289	    if (newPassword !== confirmPassword) {
   290	      showToast('Passwords do not match.');
   291	      return;
   292	    }
   293	    setIsSavingPassword(true);
   294	    try {
   295	      const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc('org_change_password', {
   296	        p_org_id: organization.org_id,
   297	        p_old: currentPassword.trim(),
   298	        p_new: newPassword.trim(),
   299	      });
   300	      if (error) throw error;
   301	      const res = data as any;
   302	      if (!res || !res.ok) {
   303	        const errCode = res?.error || res?.reason;
   304	        if (errCode === 'too_many_attempts') {
   305	          showToast('Too many attempts. Try again in 15 minutes.');
   306	          return;
   307	        }
   308	        if (errCode === 'weak_password') {
   309	          showToast('New password is too short (minimum 6 characters).');
   310	          return;
   311	        }
   312	        showToast('Current password is wrong.');
   313	        return;
   314	      }
   315	      showToast('Master password updated.');
   316	      onPasswordChanged?.();
   317	      setIsUpdatingPassword(false);
   318	      setCurrentPassword('');
   319	      setNewPassword('');
   320	      setConfirmPassword('');
   321	    } catch (err: any) {
   322	      const msg = err.message || '';
   323	      if (msg.includes('too_many_attempts')) {
   324	        showToast('Too many attempts. Try again in 15 minutes.');
   325	      } else if (msg.includes('invalid') || msg.includes('wrong')) {
   326	        showToast('Current password is wrong.');
   327	      } else {
   328	        showToast(`Password update failed: ${msg || 'Error'}`);
   329	      }
   330	    } finally {
   331	      setIsSavingPassword(false);
   332	    }
   333	  };
   334	
   335	  // Danger Zone: Reset Data
   336	  const handleExecuteReset = async () => {
   337	    if (resetInput.trim().toUpperCase() !== 'RESET') {
   338	      showToast("Type 'RESET' to confirm.");
   339	      return;
   340	    }
   341	    setIsProcessingDanger(true);
   342	    try {
   343	      await dbService.resetOrganizationData(organization.org_id);
   344	      setResetSuccess(true);
   345	      setResetInput('');
   346	      setIsDangerShutterOpen(false);
   347	      showToast('Reset complete. All competition data has been cleared.');
   348	      void syncNow({ forceFull: true, reason: 'manual' });
   349	    } catch (err: any) {
   350	      if (err?.code === 'ROSTER_LOCKED' || err?.message?.includes('ROSTER_LOCKED')) {
   351	        showToast('Unlock your events first.');
   352	      } else {
   353	        showToast(`Reset failed: ${err.message}`);
   354	      }
   355	    } finally {
   356	      setIsProcessingDanger(false);
   357	    }
   358	  };
   359	
   360	  // Danger Zone: Delete Account
   361	  const handleExecuteDelete = async () => {
   362	    if (deleteInput.trim().toUpperCase() !== 'DELETE') {
   363	      showToast("Type 'DELETE' to confirm.");
   364	      return;
   365	    }
   366	    setIsProcessingDanger(true);
   367	    try {
   368	      await dbService.deleteOrganization(organization.org_id);
   369	      setShowDeleteConfirm(false);
   370	      setDeleteInput('');
   371	      handleCloseModal();
   372	      onLogout();
   373	    } catch (err: any) {
   374	      if (err?.code === 'ROSTER_LOCKED' || err?.message?.includes('ROSTER_LOCKED')) {
   375	        showToast('Unlock your events first.');
   376	      } else {
   377	        showToast(`Delete failed: ${err.message}`);
   378	      }
   379	    } finally {
   380	      setIsProcessingDanger(false);
   381	    }
   382	  };
   383	
   384	  const modalContent = (
   385	    <div
   386	      className="fixed inset-0 z-[100] flex items-center justify-center p-3 sm:p-4"
   387	      role="dialog"
   388	      aria-modal="true"
   389	      aria-labelledby="account-modal-title"
   390	    >
   391	      {/* Solid dark scrim — no backdrop-filter (C1) */}
   392	      <div
   393	        onClick={handleCloseModal}
   394	        className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/60 transition-opacity duration-200 motion-reduce:transition-none"
   395	      />
   396	
   397	      {/* Modal Dialog matching Settings Sheet tokens (C1) */}
   398	      <div className="relative w-full max-w-lg bg-stone-50 dark:bg-zinc-950 text-zinc-900 dark:text-white border border-stone-200 dark:border-zinc-800 rounded-3xl shadow-2xl flex flex-col max-h-[90vh] overflow-hidden animate-spring-sheet select-none">
   399	        {/* Modal Header */}
   400	        <div className="px-5 py-4 shrink-0 flex items-center justify-between border-b border-stone-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-950">
   401	          <div className="flex items-center space-x-3 min-w-0">
   402	            <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 border border-emerald-500/20 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
   403	              <User className="w-5 h-5 stroke-[2.2]" />
   404	            </div>
   405	            <div className="min-w-0">
   406	              <div className="flex items-center space-x-1.5">
   407	                <h2
   408	                  id="account-modal-title"
   409	                  className="text-base font-bold text-zinc-900 dark:text-white leading-tight"
   410	                >
   411	                  Account &amp; Security
   412	                </h2>
   413	                {verificationStatus === 'verified' && (
   414	                  <span title="Verified Organization" className="inline-flex">
   415	                    <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-500 shrink-0" />
   416	                  </span>
   417	                )}
   418	              </div>
   419	              <p className="text-[11px] text-zinc-500 font-mono truncate">
   420	                {organization.name}
   421	              </p>
   422	            </div>
   423	          </div>
   424	
   425	          <button
   426	            ref={closeBtnRef}
   427	            type="button"
   428	            onClick={handleCloseModal}
   429	            className="w-11 h-11 flex items-center justify-center text-zinc-500 hover:text-zinc-900 dark:hover:text-white rounded-xl bg-stone-100 dark:bg-zinc-900 border border-stone-200 dark:border-zinc-800 transition-colors cursor-pointer shrink-0"
   430	            aria-label="Close modal"
   431	          >
   432	            <X className="w-4 h-4" />
   433	          </button>
   434	        </div>
   435	
   436	        {/* Modal Body: Solid Inset Grouped Cards */}
   437	        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-4 py-3 space-y-1 bg-stone-100 dark:bg-black overscroll-contain pb-16">
   438	          {/* ======================================================== */}
   439	          {/* TOP: SIGN OUT ATTACHED TO ORGANIZATION IDENTITY (C2)     */}
   440	          {/* ======================================================== */}
   441	          <div className="mt-2 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-950 rounded-2xl border border-stone-200 dark:border-zinc-800 overflow-hidden shadow-sm divide-y divide-stone-100 dark:divide-zinc-800/60">
   442	            {/* Padded Sign Out Button matching Change Password shape & padding (C2) */}
   443	            <div className="p-4">
   444	              <button
   445	                type="button"
   446	                onClick={() => {
   447	                  triggerHaptic('tick');
   448	                  setShowLogoutConfirm(true);
   449	                }}
   450	                className="w-full min-h-[44px] px-4 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition-colors cursor-pointer flex items-center justify-center space-x-2 bg-rose-700 hover:bg-rose-800 dark:bg-rose-700/90 dark:hover:bg-rose-700 text-white shadow-sm group"
   451	              >
   452	                <LogOut className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-white transition-transform group-hover:translate-x-0.5 motion-reduce:transition-none" />
   453	                <span>Sign out</span>
   454	              </button>
   455	            </div>
   456	
   457	            {/* Organization Identity Section Header (attached directly below Sign Out) */}
   458	            <div className="px-4 py-2 bg-stone-50 dark:bg-zinc-900/60">
   459	              <h4 className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-500">
   460	                Organization Identity
   461	              </h4>
   462	            </div>
   463	
   464	            {/* Row 1: Organization Name — pencil to the right, NO "Host Entity" (C3) */}
   465	            <div className="p-4 space-y-2">
   466	              {!isEditingName ? (
   467	                <div className="space-y-1">
   468	                  <span className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-500 block">
   469	                    Organization Name
   470	                  </span>
   471	                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2.5">
   472	                    <p className="text-sm sm:text-base font-bold text-zinc-900 dark:text-white break-words min-w-0 flex-1">
   473	                      {organization.name}
   474	                    </p>
   475	                    <button
   476	                      type="button"
   477	                      onClick={() => {
   478	                        setEditName(organization.name);
   479	                        setIsEditingName(true);
   480	                      }}
   481	                      aria-label="Edit organisation name"
   482	                      title="Edit organisation name"
   483	                      className="w-11 h-11 rounded-xl bg-stone-100 dark:bg-zinc-900 hover:bg-stone-200 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 border border-stone-200 dark:border-zinc-800 flex items-center justify-center transition-colors cursor-pointer shrink-0"
   484	                    >
   485	                      <Pencil className="w-4 h-4 text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400" />
   486	                    </button>
   487	                  </div>
   488	                </div>
   489	              ) : (
   490	                <form onSubmit={handleSaveName} className="space-y-3 pt-1 animate-in fade-in duration-150">
   491	                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
   492	                    <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 uppercase tracking-wider">
   493	                      Edit Organization Name
   494	                    </span>
   495	                  </div>
   496	
   497	                  <input
   498	                    type="text"
   499	                    autoFocus
   500	                    required
   501	                    placeholder="e.g. Cambridge International Festival"
   502	                    value={editName}
   503	                    onChange={(e) => setEditName(e.target.value)}
   504	                    className="w-full min-h-[44px] px-3.5 bg-stone-50 dark:bg-zinc-900 border border-stone-200 dark:border-zinc-800 rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold text-zinc-900 dark:text-white placeholder-zinc-400 focus:outline-none focus:border-emerald-500"
   505	                  />
   506	
   507	                  <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 pt-0.5">
   508	                    <button
   509	                      type="button"
   510	                      onClick={() => {
   511	                        setEditName(organization.name);
   512	                        setIsEditingName(false);
   513	                      }}
   514	                      disabled={isSavingName}
   515	                      className="min-h-[44px] px-3 bg-stone-100 dark:bg-zinc-900 hover:bg-stone-200 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 text-xs font-semibold rounded-xl transition-colors cursor-pointer"
   516	                    >
   517	                      Cancel
   518	                    </button>
   519	                    <button
   520	                      type="submit"
   521	                      disabled={isSavingName || !editName.trim()}
   522	                      className="min-h-[44px] px-3 bg-emerald-700 hover:bg-emerald-800 dark:bg-emerald-700/90 dark:hover:bg-emerald-700 disabled:opacity-50 text-white text-xs font-bold rounded-xl transition-colors shadow-sm flex items-center justify-center space-x-1.5 cursor-pointer"
   523	                    >
   524	                      {isSavingName ? <span>Saving...</span> : <span>Save Name</span>}
   525	                    </button>
   526	                  </div>
   527	                </form>
   528	              )}
   529	            </div>
   530	
   531	            {/* Row 2: Email — heading "Email", verified tick only (no "Verified" text), pencil right of heading, NO tick next to email ID, NO mail button (C4) */}
   532	            <div className="p-4 space-y-2.5">
   533	              {!isEditingEmail ? (
   534	                <div className="space-y-1.5">
   535	                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
   536	                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 min-w-0">
   537	                      <span className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-500">
   538	                        Email
   539	                      </span>
   540	                      {verificationStatus === 'verified' && (
   541	                        <CheckCircle2
   542	                          className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-500 shrink-0"
   543	                          aria-label="Verified"
   544	                        />
   545	                      )}
   546	                      {verificationStatus === 'pending' && (
   547	                        <Clock
   548	                          className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-500 shrink-0"
   549	                          aria-label="Under Verification"
   550	                        />
   551	                      )}
   552	                      {verificationStatus === 'rejected' && (
   553	                        <XCircle
   554	                          className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-red-500 shrink-0"
   555	                          aria-label="Verification Failed"
   556	                        />
   557	                      )}
   558	                    </div>
   559	
   560	                    <button
   561	                      type="button"
   562	                      onClick={() => {
   563	                        setEditEmail(organization.email || '');
   564	                        setIsEditingEmail(true);
   565	                      }}
   566	                      aria-label={organization.email ? 'Edit email' : 'Add email'}
   567	                      title={organization.email ? 'Edit email' : 'Add email'}
   568	                      className="w-11 h-11 rounded-xl bg-stone-100 dark:bg-zinc-900 hover:bg-stone-200 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 border border-stone-200 dark:border-zinc-800 flex items-center justify-center transition-colors cursor-pointer shrink-0"
   569	                    >
   570	                      <Pencil className="w-4 h-4 text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400" />
   571	                    </button>
   572	                  </div>
   573	
   574	                  <p className="text-sm font-semibold font-mono text-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-200 break-all">
   575	                    {organization.email || (
   576	                      <span className="text-zinc-400 italic">No email configured</span>
   577	                    )}
   578	                  </p>
   579	
   580	                  {/* Pending Verification Info Note */}
   581	                  {verificationStatus === 'pending' && (
   582	                    <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-amber-500/10 border border-amber-500/25 text-[11px] text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-300 leading-relaxed flex items-start space-x-2 mt-2">
   583	                      <AlertTriangle className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-500 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
   584	                      <span>
   585	                        Your organization and email are currently under manual review by the Security Team. Unverified or invalid accounts may be removed if rejected.
   586	                      </span>
   587	                    </div>
   588	                  )}
   589	
   590	                  {/* Rejected Verification Alert with Developer Contact & Telegram */}
   591	                  {verificationStatus === 'rejected' && (
   592	                    <div className="p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-red-500/10 border border-red-500/30 space-y-2.5 mt-2">
   593	                      <div className="flex items-start space-x-2 text-xs text-red-600 dark:text-red-400 font-semibold leading-relaxed">
   594	                        <XCircle className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0 mt-0.5 text-red-500" />
   595	                        <span>
   596	                          {organization.rejection_reason
   597	                            ? `Verification rejected: ${organization.rejection_reason}`
   598	                            : 'Email verification failed. Please update your email or contact the Developer / Security Team immediately before account removal:'}
   599	                        </span>
   600	                      </div>
   601	                      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 pt-0.5">
   602	                        <a
   603	                          href="mailto:spesium.code@proton.me"
   604	                          className="inline-flex items-center space-x-1.5 min-h-[44px] px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 border border-red-500/30 text-[11px] font-mono font-bold text-red-600 dark:text-red-400 hover:bg-red-500/10 transition-colors"
   605	                        >
   606	                          <Mail className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
   607	                          <span>spesium.code@proton.me</span>
   608	                        </a>
   609	                        <a
   610	                          href="https://t.me/+ljTOKLi5dQs1MTM9"
   611	                          target="_blank"
   612	                          rel="noopener noreferrer"
   613	                          className="inline-flex items-center space-x-1.5 min-h-[44px] px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-blue-500/15 border border-blue-500/30 text-[11px] font-bold text-blue-600 dark:text-blue-400 hover:bg-blue-500/25 transition-colors"
   614	                        >
   615	                          <Send className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
   616	                          <span>Join Telegram Support</span>
   617	                        </a>
   618	                      </div>
   619	                    </div>
   620	                  )}
   621	                </div>
   622	              ) : (
   623	                <form onSubmit={handleSaveEmail} className="space-y-3 pt-1 animate-in fade-in duration-150">
   624	                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
   625	                    <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 uppercase tracking-wider">
   626	                      Update Email
   627	                    </span>
   628	                  </div>
   629	
   630	                  <input
   631	                    type="email"
   632	                    autoFocus
   633	                    required
   634	                    placeholder="e.g. director@fairjudge.io"
   635	                    value={editEmail}
   636	                    onChange={(e) => setEditEmail(e.target.value)}
   637	                    className="w-full min-h-[44px] px-3.5 bg-stone-50 dark:bg-zinc-900 border border-stone-200 dark:border-zinc-800 rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold text-zinc-900 dark:text-white placeholder-zinc-400 focus:outline-none focus:border-emerald-500"
   638	                  />
   639	
   640	                  <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 pt-0.5">
   641	                    <button
   642	                      type="button"
   643	                      onClick={() => {
   644	                        setEditEmail(organization.email || '');
   645	                        setIsEditingEmail(false);
   646	                      }}
   647	                      disabled={isSavingEmail}
   648	                      className="min-h-[44px] px-3 bg-stone-100 dark:bg-zinc-900 hover:bg-stone-200 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 text-xs font-semibold rounded-xl transition-colors cursor-pointer"
   649	                    >
   650	                      Cancel
   651	                    </button>
   652	                    <button
   653	                      type="submit"
   654	                      disabled={isSavingEmail || !editEmail.trim()}
   655	                      className="min-h-[44px] px-3 bg-emerald-700 hover:bg-emerald-800 dark:bg-emerald-700/90 dark:hover:bg-emerald-700 disabled:opacity-50 text-white text-xs font-bold rounded-xl transition-colors shadow-sm flex items-center justify-center space-x-1.5 cursor-pointer"
   656	                    >
   657	                      {isSavingEmail ? <span>Saving...</span> : <span>Save Email</span>}
   658	                    </button>
   659	                  </div>
   660	                </form>
   661	              )}
   662	            </div>
   663	
   664	            {/* Row 3: Organization Code — one line, code on right, NO "Jury Login Key" (C5) */}
   665	            <div className="p-4 flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
   666	              <span className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-500 shrink-0">
   667	                Organization Code
   668	              </span>
   669	
   670	              <div className="flex items-center space-x-2 shrink-0 min-w-0">
   671	                <span className="text-sm font-mono font-bold tracking-wider text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 bg-emerald-500/10 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl border border-emerald-500/20 truncate">
   672	                  {organization.org_id}
   673	                </span>
   674	                <button
   675	                  type="button"
   676	                  onClick={handleCopyOrgId}
   677	                  aria-label={copiedOrgId ? 'Copied organisation code' : 'Copy organisation code'}
   678	                  title={copiedOrgId ? 'Copied' : 'Copy Org ID'}
   679	                  className="w-11 h-11 rounded-xl bg-stone-100 dark:bg-zinc-900 text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 hover:text-zinc-900 dark:hover:text-white border border-stone-200 dark:border-zinc-800 flex items-center justify-center transition-colors cursor-pointer shrink-0"
   680	                >
   681	                  {copiedOrgId ? (
   682	                    <Check className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-500" />
   683	                  ) : (
   684	                    <Copy className="w-4 h-4 text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400" />
   685	                  )}
   686	                </button>
   687	              </div>
   688	            </div>
   689	          </div>
   690	
   691	          {/* ======================================================== */}
   692	          {/* CARD 2: SECURITY — BLUE "Change password" BTN (C6)       */}
   693	          {/* ======================================================== */}
   694	          <div>
   695	            <h4 className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-500 ml-4 mb-2 mt-6">
   696	              Password
   697	            </h4>
   698	            <div className="bg-white dark:bg-zinc-950 rounded-2xl border border-stone-200 dark:border-zinc-800 overflow-hidden shadow-sm p-4">
   699	              <button
   700	                type="button"
   701	                onClick={() => setIsUpdatingPassword(!isUpdatingPassword)}
   702	                className={`w-full min-h-[44px] px-4 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition-colors cursor-pointer flex items-center justify-center space-x-2 ${
   703	                  isUpdatingPassword
   704	                    ? 'bg-stone-100 dark:bg-zinc-900 hover:bg-stone-200 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-200 border border-stone-200 dark:border-zinc-800'
   705	                    : 'bg-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-800 dark:bg-blue-700/90 dark:hover:bg-blue-700 text-white shadow-sm'
   706	                }`}
   707	              >
   708	                <Lock className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
   709	                <span>{isUpdatingPassword ? 'Cancel' : 'Change password'}</span>
   710	              </button>
   711	
   712	              {/* Password Update Form (Inline Expandable) */}
   713	              {isUpdatingPassword && (
   714	                <form onSubmit={handleSavePassword} className="mt-3 pt-3 border-t border-stone-100 dark:border-zinc-800/60 space-y-2.5 animate-in fade-in duration-150">
   715	                  {mustChangePassword && (
   716	                    <div className="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-amber-500/10 border border-amber-500/30 text-[11px] font-medium text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-300 flex items-start gap-2">
   717	                      <AlertTriangle className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
   718	                      <span>Temporary password active — please set a new master password now.</span>
   719	                    </div>
   720	                  )}
   721	                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
   722	                    <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-zinc-500 uppercase tracking-wider">
   723	                      New Password
   724	                    </span>
   725	                    <button
   726	                      type="button"
   727	                      onClick={() => setShowPassword(!showPassword)}
   728	                      className="min-h-[44px] px-2 text-[11px] text-zinc-500 hover:text-zinc-700 dark:hover:text-zinc-300 flex items-center space-x-1 cursor-pointer"
   729	                    >
   730	                      {showPassword ? <EyeOff className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> : <Eye className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />}
   731	                      <span>{showPassword ? 'Hide' : 'Show'}</span>
   732	                    </button>
   733	                  </div>
   734	
   735	                  <input
   736	                    type={showPassword ? 'text' : 'password'}
   737	                    placeholder="Current password"
   738	                    value={currentPassword}
   739	                    onChange={(e) => setCurrentPassword(e.target.value)}
   740	                    required
   741	                    className="w-full min-h-[44px] px-3.5 bg-stone-50 dark:bg-zinc-900 border border-stone-200 dark:border-zinc-800 rounded-xl text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-amber-500"
   742	                  />
   743	
   744	                  <input
   745	                    type={showPassword ? 'text' : 'password'}
   746	                    placeholder="New password (minimum 6 characters)"
   747	                    value={newPassword}
   748	                    onChange={(e) => setNewPassword(e.target.value)}
   749	                    required
   750	                    className="w-full min-h-[44px] px-3.5 bg-stone-50 dark:bg-zinc-900 border border-stone-200 dark:border-zinc-800 rounded-xl text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-amber-500"
   751	                  />
   752	
   753	                  <input
   754	                    type={showPassword ? 'text' : 'password'}
   755	                    placeholder="Confirm new password"
   756	                    value={confirmPassword}
   757	                    onChange={(e) => setConfirmPassword(e.target.value)}
   758	                    required
   759	                    className="w-full min-h-[44px] px-3.5 bg-stone-50 dark:bg-zinc-900 border border-stone-200 dark:border-zinc-800 rounded-xl text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-amber-500"
   760	                  />
   761	
   762	                  <button
   763	                    type="submit"
   764	                    disabled={isSavingPassword || !currentPassword || !newPassword || newPassword !== confirmPassword}
   765	                    className="w-full min-h-[44px] bg-emerald-700 hover:bg-emerald-800 dark:bg-emerald-700/90 dark:hover:bg-emerald-700 disabled:opacity-50 text-white text-xs font-bold rounded-xl transition-colors active:scale-[0.98] motion-reduce:transition-none cursor-pointer shadow-sm flex items-center justify-center space-x-1.5"
   766	                  >
   767	                    <Lock className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
   768	                    <span>{isSavingPassword ? 'Saving Password...' : 'Save New Password'}</span>
   769	                  </button>
   770	                </form>
   771	              )}
   772	            </div>
   773	          </div>
   774	
   775	          {/* ======================================================== */}
   776	          {/* CARD 3: BACKUP & RESTORE — SIDE BY SIDE, NO HINTS (C7)   */}
   777	          {/* ======================================================== */}
   778	          <div>
   779	            <h4 className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-500 ml-4 mb-2 mt-6">
   780	              Backup &amp; Restore
   781	            </h4>
   782	            <div className="bg-white dark:bg-zinc-950 rounded-2xl border border-stone-200 dark:border-zinc-800 overflow-hidden shadow-sm p-3.5">
   783	              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2.5">
   784	                <button
   785	                  type="button"
   786	                  onClick={() => setShowBackupSheet(true)}
   787	                  className="min-h-[44px] px-3.5 rounded-xl bg-emerald-700 hover:bg-emerald-800 dark:bg-emerald-700/90 dark:hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white text-xs font-bold transition-colors cursor-pointer flex items-center justify-center space-x-1.5 shadow-sm"
   788	                >
   789	                  <Download className="w-4 h-4" />
   790	                  <span>Backup</span>
   791	                </button>
   792	
   793	                <button
   794	                  type="button"
   795	                  onClick={() => setShowRestoreSheet(true)}
   796	                  className="min-h-[44px] px-3.5 rounded-xl bg-amber-700 hover:bg-amber-800 dark:bg-amber-700/90 dark:hover:bg-amber-700 text-white text-xs font-bold transition-colors cursor-pointer flex items-center justify-center space-x-1.5 shadow-sm"
   797	                >
   798	                  <Upload className="w-4 h-4" />
   799	                  <span>Restore</span>
   800	                </button>
   801	              </div>
   802	            </div>
   803	          </div>
   804	
   805	          {/* ======================================================== */}
   806	          {/* CARD 4: ABOUT & SUPPORT                                  */}
   807	          {/* ======================================================== */}
   808	          <div>
   809	            <h4 className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-500 ml-4 mb-2 mt-6">
   810	              About &amp; Support
   811	            </h4>
   812	            <div className="bg-white dark:bg-zinc-950 rounded-2xl border border-stone-200 dark:border-zinc-800 overflow-hidden shadow-sm divide-y divide-stone-100 dark:divide-zinc-800/60">
   813	              {/* Brand Anchor */}
   814	              <div className="p-4 flex items-center justify-between">
   815	                <div className="flex items-center space-x-3">
   816	                  <BrandBadge size="sm" role="organiser" motion="still" />
   817	                  <div>
   818	                    <span className="text-sm font-bold text-zinc-900 dark:text-white block font-sans">
   819	                      Fair Judge
   820	                    </span>
   821	                    <span className="text-[10px] text-zinc-500 uppercase tracking-widest font-mono">
   822	                      Audited Scoring Terminal
   823	                    </span>
   824	                  </div>
   825	                </div>
   826	              </div>
   827	
   828	              {/* Telegram Community */}
   829	              <a
   830	                href="https://t.me/+ljTOKLi5dQs1MTM9"
   831	                target="_blank"
   832	                rel="noopener noreferrer"
   833	                className="min-h-[44px] p-4 flex items-center justify-between hover:bg-stone-50 dark:hover:bg-zinc-900/40 transition-colors group cursor-pointer"
   834	              >
   835	                <div className="flex items-center space-x-3">
   836	                  <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-blue-500/10 border border-blue-500/20 text-blue-500 dark:text-blue-400 flex items-center justify-center group-hover:scale-105 transition-transform motion-reduce:transition-none">
   837	                    <MessageCircle className="w-4 h-4" />
   838	                  </div>
   839	                  <div>
   840	                    <span className="text-xs font-bold text-zinc-900 dark:text-white block group-hover:text-blue-500 dark:group-hover:text-blue-400 transition-colors">
   841	                      Join our Telegram Community
   842	                    </span>
   843	                    <span className="text-[10px] text-zinc-500">
   844	                      Product updates, live support &amp; feedback
   845	                    </span>
   846	                  </div>
   847	                </div>
   848	                <ExternalLink className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-zinc-400 group-hover:text-blue-500 dark:group-hover:text-blue-400 transition-colors" />
   849	              </a>
   850	
   851	              {/* Ko-fi Developer Support */}
   852	              <a
   853	                href="https://ko-fi.com/sahilkumarrout"
   854	                target="_blank"
   855	                rel="noopener noreferrer"
   856	                className="min-h-[44px] p-4 flex items-center justify-between hover:bg-stone-50 dark:hover:bg-zinc-900/40 transition-colors group cursor-pointer"
   857	              >
   858	                <div className="flex items-center space-x-3">
   859	                  <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-amber-500/10 border border-amber-500/20 text-amber-500 flex items-center justify-center group-hover:scale-105 transition-transform motion-reduce:transition-none">
   860	                    <Coffee className="w-4 h-4" />
   861	                  </div>
   862	                  <div>
   863	                    <span className="text-xs font-bold text-zinc-900 dark:text-white block group-hover:text-amber-500 dark:group-hover:text-amber-400 transition-colors">
   864	                      Support the Developer
   865	                    </span>
   866	                    <span className="text-[10px] text-zinc-500">
   867	                      Fuel development via Ko-fi
   868	                    </span>
   869	                  </div>
   870	                </div>
   871	                <ExternalLink className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-zinc-400 group-hover:text-amber-500 dark:group-hover:text-amber-400 transition-colors" />
   872	              </a>
   873	            </div>
   874	          </div>
   875	
   876	          {/* ======================================================== */}
   877	          {/* CARD 5: DANGER ZONE WITH TAP-TO-OPEN SHUTTER (C8)        */}
   878	          {/* ======================================================== */}
   879	          <div>
   880	            <h4 className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-red-600/80 dark:text-red-400/80 ml-4 mb-2 mt-6">
   881	              Danger Zone
   882	            </h4>
   883	            <div className="relative bg-white dark:bg-zinc-950 rounded-2xl border border-red-200 dark:border-red-950 overflow-hidden shadow-sm">
   884	              {/* Top-Centre Shutter Handle Button */}
   885	              <button
   886	                type="button"
   887	                onClick={() => {
   888	                  triggerHaptic('tick');
   889	                  setIsDangerShutterOpen((prev) => !prev);
   890	                }}
   891	                aria-expanded={isDangerShutterOpen}
   892	                aria-controls="danger-zone-panel"
   893	                aria-label={isDangerShutterOpen ? 'Close Danger Zone shutter' : 'Open Danger Zone shutter'}
   894	                className="relative z-20 w-full min-h-[44px] px-4 flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 bg-stone-50 dark:bg-zinc-900 border-b border-red-100 dark:border-red-950/60 hover:bg-stone-100 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800/80 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
   895	              >
   896	                <span className="w-9 h-1 rounded-full bg-red-400/70 dark:bg-red-500/60" />
   897	                <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[10px] font-mono font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-red-600 dark:text-red-400">
   898	                  <ShieldAlert className="w-3 h-3" />
   899	                  <span>{isDangerShutterOpen ? 'Tap to lock Danger Zone' : 'Tap to unlock Danger Zone'}</span>
   900	                  <ChevronDown
   901	                    className={`w-3 h-3 transition-transform duration-200 motion-reduce:transition-none ${
   902	                      isDangerShutterOpen ? 'rotate-180' : 'rotate-0'
   903	                    }`}
   904	                  />
   905	                </span>
   906	              </button>
   907	
   908	              {/* Danger Zone Controls + Smooth Shutter Overlay */}
   909	              <div className="relative overflow-hidden">
   910	                <div
   911	                  id="danger-zone-panel"
   912	                  aria-hidden={!isDangerShutterOpen}
   913	                  data-open={isDangerShutterOpen ? 'true' : 'false'}
   914	                  className="p-4 space-y-2.5 fj-danger-panel"
   915	                >
   916	                  <button
   917	                    type="button"
   918	                    disabled={!isDangerShutterOpen}
   919	                    tabIndex={isDangerShutterOpen ? 0 : -1}
   920	                    onClick={() => {
   921	                      setResetSuccess(false);
   922	                      setResetInput('');
   923	                      setShowResetConfirm(true);
   924	                    }}
   925	                    className="w-full min-h-[44px] px-4 rounded-xl bg-red-700 hover:bg-red-800 dark:bg-red-700/90 dark:hover:bg-red-700 disabled:pointer-events-none text-white text-xs font-bold transition-colors cursor-pointer flex items-center justify-center space-x-2 shadow-sm"
   926	                  >
   927	                    <RotateCcw className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
   928	                    <span>Reset Data</span>
   929	                  </button>
   930	
   931	                  <button
   932	                    type="button"
   933	                    disabled={!isDangerShutterOpen}
   934	                    tabIndex={isDangerShutterOpen ? 0 : -1}
   935	                    onClick={() => setShowDeleteConfirm(true)}
   936	                    className="w-full min-h-[44px] px-4 rounded-xl bg-red-700 hover:bg-red-800 dark:bg-red-700/90 dark:hover:bg-red-700 disabled:pointer-events-none text-white text-xs font-bold transition-colors cursor-pointer flex items-center justify-center space-x-2 shadow-sm"
   937	                  >
   938	                    <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
   939	                    <span>Delete Organization</span>
   940	                  </button>
   941	                </div>
   942	
   943	                {/* Protected Shutter Sheet (NO backdrop-filter, smooth GPU slide up/down + reveal) */}
   944	                <div
   945	                  aria-hidden="true"
   946	                  data-open={isDangerShutterOpen ? 'true' : 'false'}
   947	                  className={`absolute inset-0 z-10 bg-stone-200/95 dark:bg-zinc-900/95 flex items-center justify-center fj-danger-shutter ${
   948	                    isDangerShutterOpen ? 'pointer-events-none' : 'pointer-events-auto'
   949	                  }`}
   950	                >
   951	                  <div className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-white dark:bg-zinc-950 border border-red-200 dark:border-red-900/60 shadow-sm flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px] font-mono font-bold text-red-600 dark:text-red-400">
   952	                    <Lock className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
   953	                    <span>Protected</span>

  1184	              />

  1190	                onClick={() => {

  1192	                  setDeleteInput('');

  1195	              >

  1197	              </button>
  1198	              <button
  1199	                type="button"
  1200	                onClick={handleExecuteDelete}

  1202	                className="min-h-[44px] bg-red-700 hover:bg-red-800 dark:bg-red-700/90 dark:hover:bg-red-700 disabled:opacity-40 text-white text-xs font-bold rounded-xl shadow-sm transition-colors cursor-pointer flex items-center justify-center space-x-1.5"
  1203	              >
  1204	                {isProcessingDanger ? <span>Deleting...</span> : <span>Confirm Delete</span>}
  1205	              </button>
  1206	            </div>
  1207	          </div>
  1208	        </div>
  1209	      )}
  1210	
  1211	      {/* Backup and Restore Modals */}
  1212	      <BackupSheet
  1213	        isOpen={showBackupSheet}
  1214	        onClose={() => setShowBackupSheet(false)}
  1215	        organization={organization}
  1216	      />
  1217	      <RestoreSheet
  1218	        isOpen={showRestoreSheet}
  1219	        onClose={() => setShowRestoreSheet(false)}
  1220	        organization={organization}
  1221	        onRestoreComplete={() => {
  1222	          eventBus.emit('cache_updated', { table: 'all' });
  1223	          if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
  1224	            window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('fairjudge_session_change'));
  1225	          }
  1226	        }}
  1227	      />
  1228	    </div>
  1229	  );
  1230	
  1231	  return typeof document !== 'undefined' ? createPortal(modalContent, document.body) : modalContent;
  1232	}

==================== FILE: src/components/common/ToastProvider.tsx ====================
     1	import React, {
     2	  createContext,
     3	  useContext,
     4	  useState,
     5	  useEffect,
     6	  useCallback,
     7	  useRef,
     8	  ReactNode,
     9	} from 'react';
    10	import { CheckCircle2, AlertTriangle, AlertCircle, Info } from 'lucide-react';
    11	import { eventBus, ToastPayload } from '../../lib/eventBus';
    12	
    13	export type ToastType = 'success' | 'warning' | 'info' | 'error';
    14	
    15	export interface ToastItem {
    16	  id: string;
    17	  type: ToastType;
    18	  message: string;
    19	  duration: number;
    20	}
    21	
    22	export interface ToastContextValue {
    23	  showToast: (message: string, type?: ToastType, duration?: number) => void;
    24	  toast: {
    25	    success: (message: string, duration?: number) => void;
    26	    error: (message: string, duration?: number) => void;
    27	    warning: (message: string, duration?: number) => void;
    28	    info: (message: string, duration?: number) => void;
    29	  };
    30	}
    31	
    32	const ToastContext = createContext<ToastContextValue | null>(null);
    33	
    34	/**
    35	 * Global showToast function that dispatches via eventBus.
    36	 * Accessible from anywhere in the application (inside or outside React components).
    37	 */
    38	export function showToast(
    39	  message: string,
    40	  type: ToastType = 'success',
    41	  duration: number = 3200
    42	) {
    43	  eventBus.emit('toast', {
    44	    id: `toast_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 7)}`,
    45	    type,
    46	    title: message,
    47	    message,
    48	    duration,
    49	  });
    50	}
    51	
    52	export const toast = {
    53	  success: (msg: string, d?: number) => showToast(msg, 'success', d),
    54	  error: (msg: string, d?: number) => showToast(msg, 'error', d),
    55	  warning: (msg: string, d?: number) => showToast(msg, 'warning', d),
    56	  info: (msg: string, d?: number) => showToast(msg, 'info', d),
    57	};
    58	
    59	export function useToast(): ToastContextValue {
    60	  const context = useContext(ToastContext);
    61	  if (context) return context;
    62	  return {
    63	    showToast,
    64	    toast,
    65	  };
    66	}
    67	
    68	interface StackedToastCardProps {
    69	  item: ToastItem;
    70	  index: number;
    71	  total: number;
    72	  onDismiss: (id: string) => void;
    73	}
    74	
    75	/**
    76	 * ColorOS-Grade GPU-Accelerated Stacked Toast Item with Native Touch Swipe-to-Dismiss
    77	 */
    78	function StackedToastCard({ item, index, onDismiss }: StackedToastCardProps) {
    79	  const [dragX, setDragX] = useState(0);
    80	  const [isDragging, setIsDragging] = useState(false);
    81	  const [dismissDirection, setDismissDirection] = useState<'left' | 'right' | null>(null);
    82	
    83	  const startXRef = useRef(0);
    84	  const currentXRef = useRef(0);
    85	  const isPointerDownRef = useRef(false);
    86	
    87	  // Stacking calculation (Phase 14.6 ColorOS Stack Specification)
    88	  // index 0 (front): scale 1.00, translateY 0px, opacity 1.0, zIndex 30
    89	  // index 1: scale 0.95, translateY -12px, opacity 0.78, zIndex 20
    90	  // index 2: scale 0.90, translateY -24px, opacity 0.52, zIndex 10
    91	  const offsetY = -index * 12;
    92	  const scale = Math.max(0.85, 1 - index * 0.05);
    93	  const baseOpacity = Math.max(0.4, 1 - index * 0.25);
    94	  const zIndex = 30 - index * 10;
    95	  const isFront = index === 0;
    96	
    97	  // Touch Handlers (Mobile Native)
    98	  const handleTouchStart = (e: React.TouchEvent) => {
    99	    if (!isFront) return;
   100	    startXRef.current = e.touches[0].clientX;
   101	    currentXRef.current = e.touches[0].clientX;
   102	    isPointerDownRef.current = true;
   103	    setIsDragging(true);
   104	  };
   105	
   106	  const handleTouchMove = (e: React.TouchEvent) => {
   107	    if (!isPointerDownRef.current || !isFront) return;
   108	    currentXRef.current = e.touches[0].clientX;
   109	    const delta = currentXRef.current - startXRef.current;
   110	    setDragX(delta);
   111	  };
   112	
   113	  const handleTouchEnd = () => {
   114	    if (!isPointerDownRef.current || !isFront) return;
   115	    isPointerDownRef.current = false;
   116	    setIsDragging(false);
   117	
   118	    // 80px swipe threshold
   119	    if (Math.abs(dragX) > 80) {
   120	      const dir = dragX > 0 ? 'right' : 'left';
   121	      setDismissDirection(dir);
   122	      setTimeout(() => {
   123	        onDismiss(item.id);
   124	      }, 200);
   125	    } else {
   126	      // Spring back to 0
   127	      setDragX(0);
   128	    }
   129	  };
   130	
   131	  // Mouse Handlers (Desktop Fluidity)
   132	  const handleMouseDown = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
   133	    if (!isFront || e.button !== 0) return;
   134	    startXRef.current = e.clientX;
   135	    currentXRef.current = e.clientX;
   136	    isPointerDownRef.current = true;
   137	    setIsDragging(true);
   138	
   139	    const handleMouseMove = (moveEvent: MouseEvent) => {
   140	      if (!isPointerDownRef.current) return;
   141	      currentXRef.current = moveEvent.clientX;
   142	      const delta = currentXRef.current - startXRef.current;
   143	      setDragX(delta);
   144	    };
   145	
   146	    const handleMouseUp = () => {
   147	      isPointerDownRef.current = false;
   148	      setIsDragging(false);
   149	      window.removeEventListener('mousemove', handleMouseMove);
   150	      window.removeEventListener('mouseup', handleMouseUp);
   151	
   152	      if (Math.abs(currentXRef.current - startXRef.current) > 80) {
   153	        const dir = (currentXRef.current - startXRef.current) > 0 ? 'right' : 'left';
   154	        setDismissDirection(dir);
   155	        setTimeout(() => {
   156	          onDismiss(item.id);
   157	        }, 200);
   158	      } else {
   159	        setDragX(0);
   160	      }
   161	    };
   162	
   163	    window.addEventListener('mousemove', handleMouseMove);
   164	    window.addEventListener('mouseup', handleMouseUp);
   165	  };
   166	
   167	  // Color & Icon Taxonomy
   168	  const isError = item.type === 'error';
   169	  const isWarning = item.type === 'warning';
   170	  const isInfo = item.type === 'info';
   171	
   172	  const badgeTheme = isError
   173	    ? 'bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-400 border-rose-500/30 shadow-[0_0_12px_rgba(244,63,94,0.2)]'
   174	    : isWarning
   175	    ? 'bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-400 border-amber-500/30 shadow-[0_0_12px_rgba(245,158,11,0.2)]'
   176	    : isInfo
   177	    ? 'bg-sky-500/10 text-sky-400 border-sky-500/30 shadow-[0_0_12px_rgba(14,165,233,0.2)]'
   178	    : 'bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-400 border-emerald-500/30 shadow-[0_0_12px_rgba(16,185,129,0.2)]';
   179	
   180	  // Compute GPU translation & opacity
   181	  let translateXStyle = `${dragX}px`;
   182	  let currentOpacity = baseOpacity;
   183	
   184	  if (dismissDirection === 'right') {
   185	    translateXStyle = '120%';
   186	    currentOpacity = 0;
   187	  } else if (dismissDirection === 'left') {
   188	    translateXStyle = '-120%';
   189	    currentOpacity = 0;
   190	  } else if (isDragging && isFront) {
   191	    const fadeRatio = Math.max(0.2, 1 - Math.abs(dragX) / 220);
   192	    currentOpacity = baseOpacity * fadeRatio;
   193	  }
   194	
   195	  return (
   196	    <div
   197	      role="alert"
   198	      aria-live="polite"
   199	      onTouchStart={handleTouchStart}
   200	      onTouchMove={handleTouchMove}
   201	      onTouchEnd={handleTouchEnd}
   202	      onTouchCancel={handleTouchEnd}
   203	      onMouseDown={handleMouseDown}
   204	      style={{
   205	        transform: `translate3d(${translateXStyle}, ${offsetY}px, 0) scale(${scale})`,
   206	        opacity: currentOpacity,
   207	        zIndex,
   208	        pointerEvents: isFront ? 'auto' : 'none',
   209	        transition: isDragging
   210	          ? 'none'
   211	          : 'transform 260ms cubic-bezier(0.32, 0.72, 0, 1), opacity 200ms cubic-bezier(0.32, 0.72, 0, 1)',
   212	        willChange: 'transform, opacity',
   213	      }}
   214	      className={`absolute bottom-0 left-0 right-0 w-full max-w-sm mx-auto select-none rounded-2xl bg-zinc-900 dark:bg-zinc-950 border border-zinc-800 shadow-[0_16px_40px_rgba(0,0,0,0.7)] p-3.5 flex items-center gap-3.5 motion-reduce:transition-none ${
   215	        isFront ? 'cursor-grab active:cursor-grabbing hover:border-zinc-700' : 'cursor-default'
   216	      }`}
   217	    >
   218	      {/* Visual Accent Glowing Icon Pill */}
   219	      <div className={`w-8 h-8 rounded-xl border flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 ${badgeTheme}`}>
   220	        {isError ? (
   221	          <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 stroke-[2.2]" />
   222	        ) : isWarning ? (
   223	          <AlertTriangle className="w-4 h-4 stroke-[2.2]" />
   224	        ) : isInfo ? (
   225	          <Info className="w-4 h-4 stroke-[2.2]" />
   226	        ) : (
   227	          <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 stroke-[2.2]" />
   228	        )}
   229	      </div>
   230	
   231	      {/* Message Text with High Legibility Micro-Typography */}
   232	      <div className="flex-1 min-w-0 pr-1">
   233	        <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-100 dark:text-zinc-100 leading-snug break-words line-clamp-2">
   234	          {item.message}
   235	        </p>
   236	      </div>
   237	
   238	      {/* Front Toast Subtle Swipe Indicator Pill */}
   239	      {isFront && (
   240	        <div
   241	          onClick={(e) => {
   242	            e.stopPropagation();
   243	            onDismiss(item.id);
   244	          }}
   245	          title="Dismiss"
   246	          className="shrink-0 w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-zinc-800/60 hover:bg-zinc-700/80 text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-200 flex items-center justify-center text-[10px] cursor-pointer transition-colors active:scale-90"
   247	        >
   248	          ×
   249	        </div>
   250	      )}
   251	    </div>
   252	  );
   253	}
   254	
   255	/**
   256	 * Standardized Global ColorOS Stacked Toast Notification Center
   257	 * - Root container has absolute maximum z-index (z-[9999] pointer-events-none)
   258	 * - Fixed bottom floating placement (clears headers and sticky elements)
   259	 * - Holds at most 3 layered notifications with spring-physics transforms
   260	 */
   261	export function ToastContainer() {
   262	  const [toasts, setToasts] = useState<ToastItem[]>([]);
   263	
   264	  const handleDismiss = useCallback((id: string) => {
   265	    setToasts((prev) => prev.filter((t) => t.id !== id));
   266	  }, []);
   267	
   268	  useEffect(() => {
   269	    const unsub = eventBus.on('toast', (payload: ToastPayload) => {
   270	      const msg = payload.message || payload.title || '';
   271	      if (!msg) return;
   272	
   273	      const id = payload.id || `toast_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 7)}`;
   274	      const duration = payload.duration || 3200;
   275	      const type: ToastType = payload.type || 'success';
   276	
   277	      const newToast: ToastItem = {
   278	        id,
   279	        type,
   280	        message: msg,
   281	        duration,
   282	      };
   283	
   284	      setToasts((prev) => {
   285	        // Prevent duplicate spam of identical message
   286	        const filtered = prev.filter((t) => t.message !== msg);
   287	        // Prepend new toast and keep maximum 3 in the stack
   288	        return [newToast, ...filtered].slice(0, 3);
   289	      });
   290	
   291	      // Auto dismiss after duration
   292	      const timer = setTimeout(() => {
   293	        setToasts((current) => current.filter((t) => t.id !== id));
   294	      }, duration);
   295	
   296	      return () => clearTimeout(timer);
   297	    });
   298	
   299	    return unsub;
   300	  }, []);
   301	
   302	  if (toasts.length === 0) return null;
   303	
   304	  return (
   305	    <div
   306	      role="region"
   307	      aria-label="System Notifications"
   308	      className="fixed bottom-6 left-0 right-0 mx-auto w-full max-w-sm px-4 pointer-events-none z-[9999] h-20 flex items-end justify-center select-none"
   309	    >
   310	      {toasts.map((item, index) => (
   311	        <StackedToastCard
   312	          key={item.id}
   313	          item={item}
   314	          index={index}
   315	          total={toasts.length}
   316	          onDismiss={handleDismiss}
   317	        />
   318	      ))}
   319	    </div>
   320	  );
   321	}
   322	
   323	export function ToastProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
   324	  const handleShowToast = useCallback(
   325	    (message: string, type: ToastType = 'success', duration: number = 3200) => {
   326	      showToast(message, type, duration);
   327	    },
   328	    []
   329	  );
   330	
   331	  const contextValue: ToastContextValue = {
   332	    showToast: handleShowToast,
   333	    toast,
   334	  };
   335	
   336	  return (
   337	    <ToastContext.Provider value={contextValue}>
   338	      {children}
   339	      <ToastContainer />
   340	    </ToastContext.Provider>
   341	  );
   342	}

    36	export const READ_TIMEOUT_MS = 8000;
    37	
    38	export interface ReadResult<T> {
    39	  ok: boolean;
    40	  data: T;
    41	  fromCache: boolean;
    42	  error: string | null;
    43	  errorCode?: string | null;
    44	  timedOut: boolean;
    45	  notFound?: boolean;
    46	  unauthorized?: boolean;
    47	}
    48	
    49	class ReadTimeoutError extends Error {
    50	  public readonly isTimeout = true;
    51	  constructor(label: string, ms: number) {
    52	    super(`${label} timed out after ${Math.round(ms / 1000)}s`);
    53	    this.name = 'ReadTimeoutError';
    54	  }
    55	}
    56	
    57	async function withReadTimeout<T>(
    58	  promiseLike: PromiseLike<T>,
    59	  label: string,
    60	  timeoutMs: number = READ_TIMEOUT_MS
    61	): Promise<T> {
    62	  let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;
    63	  const timeoutPromise = new Promise<never>((_, reject) => {
    64	    timer = setTimeout(() => {
    65	      reject(new ReadTimeoutError(label, timeoutMs));
    66	    }, timeoutMs);
    67	  });
    68	  try {
    69	    return await Promise.race([Promise.resolve(promiseLike), timeoutPromise]);
    70	  } finally {
    71	    if (timer !== null) clearTimeout(timer);
    72	  }
    73	}
    74	
    75	const inflightNormalReads = new Map<string, Promise<ReadResult<any>>>();
    76	const inflightForcedReads = new Map<string, Promise<ReadResult<any>>>();
    77	
    78	function coalesceRead<T>(
    79	  key: string,
    80	  force: boolean,
    81	  loader: () => Promise<ReadResult<T>>
    82	): Promise<ReadResult<T>> {
    83	  const existingForced = inflightForcedReads.get(key) as Promise<ReadResult<T>> | undefined;
    84	  if (existingForced) {
    85	    return existingForced;
    86	  }
    87	  if (!force) {
    88	    const existingNormal = inflightNormalReads.get(key) as Promise<ReadResult<T>> | undefined;
    89	    if (existingNormal) {
    90	      return existingNormal;
    91	    }
    92	  }
    93	  const map = force ? inflightForcedReads : inflightNormalReads;
    94	  const promise = loader().finally(() => {
    95	    if (map.get(key) === promise) {
    96	      map.delete(key);
    97	    }
    98	  });
    99	  map.set(key, promise);
   100	  return promise;
   101	}
   102	
   103	function classifyReadError(err: any): {
   104	  message: string;
   105	  code: string | null;
   106	  timedOut: boolean;
   107	  unauthorized: boolean;
   108	} {
   109	  const timedOut = Boolean(
   110	    err instanceof ReadTimeoutError ||
   111	      err?.isTimeout ||
   112	      err?.name === 'AbortError' ||
   113	      String(err?.message || '').toLowerCase().includes('timed out')
   114	  );
   115	  const code = err?.code ? String(err.code) : err?.status ? String(err.status) : null;
   116	  const rawMsg = typeof err?.message === 'string' && err.message.trim()
   117	    ? err.message.trim()
   118	    : 'Network or database read failed';
   119	  const lower = rawMsg.toLowerCase();
   120	  const unauthorized = Boolean(
   121	    code === '401' ||
   122	      code === '403' ||
   123	      code === '42501' ||
   124	      code === 'PGRST301' ||
   125	      code === 'unauthorized' ||
   126	      lower.includes('unauthorized') ||
   127	      lower.includes('jwt expired') ||
   128	      lower.includes('invalid jwt')
   129	  );
   130	  return {
   131	    message: timedOut ? 'Request timed out after 8 seconds' : rawMsg,
   132	    code,
   133	    timedOut,
   134	    unauthorized,
   135	  };
   136	}

   147	export function getSupabaseCredentials(): { url: string; anonKey: string } {
   148	  let url = '';
   149	  let anonKey = '';
   150	  if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
   151	    try {
   152	      url = localStorage.getItem(SUPABASE_STORAGE_CONFIG_KEYS.URL) || '';
   153	      anonKey = localStorage.getItem(SUPABASE_STORAGE_CONFIG_KEYS.ANON_KEY) || '';
   154	    } catch {}
   155	  }
   156	  if (!url) {
   157	    url = (import.meta.env.VITE_SUPABASE_URL || '').trim();
   158	  }
   159	  if (!anonKey) {
   160	    anonKey = (import.meta.env.VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY || '').trim();
   161	  }
   162	  return { url: url.trim(), anonKey: anonKey.trim() };
   163	}
   164	
   165	export function setSupabaseCredentials(url: string, anonKey: string): void {
   166	  if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
   167	    localStorage.setItem(SUPABASE_STORAGE_CONFIG_KEYS.URL, url.trim());
   168	    localStorage.setItem(SUPABASE_STORAGE_CONFIG_KEYS.ANON_KEY, anonKey.trim());
   169	  }
   170	  resetSupabaseClient();
   171	}
   172	
   173	export function clearSupabaseCredentials(): void {
   174	  if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
   175	    localStorage.removeItem(SUPABASE_STORAGE_CONFIG_KEYS.URL);
   176	    localStorage.removeItem(SUPABASE_STORAGE_CONFIG_KEYS.ANON_KEY);
   177	  }
   178	  resetSupabaseClient();
   179	}

   181	export function isSupabaseConfigured(): boolean {
   182	  const { url, anonKey } = getSupabaseCredentials();
   183	  return Boolean(
   184	    url &&
   185	    anonKey &&
   186	    url.startsWith('http') &&
   187	    !url.includes('placeholder') &&
   188	    !url.includes('paste_your_url_here')
   189	  );
   190	}

   192	// Intercepts and sanitizes error payloads to eliminate raw HTML dumps (Directive 1)
   193	export function sanitizeDbError(err: any): Error {
   194	  const rawMsg = err?.message || (typeof err === 'string' ? err : JSON.stringify(err || ''));
   195	  const lower = rawMsg.toLowerCase();
   196	
   197	  if (
   198	    lower.includes('<!doctype') ||
   199	    lower.includes('<html') ||
   200	    lower.includes('<head') ||
   201	    lower.includes('<body') ||
   202	    lower.includes('send was called before connect') ||
   203	    lower.includes('failed to fetch') ||
   204	    lower.includes('networkerror') ||
   205	    lower.includes('unexpected token <') ||
   206	    lower.includes('is not valid json') ||
   207	    (lower.includes('404') && lower.includes('supabase-proxy'))
   208	  ) {
   209	    return new Error('Network Error: Could not reach the database. Check Supabase credentials.');
   210	  }
   211	
   212	  return new Error(rawMsg || 'Database operation failed.');
   213	}

   217	export function getSupabaseClient(): SupabaseClient {
   218	  if (!activeClient) {
   219	    const { url, anonKey } = getSupabaseCredentials();
   220	    const validUrl = isSupabaseConfigured() ? url : 'https://placeholder.supabase.co';
   221	    const validKey = anonKey || 'placeholder-anon-key';
   222	
   223	    activeClient = createClient(validUrl, validKey, {
   224	      auth: {

   226	        autoRefreshToken: true,

   228	    });
   229	  }

   239	// Standard Supabase client instance (proxied dynamically to localStorage or env credentials)
   240	export const supabase: SupabaseClient = new Proxy({} as SupabaseClient, {
   241	  get(_target, prop) {
   242	    const client = getSupabaseClient();
   243	    const val = (client as any)[prop];
   244	    if (typeof val === 'function') {
   245	      return val.bind(client);
   246	    }
   247	    return val;
   248	  },
   249	});

   267	export function generateUUID(): string {
   268	  if (typeof crypto !== 'undefined' && crypto.randomUUID) {
   269	    return crypto.randomUUID();
   270	  }
   271	  return 'xxxxxxxx-xxxx-4xxx-yxxx-xxxxxxxxxxxx'.replace(/[xy]/g, (c) => {
   272	    const r = (Math.random() * 16) | 0;
   273	    const v = c === 'x' ? r : (r & 0x3) | 0x8;
   274	    return v.toString(16);
   275	  });
   276	}

   278	export function generateOrgId(): string {
   279	  const chars = 'ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ23456789';
   280	  let randomPart = '';
   281	  for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
   282	    randomPart += chars.charAt(Math.floor(Math.random() * chars.length));
   283	  }
   284	  return `FJ-${randomPart}`;
   285	}
