   340	function isServerRejectionError(err: any): boolean {
   341	  const msg = String(err?.message || '').toLowerCase();
   342	  return Boolean(
   343	    err?.code === 'unauthorized' ||
   344	    err?.code === 'forbidden' ||
   345	    err?.code === 'ROSTER_LOCKED' ||
   346	    err?.code === 'rpc_unavailable' ||
   347	    err?.code === 'not_found' ||
   348	    err?.code === '42501' ||
   349	    err?.code === '23505' ||
   350	    err?.code === '23503' ||
   351	    err?.code === '23502' ||
   352	    err?.code === '22P02' ||
   353	    err?.code === 'RLS Permission Denied' ||
   354	    err?.message?.includes('ROSTER_LOCKED') ||
   355	    err?.message?.includes('RLS Permission Denied') ||
   356	    msg.includes('permission denied') ||
   357	    msg.includes('row-level security') ||
   358	    msg.includes('not assigned') ||
   359	    parseOrgEnforcementError(err)
   360	  );
   361	}
   362	
   363	let lastClockSyncAt = 0;
   364	let inflightClockSync: Promise<number> | null = null;
   365	
   366	export async function syncServerClockOffset(force: boolean = false): Promise<number> {
   367	  if (!isSupabaseConfigured() || !isOnlineNavigator()) {
   368	    return getServerClockOffsetMs();
   369	  }
   370	  const nowMono = Date.now();
   371	  if (!force && lastClockSyncAt > 0 && nowMono - lastClockSyncAt < 60_000) {
   372	    return getServerClockOffsetMs();
   373	  }
   374	  if (inflightClockSync) return inflightClockSync;
   375	
   376	  inflightClockSync = (async () => {
   377	    try {
   378	      const tBefore = Date.now();
   379	      const { data, error } = await withReadTimeout<{ data: any; error: any }>(
   380	        (supabase.rpc as any)('fj_server_time'),
   381	        'Sync server clock',
   382	        5000
   383	      );
   384	      const tAfter = Date.now();
   385	      if (!error && data) {
   386	        const serverMs = Date.parse(String(data));
   387	        if (Number.isFinite(serverMs)) {
   388	          const clientMidMs = (tBefore + tAfter) / 2;
   389	          const offsetMs = serverMs - clientMidMs;
   390	          setServerClockOffsetMs(offsetMs);
   391	          lastClockSyncAt = Date.now();
   392	          return offsetMs;
   393	        }
   394	      }
   395	    } catch (err) {
   396	      console.warn('Server clock sync note:', err);
   397	    } finally {
   398	      inflightClockSync = null;
   399	    }
   400	    return getServerClockOffsetMs();
   401	  })();
   402	
   403	  return inflightClockSync;
   404	}
   405	
   406	/**
   407	 * Executes a Stage D3 / 15.10 token-authenticated write RPC exclusively.
   408	 * Never falls back to direct table writes.
   409	 * Emits `session_expired` when online with a missing/expired token.
   410	 */
   411	export async function callSecuredWriteRpc<T = any>(
   412	  rpcName: string,
   413	  params: Record<string, any>
   414	): Promise<{ data?: T }> {
   415	  const token = getStoredSessionToken() || '';
   416	  if (!token) {
   417	    eventBus.emit('session_expired', {
   418	      role: isAuthRequiredSessionRole(),
   419	      reason: 'missing',
   420	    });
   421	    const authErr = new Error('Session token missing. Please verify your credentials to continue.');
   422	    (authErr as any).code = 'unauthorized';
   423	    throw authErr;
   424	  }
   425	
   426	  const { data, error } = await withReadTimeout(
   427	    supabase.rpc(rpcName, {
   428	      p_token: token,
   429	      ...params,
   430	    }),
   431	    `Write RPC (${rpcName})`,
   432	    20_000
   433	  );
   434	
   435	  if (error) {
   436	    const msg = (error.message || '').toLowerCase();
   437	    if (error.code === 'PGRST202' || error.code === '42883' || msg.includes(`function public.${rpcName}`)) {
   438	      const rpcErr = new Error('Database security RPCs are not deployed. Please run sql/15.10-stage1-security-lock.sql.');
   439	      (rpcErr as any).code = 'rpc_unavailable';
   440	      throw rpcErr;
   441	    }
   442	    throw error;
   443	  }
   444	
   445	  const res = data as any;
   446	  if (!res || !res.ok) {
   447	    const errCode = res?.error || 'RPC_ERROR';
   448	    const msg = res?.message || res?.error || 'Database operation rejected.';
   449	    if (errCode === 'unauthorized') {
   450	      eventBus.emit('session_expired', {
   451	        role: isAuthRequiredSessionRole(),
   452	        reason: 'expired',
   453	      });
   454	    }
   455	    const err = new Error(msg);
   456	    (err as any).code = errCode === 'ROSTER_LOCKED' ? 'ROSTER_LOCKED' : errCode;
   457	    throw err;
   458	  }
   459	
   460	  return { data: res.data as T };
   461	}
   462	
   463	/**
   464	 * Executes a Phase 2A token-authenticated read RPC exclusively.
   465	 * Rejects unauthenticated callers when no session token is available.
   466	 */
   467	export async function callSecuredReadRpc<T = any>(
   468	  rpcName: string,
   469	  params: Record<string, any> = {},
   470	  tokenOverride?: string
   471	): Promise<{ data?: T; not_found?: boolean; can_delete?: boolean; judges?: any[]; participants?: any[] }> {
   472	  const token = (tokenOverride || getStoredSessionToken() || '').trim();
   473	  if (!token) {
   474	    const authErr = new Error('Session expired or missing. Please sign in again.');
   475	    (authErr as any).code = 'unauthorized';
   476	    throw authErr;
   477	  }
   478	
   479	  const { data, error } = await withReadTimeout(
   480	    supabase.rpc(rpcName, {
   481	      p_token: token,
   482	      ...params,
   483	    }),
   484	    `Read RPC (${rpcName})`,
   485	    READ_TIMEOUT_MS
   486	  );
   487	
   488	  if (error) {
   489	    const msg = (error.message || '').toLowerCase();
   490	    if (error.code === 'PGRST202' || error.code === '42883' || msg.includes(`function public.${rpcName}`)) {
   491	      const rpcErr = new Error(
   492	        'Database read RPCs are not deployed yet. Please run sql/15.12-phase2a-read-rpcs.sql.'
   493	      );
   494	      (rpcErr as any).code = 'rpc_unavailable';
   495	      throw rpcErr;
   496	    }
   497	    throw error;
   498	  }
   499	
   500	  const res = data as any;
   501	  if (!res || !res.ok) {
   502	    const errCode = res?.error || 'RPC_ERROR';
   503	    const msg = res?.message || res?.error || 'Database read rejected.';
   504	    const err = new Error(msg);
   505	    (err as any).code = errCode;
   506	    throw err;
   507	  }
   508	
   509	  return res;
   510	}
   511	
   512	export function setStoredSession(session: StoredUserSession | null): void {
   513	  try {
   514	    if (!session) {
   515	      localStorage.removeItem(STORAGE_KEYS.ACTIVE_SESSION);
   516	      localStorage.removeItem(STORAGE_KEYS.JUDGE_DEVICE_TOKEN);
   517	    } else {
   518	      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.ACTIVE_SESSION, JSON.stringify(session));
   519	    }
   520	    if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
   521	      window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('fairjudge_session_change'));
   522	    }
   523	  } catch {}
   524	}
   525	
   526	// ==========================================
   527	// 5-MINUTE SWR CACHE THROTTLE & EVENT BUS
   528	// ==========================================
   529	
   530	export function isCacheValid(key: string): boolean {
   531	  return isCacheFresh(key);
   532	}
   533	
   534	export function updateCacheTimestamp(key: string): void {
   535	  touchCacheTimestamp(key);
   536	}
   537	
   538	export function invalidateCache(key?: string): Promise<void> {
   539	  return invalidateIDBCache(key);
   540	}
   541	
   542	function loadCache<T>(key: string, defaultData: T[] = []): T[] {
   543	  return getHotCache<T[]>(key, defaultData);
   544	}
   545	
   546	// Asynchronously writes to IndexedDB and immediately updates the in-memory hot mirror
   547	function saveCache<T>(key: string, data: T[], silent: boolean = false) {
   548	  setIndexedDBCache(key, data, silent);
   549	  if (!silent) {
   550	    notifyDatabaseChange(key);
   551	  }
   552	}
   553	
   554	const DB_CHANGE_EVENT = 'fairjudge_db_change';
   555	let debounceTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;
   556	let batchedTables = new Set<DbTableName>();
   557	
   558	// Debounced event broadcaster: batches multiple database changes into EventBus & CustomEvents
   559	export function notifyDatabaseChange(table: string) {
   560	  batchedTables.add(normalizeDbTableName(table));
   561	  if (debounceTimer) {
   562	    clearTimeout(debounceTimer);
   563	  }
   564	  debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
   565	    const tables = Array.from(batchedTables);
   566	    batchedTables.clear();
   567	    const emittedTable: DbTableName = tables.length === 1 ? tables[0] : DB_TABLES.ALL;
   568	
   569	    // Broadcast through typed EventBus
   570	    eventBus.emit('cache_updated', { table: emittedTable });
   571	
   572	    if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
   573	      window.dispatchEvent(
   574	        new CustomEvent(DB_CHANGE_EVENT, {
   575	          detail: { table: emittedTable, tables },
   576	        })
   577	      );
   578	    }
   579	    debounceTimer = null;
   580	  }, 150);
   581	}
   582	
   583	export function subscribeToDatabase(callback: (table: string) => void): () => void {
   584	  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return () => {};
   585	
   586	  const handler = (e: Event) => {
   587	    const custom = e as CustomEvent<{ table: string }>;
   588	    callback(custom.detail?.table || DB_TABLES.ALL);
   589	  };
   590	  window.addEventListener(DB_CHANGE_EVENT, handler);
   591	
   592	  let unsubscribed = false;
   593	  return () => {
   594	    if (unsubscribed) return;
   595	    unsubscribed = true;
   596	    window.removeEventListener(DB_CHANGE_EVENT, handler);
   597	  };
   598	}
   599	
   600	// ==========================================
   601	// DATA NORMALIZERS (PostgreSQL <-> UI)
   602	// ==========================================
   603	
   604	export function normalizeOrg(o: any): Organization {
   605	  const { password: _p, ...rest } = o || {};
   606	  return {
   607	    ...rest,
   608	    verification_status: (rest.verification_status || 'pending') as VerificationStatus,
   609	  };
   610	}
   611	
   612	export function normalizeEvent(e: any): SupabaseEvent {
   613	  const schema = Array.isArray(e.rubric_schema)
   614	    ? e.rubric_schema
   615	    : Array.isArray(e.rubric)
   616	    ? e.rubric
   617	    : [];
   618	  return {
   619	    ...e,
   620	    status: (e.status?.toLowerCase() as EventStatus) || 'draft',
   621	    is_locked: Boolean(e.is_locked),
   622	    is_archived: Boolean(e.is_archived),
   623	    rubric_schema: schema,
   624	    rubric: schema,
   625	    assigned_judges: Array.isArray(e.assigned_judges) ? e.assigned_judges : [],
   626	    assigned_participants: Array.isArray(e.assigned_participants) ? e.assigned_participants : [],
   627	    participant_sl_numbers: e.participant_sl_numbers || {},
   628	  };
   629	}
   630	
   631	export function normalizeJudge(j: any): SupabaseJudge {
   632	  const isJudgeDevice = getStoredSession()?.role === 'judge';
   633	  const cleanPin = isJudgeDevice ? '' : j.pin || j.judge_pin || '';
   634	  return {
   635	    ...j,
   636	    pin: cleanPin,
   637	    judge_pin: cleanPin,
   638	    assigned_events: Array.isArray(j.assigned_events) ? j.assigned_events : [],
   639	    current_session_token: null,
   640	  };
   641	}
   642	
   643	export function normalizeScore(s: any): SupabaseScore {
   644	  const criteria = s.score_data || s.score_payload?.criteria || {};
   645	  const total = Number(s.total_score ?? s.score_payload?.total_score ?? 0);
   646	  return {
   647	    ...s,
   648	    total_score: total,
   649	    score_data: criteria,
   650	    score_payload: s.score_payload || {
   651	      criteria,
   652	      total_score: total,
   653	      submitted_at: s.created_at || new Date().toISOString(),
   654	    },
   655	  };
   656	}

   658	export interface PublicLinkSettings {
   659	  delaySeconds: number;
   660	  isPaused: boolean;
   661	  isRevoked: boolean;
   662	  lastUpdated: string;
   663	  // Phase 14.3: 6 Broadcast Settings
   664	  blindMode?: boolean; // 1. Score Secrecy (Blind Mode)
   665	  spotlightMode?: boolean; // 2. Spotlight / Focus Mode
   666	  tickerText?: string; // 3. Custom Broadcast Ticker
   667	  confettiTrigger?: number; // 4. Confetti Trigger timestamp
   668	  paginationTimer?: number; // 5. Custom Pagination Timer in seconds
   669	}

   675	function notifyCloudSync(entityName: string = 'Update') {
   676	  eventBus.emit('toast', {
   677	    type: 'success',
   678	    title: 'Synced to Cloud',
   679	    message: `${entityName} synced to cloud ledger.`,
   680	  });
   681	  eventBus.emit('sync_status', { status: 'synced' });
   682	}
   683	
   684	function notifyOfflineSaved(entityName: string = 'Change') {
   685	  eventBus.emit('toast', {
   686	    type: 'warning',
   687	    title: 'Saved locally',
   688	    message: `${entityName} saved locally. Pending cloud sync.`,
   689	  });
   690	  eventBus.emit('sync_status', { status: 'offline' });
   691	}

   693	// ==========================================
   694	// PRODUCTION SUPABASE DATA SERVICE
   695	// ==========================================
   696	
   697	export const dbService = {
   698	  // ------------------------------------------
   699	  // SYNCHRONOUS CACHE READERS (Anti-Flicker)
   700	  // ------------------------------------------
   701	  getCachedEvents(orgId: string): SupabaseEvent[] {
   702	    const cleanOrgId = orgId.trim().toUpperCase();
   703	    const events = loadCache<SupabaseEvent>(STORAGE_KEYS.EVENTS, []);
   704	    return events
   705	      .filter((e) => (e.org_id || '').trim().toUpperCase() === cleanOrgId)
   706	      .map(normalizeEvent);
   707	  },
   708	
   709	  getCachedParticipants(orgId: string): SupabaseParticipant[] {
   710	    const cleanOrgId = orgId.trim().toUpperCase();
   711	    const participants = loadCache<SupabaseParticipant>(STORAGE_KEYS.PARTICIPANTS, []);
   712	    return participants.filter((p) => (p.org_id || '').trim().toUpperCase() === cleanOrgId);
   713	  },
   714	
   715	  getCachedJudges(orgId: string): SupabaseJudge[] {
   716	    const cleanOrgId = orgId.trim().toUpperCase();
   717	    const judges = loadCache<SupabaseJudge>(STORAGE_KEYS.JUDGES, []);
   718	    return judges
   719	      .filter((j) => (j.org_id || '').trim().toUpperCase() === cleanOrgId)
   720	      .map(normalizeJudge);
   721	  },
   722	
   723	  getCachedScores(orgId?: string): SupabaseScore[] {
   724	    const scores = loadCache<SupabaseScore>(STORAGE_KEYS.SCORES, []);
   725	    if (orgId) {
   726	      const orgEventIds = new Set(this.getCachedEvents(orgId).map((e) => e.id));
   727	      return scores.filter((s) => orgEventIds.has(s.event_id)).map(normalizeScore);
   728	    }
   729	    return scores.map(normalizeScore);
   730	  },
   731	
   732	  hasCachedData(orgId: string): boolean {
   733	    const cleanOrgId = orgId.trim().toUpperCase();
   734	    const events = loadCache<SupabaseEvent>(STORAGE_KEYS.EVENTS, []);
   735	    return events.some((e) => e.org_id.toUpperCase() === cleanOrgId);
   736	  },
   737	
   738	  // ------------------------------------------
   739	  // 1. ORGANIZATIONS (Token-Checked, Own Org Only)
   740	  // ------------------------------------------
   741	  async getOrganizationByOrgIdResult(
   742	    orgId: string,
   743	    force: boolean = false,
   744	    tokenOverride?: string
   745	  ): Promise<ReadResult<Organization | null>> {
   746	    const cleanOrgId = orgId.trim().toUpperCase();
   747	    const cacheKey = `${STORAGE_KEYS.ORGS}_${cleanOrgId}`;
   748	    const orgs = loadCache<Organization>(STORAGE_KEYS.ORGS, []);
   749	    const found = orgs.find((o) => o.org_id.toUpperCase() === cleanOrgId);
   750	    const cachedOrg = found ? normalizeOrg(found) : null;
   751	
   752	    if (!force && isCacheValid(cacheKey) && cachedOrg) {
   753	      return { ok: true, data: cachedOrg, fromCache: true, error: null, timedOut: false };
   754	    }
   755	
   756	    return coalesceRead(cacheKey, force, async () => {
   757	      const latestOrgs = loadCache<Organization>(STORAGE_KEYS.ORGS, []);
   758	      const latestFound = latestOrgs.find((o) => o.org_id.toUpperCase() === cleanOrgId);
   759	      const latestCachedOrg = latestFound ? normalizeOrg(latestFound) : null;
   760	
   761	      if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
   762	        try {
   763	          const res = await callSecuredReadRpc<any>(
   764	            'read_organization',
   765	            { p_org_id: cleanOrgId },
   766	            tokenOverride
   767	          );
   768	
   769	          if (res.data) {
   770	            const normalized = normalizeOrg(res.data);
   771	            const currentOrgs = loadCache<Organization>(STORAGE_KEYS.ORGS, []);
   772	            saveCache(
   773	              STORAGE_KEYS.ORGS,
   774	              [normalized, ...currentOrgs.filter((o) => o.org_id.toUpperCase() !== cleanOrgId)],
   775	              true
   776	            );
   777	            updateCacheTimestamp(cacheKey);
   778	            return { ok: true, data: normalized, fromCache: false, error: null, timedOut: false };
   779	          }
   780	
   781	          return {
   782	            ok: true,
   783	            data: null,
   784	            fromCache: false,
   785	            error: null,
   786	            timedOut: false,
   787	            notFound: true,
   788	          };
   789	        } catch (err: any) {
   790	          const info = classifyReadError(err);
   791	          console.warn('Supabase getOrganizationByOrgId fallback:', info.message);
   792	          return {
   793	            ok: false,
   794	            data: latestCachedOrg,
   795	            fromCache: Boolean(latestCachedOrg),
   796	            error: info.message,
   797	            errorCode: info.code,
   798	            timedOut: info.timedOut,
   799	            unauthorized: info.unauthorized,
   800	          };
   801	        }
   802	      } else if (latestCachedOrg) {
   803	        updateCacheTimestamp(cacheKey);
   804	        return { ok: true, data: latestCachedOrg, fromCache: true, error: null, timedOut: false };
   805	      }
   806	
   807	      return {
   808	        ok: Boolean(latestCachedOrg),
   809	        data: latestCachedOrg,
   810	        fromCache: Boolean(latestCachedOrg),
   811	        error: latestCachedOrg ? null : 'Organization not found locally',
   812	        timedOut: false,
   813	        notFound: !latestCachedOrg,
   814	      };
   815	    });
   816	  },
   817	
   818	  async getOrganizationByOrgId(
   819	    orgId: string,
   820	    force: boolean = false,
   821	    tokenOverride?: string
   822	  ): Promise<Organization | null> {
   823	    const res = await this.getOrganizationByOrgIdResult(orgId, force, tokenOverride);
   824	    return res.data;
   825	  },
   826	
   827	  async updateOrganization(orgId: string, updates: Partial<Organization>): Promise<Organization> {
   828	    const cleanOrgId = orgId.trim().toUpperCase();
   829	    // Strict security: Never update or queue password through standard entity updates
   830	    const { password: _stripPassword, ...safeUpdates } = updates as any;
   831	    const orgs = loadCache<Organization>(STORAGE_KEYS.ORGS, []);
   832	    const idx = orgs.findIndex((o) => o.org_id.toUpperCase() === cleanOrgId);
   833	    const existing = idx !== -1 ? orgs[idx] : null;
   834	
   835	    const updated = normalizeOrg({
   836	      ...(existing || { org_id: cleanOrgId, id: generateUUID(), name: 'Organization' }),
   837	      ...safeUpdates,
   838	    });
   839	
   840	    const orgsSnapshot = [...orgs];
   841	
   842	    // Optimistically save to cache & notify UI
   843	    if (idx !== -1) {
   844	      orgs[idx] = updated;
   845	    } else {
   846	      orgs.unshift(updated);
   847	    }
   848	    saveCache(STORAGE_KEYS.ORGS, orgs, false);
   849	    invalidateCache(`${STORAGE_KEYS.ORGS}_${cleanOrgId}`);
   850	
   851	    if (isSupabaseConfigured() && isOnlineNavigator()) {
   852	      try {
   853	        const rpcRes = await callSecuredWriteRpc<Organization>('org_update', {
   854	          p_org_id: cleanOrgId,
   855	          p_updates: safeUpdates,
   856	        });
   857	
   858	        const syncedRow = rpcRes.data;
   859	        if (syncedRow) {
   860	          const synced = normalizeOrg(syncedRow);
   861	          const currentOrgs = loadCache<Organization>(STORAGE_KEYS.ORGS, []);
   862	          saveCache(
   863	            STORAGE_KEYS.ORGS,
   864	            currentOrgs.map((o) => (o.org_id.toUpperCase() === cleanOrgId ? synced : o)),
   865	            true
   866	          );
   867	          return synced;
   868	        }
   869	      } catch (err: any) {
   870	        if (isServerRejectionError(err)) {
   871	          saveCache(STORAGE_KEYS.ORGS, orgsSnapshot, false);
   872	          throw err;
   873	        }
   874	        console.warn('Offline mode: organization update saved locally, deferring cloud sync:', err?.message || err);
   875	        await queueMutationLocally('organizations', 'UPDATE', cleanOrgId, safeUpdates);
   876	      }
   877	    } else if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
   878	      await queueMutationLocally('organizations', 'UPDATE', cleanOrgId, safeUpdates);
   879	    }
   880	
   881	    return updated;
   882	  },
   883	
   884	  async resetOrganizationData(orgId: string): Promise<void> {
   885	    const cleanOrgId = orgId.toUpperCase().trim();
   886	
   887	    if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
   888	      await callSecuredWriteRpc('org_reset_data', { p_org_id: cleanOrgId });
   889	    }
   890	
   891	    // Clear local cache for this org
   892	    const events = loadCache<SupabaseEvent>(STORAGE_KEYS.EVENTS, []);
   893	    const orgEventIds = new Set(
   894	      events.filter((e) => (e.org_id || '').toUpperCase().trim() === cleanOrgId).map((e) => e.id)
   895	    );
   896	    saveCache(
   897	      STORAGE_KEYS.EVENTS,
   898	      events.filter((e) => (e.org_id || '').toUpperCase().trim() !== cleanOrgId),
   899	      false
   900	    );
   901	
   902	    const scores = loadCache<SupabaseScore>(STORAGE_KEYS.SCORES, []);
   903	    saveCache(
   904	      STORAGE_KEYS.SCORES,
   905	      scores.filter(
   906	        (s) => !orgEventIds.has(s.event_id) && ((s as any).org_id || '').toUpperCase().trim() !== cleanOrgId
   907	      ),
   908	      false
   909	    );
   910	
   911	    const participants = loadCache<SupabaseParticipant>(STORAGE_KEYS.PARTICIPANTS, []);
   912	    const orgParticipantIds = new Set(
   913	      participants.filter((p) => (p.org_id || '').toUpperCase().trim() === cleanOrgId).map((p) => p.id)
   914	    );
   915	    saveCache(
   916	      STORAGE_KEYS.PARTICIPANTS,
   917	      participants.filter((p) => (p.org_id || '').toUpperCase().trim() !== cleanOrgId),
   918	      false
   919	    );
   920	
   921	    const judges = loadCache<SupabaseJudge>(STORAGE_KEYS.JUDGES, []);
   922	    const orgJudgeIds = new Set(
   923	      judges.filter((j) => (j.org_id || '').toUpperCase().trim() === cleanOrgId).map((j) => j.id)
   924	    );
   925	    saveCache(
   926	      STORAGE_KEYS.JUDGES,
   927	      judges.filter((j) => (j.org_id || '').toUpperCase().trim() !== cleanOrgId),
   928	      false
   929	    );
   930	
   931	    await invalidateCache();
   932	    await clearOfflineWorkForOrg(cleanOrgId, {
   933	      eventIds: orgEventIds,
   934	      participantIds: orgParticipantIds,
   935	      judgeIds: orgJudgeIds,
   936	    });
   937	    eventBus.emit('cache_updated', { table: DB_TABLES.ALL });
   938	  },
   939	
   940	  async deleteOrganization(orgId: string): Promise<void> {
   941	    const cleanOrgId = orgId.toUpperCase().trim();
   942	
   943	    if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
   944	      await callSecuredWriteRpc('org_delete', { p_org_id: cleanOrgId });
   945	      // Clear local cache after confirmed RPC deletion (never re-call org_reset_data RPC on org_delete)
   946	      const events = loadCache<SupabaseEvent>(STORAGE_KEYS.EVENTS, []);
   947	      const orgEventIds = new Set(
   948	        events.filter((e) => (e.org_id || '').toUpperCase().trim() === cleanOrgId).map((e) => e.id)
   949	      );
   950	      saveCache(STORAGE_KEYS.EVENTS, events.filter((e) => (e.org_id || '').toUpperCase().trim() !== cleanOrgId), false);
   951	      const scores = loadCache<SupabaseScore>(STORAGE_KEYS.SCORES, []);
   952	      saveCache(
   953	        STORAGE_KEYS.SCORES,
   954	        scores.filter(
   955	          (s) => !orgEventIds.has(s.event_id) && ((s as any).org_id || '').toUpperCase().trim() !== cleanOrgId
   956	        ),
   957	        false
   958	      );
   959	      const participants = loadCache<SupabaseParticipant>(STORAGE_KEYS.PARTICIPANTS, []);
   960	      const orgParticipantIds = new Set(
   961	        participants.filter((p) => (p.org_id || '').toUpperCase().trim() === cleanOrgId).map((p) => p.id)
   962	      );
   963	      saveCache(STORAGE_KEYS.PARTICIPANTS, participants.filter((p) => (p.org_id || '').toUpperCase().trim() !== cleanOrgId), false);
   964	      const judges = loadCache<SupabaseJudge>(STORAGE_KEYS.JUDGES, []);
   965	      const orgJudgeIds = new Set(
   966	        judges.filter((j) => (j.org_id || '').toUpperCase().trim() === cleanOrgId).map((j) => j.id)
   967	      );
   968	      saveCache(STORAGE_KEYS.JUDGES, judges.filter((j) => (j.org_id || '').toUpperCase().trim() !== cleanOrgId), false);
   969	      await invalidateCache();
   970	      await clearOfflineWorkForOrg(cleanOrgId, {
   971	        eventIds: orgEventIds,
   972	        participantIds: orgParticipantIds,
   973	        judgeIds: orgJudgeIds,
   974	      });
   975	    } else {
   976	      await this.resetOrganizationData(cleanOrgId);
   977	    }
   978	
   979	    const orgs = loadCache<Organization>(STORAGE_KEYS.ORGS, []);
   980	    saveCache(
   981	      STORAGE_KEYS.ORGS,
   982	      orgs.filter((o) => (o.org_id || '').toUpperCase().trim() !== cleanOrgId),
   983	      false
   984	    );
   985	    setStoredSession(null);
   986	    await invalidateCache();
   987	    eventBus.emit('cache_updated', { table: DB_TABLES.ALL });
   988	  },
   989	
   990	  // ------------------------------------------
   991	  // 2. PUBLIC LINK SETTINGS
   992	  // ------------------------------------------
   993	  getPublicSettings(orgId: string): PublicLinkSettings {
   994	    try {
   995	      const raw = localStorage.getItem(`${STORAGE_KEYS.PUBLIC_SETTINGS}_${orgId.toUpperCase()}`);
   996	      if (raw) return JSON.parse(raw);
   997	    } catch {}
   998	    return {
   999	      delaySeconds: 0,
  1000	      isPaused: false,
  1001	      isRevoked: false,
  1002	      lastUpdated: new Date().toISOString(),
  1003	      blindMode: false,
  1004	      spotlightMode: true,
  1005	      tickerText: '',
  1006	      confettiTrigger: 0,
  1007	      paginationTimer: 60,
  1008	    };
  1009	  },
  1010	
  1011	  savePublicSettings(orgId: string, settings: Partial<PublicLinkSettings>): PublicLinkSettings {
  1012	    const current = this.getPublicSettings(orgId);
  1013	    const updated: PublicLinkSettings = {
  1014	      ...current,
  1015	      ...settings,
  1016	      lastUpdated: new Date().toISOString(),
  1017	    };
  1018	    try {
  1019	      localStorage.setItem(
  1020	        `${STORAGE_KEYS.PUBLIC_SETTINGS}_${orgId.toUpperCase()}`,
  1021	        JSON.stringify(updated)
  1022	      );
  1023	      notifyDatabaseChange(STORAGE_KEYS.PUBLIC_SETTINGS);
  1024	    } catch {}
  1025	    return updated;
  1026	  },
  1027	
  1028	  // ------------------------------------------
  1029	  // 3. EVENTS (Optimistic Mutations + SWR)
  1030	  // ------------------------------------------
  1031	  async getEventsResult(orgId: string, force: boolean = false): Promise<ReadResult<SupabaseEvent[]>> {
  1032	    const cleanOrgId = orgId.trim().toUpperCase();
  1033	    const cacheKey = `${STORAGE_KEYS.EVENTS}_${cleanOrgId}`;
  1034	    const cachedEvents = this.getCachedEvents(cleanOrgId);
  1035	
  1036	    // 5-Minute SWR Cache Throttle: If cache is valid, return it immediately (even when empty)
  1037	    if (!force && isCacheValid(cacheKey)) {
  1038	      return { ok: true, data: cachedEvents, fromCache: true, error: null, timedOut: false };
  1039	    }
  1040	
  1041	    return coalesceRead(cacheKey, force, async () => {
  1042	      const latestCached = this.getCachedEvents(cleanOrgId);
  1043	
  1044	      if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
  1045	        try {
  1046	          const res = await callSecuredReadRpc<any[]>('read_events', {});
  1047	          const data = Array.isArray(res.data) ? res.data : [];
  1048	          const normalized = data.map(normalizeEvent);
  1049	          const allCachedEvents = loadCache<SupabaseEvent>(STORAGE_KEYS.EVENTS, []);
  1050	          const otherOrgEvents = allCachedEvents.filter(
  1051	            (e) => (e.org_id || '').trim().toUpperCase() !== cleanOrgId
  1052	          );
  1053	          const changed = !areRecordsEqual(latestCached, normalized);
  1054	          saveCache(STORAGE_KEYS.EVENTS, [...normalized, ...otherOrgEvents], !changed);
  1055	          updateCacheTimestamp(cacheKey);
  1056	          return { ok: true, data: normalized, fromCache: false, error: null, timedOut: false };
  1057	        } catch (err: any) {
  1058	          const info = classifyReadError(err);
  1059	          console.warn('Supabase getEvents fallback:', info.message);
  1060	          return {
  1061	            ok: false,
  1062	            data: latestCached,
  1063	            fromCache: latestCached.length > 0,
  1064	            error: info.message,
  1065	            errorCode: info.code,
  1066	            timedOut: info.timedOut,
  1067	            unauthorized: info.unauthorized,
  1068	          };
  1069	        }
  1070	      } else {
  1071	        updateCacheTimestamp(cacheKey);
  1072	        return { ok: true, data: latestCached, fromCache: true, error: null, timedOut: false };
  1073	      }
  1074	    });
  1075	  },
  1076	
  1077	  async getEvents(orgId: string, force: boolean = false): Promise<SupabaseEvent[]> {
  1078	    const res = await this.getEventsResult(orgId, force);
  1079	    return res.data;
  1080	  },
  1081	
  1082	  async getEventByIdResult(eventId: string): Promise<ReadResult<SupabaseEvent | null>> {
  1083	    return coalesceRead(`event_by_id_${eventId}`, false, async () => {
  1084	      const events = loadCache<SupabaseEvent>(STORAGE_KEYS.EVENTS, []);
  1085	      const found = events.find((item) => item.id === eventId);
  1086	      const cachedEvt = found ? normalizeEvent(found) : null;
  1087	
  1088	      if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
  1089	        try {
  1090	          const res = await callSecuredReadRpc<any[]>('read_events', { p_event_id: eventId });
  1091	          const rows = Array.isArray(res.data) ? res.data : [];
  1092	          if (rows[0]) {
  1093	            return {
  1094	              ok: true,
  1095	              data: normalizeEvent(rows[0]),
  1096	              fromCache: false,
  1097	              error: null,
  1098	              timedOut: false,
  1099	            };
  1100	          }
  1101	          return { ok: true, data: null, fromCache: false, error: null, timedOut: false, notFound: true };
  1102	        } catch (err: any) {
  1103	          const info = classifyReadError(err);
  1104	          console.warn('Supabase getEventById fallback:', info.message);
  1105	          return {
  1106	            ok: false,
  1107	            data: cachedEvt,
  1108	            fromCache: Boolean(cachedEvt),
  1109	            error: info.message,
  1110	            errorCode: info.code,
  1111	            timedOut: info.timedOut,
  1112	            unauthorized: info.unauthorized,
  1113	          };
  1114	        }
  1115	      }
  1116	
  1117	      return {
  1118	        ok: Boolean(cachedEvt),
  1119	        data: cachedEvt,
  1120	        fromCache: Boolean(cachedEvt),
  1121	        error: cachedEvt ? null : 'Event not found locally',
  1122	        timedOut: false,
  1123	        notFound: !cachedEvt,
  1124	      };
  1125	    });
  1126	  },
  1127	
  1128	  async getEventById(eventId: string): Promise<SupabaseEvent | null> {
  1129	    const res = await this.getEventByIdResult(eventId);
  1130	    return res.data;
  1131	  },
  1132	
  1133	  async createEvent(eventData: {
  1134	    org_id: string;
  1135	    name: string;
  1136	    category?: string;
  1137	    date?: string;
  1138	    status: EventStatus;
  1139	    rubric_schema: any[];
  1140	    assigned_judges: string[];
  1141	    assigned_participants: string[];
  1142	  }): Promise<SupabaseEvent> {
  1143	    const cleanOrgId = eventData.org_id.toUpperCase().trim();
  1144	    const normalizedStatus = (eventData.status?.toLowerCase() as EventStatus) || 'draft';
  1145	    const newId = generateUUID();
  1146	    const now = new Date().toISOString();
  1147	
  1148	    const newEvent: SupabaseEvent = normalizeEvent({
  1149	      ...eventData,
  1150	      id: newId,
  1151	      org_id: cleanOrgId,
  1152	      name: eventData.name.trim(),
  1153	      category: eventData.category?.trim() || 'General',
  1154	      date: eventData.date || now.split('T')[0],
  1155	      status: normalizedStatus,
  1156	      rubric_schema: eventData.rubric_schema || [],
  1157	      assigned_judges: eventData.assigned_judges || [],
  1158	      assigned_participants: eventData.assigned_participants || [],
  1159	      participant_sl_numbers: {},
  1160	      created_at: now,
  1161	      updated_at: now,
  1162	    });
  1163	
  1164	    // 1. OPTIMISTIC WRITE: Update local cache immediately & notify UI
  1165	    const eventsSnapshot = loadCache<SupabaseEvent>(STORAGE_KEYS.EVENTS, []);
  1166	    saveCache(STORAGE_KEYS.EVENTS, [newEvent, ...eventsSnapshot.filter((e) => e.id !== newId)], false);
  1167	    invalidateCache(`${STORAGE_KEYS.EVENTS}_${cleanOrgId}`);
  1168	
  1169	    const payload = {
  1170	      id: newEvent.id,
  1171	      org_id: newEvent.org_id,
  1172	      name: newEvent.name,
  1173	      category: newEvent.category,
  1174	      date: newEvent.date,
  1175	      status: newEvent.status,
  1176	      rubric_schema: newEvent.rubric_schema,
  1177	      assigned_judges: newEvent.assigned_judges,
  1178	      assigned_participants: newEvent.assigned_participants,
  1179	      participant_sl_numbers: newEvent.participant_sl_numbers,
  1180	    };
  1181	
  1182	    // 2. BACKGROUND CLOUD SYNC
  1183	    if (isSupabaseConfigured() && isOnlineNavigator()) {
  1184	      try {
  1185	        const rpcRes = await callSecuredWriteRpc<SupabaseEvent>('event_upsert', {
  1186	          p_record: payload,
  1187	        });
  1188	
  1189	        const createdRow = rpcRes.data;
  1190	        if (createdRow) {
  1191	          const created = normalizeEvent(createdRow);
  1192	          const currentEvents = loadCache<SupabaseEvent>(STORAGE_KEYS.EVENTS, []);
  1193	          saveCache(
  1194	            STORAGE_KEYS.EVENTS,
  1195	            currentEvents.map((e) => (e.id === created.id ? created : e)),
  1196	            true // silent
  1197	          );
  1198	          notifyCloudSync('Event');
  1199	        }
  1200	      } catch (err: any) {
  1201	        if (isServerRejectionError(err)) {
  1202	          saveCache(STORAGE_KEYS.EVENTS, eventsSnapshot, false);
  1203	          throw err;
  1204	        }
  1205	        console.warn('Offline mode: event saved locally, deferring cloud sync:', err?.message || err);
  1206	        await queueMutationLocally('events', 'INSERT', newEvent.id, payload);
  1207	        notifyOfflineSaved('Event');
  1208	      }
  1209	    } else if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
  1210	      await queueMutationLocally('events', 'INSERT', newEvent.id, payload);
  1211	      notifyOfflineSaved('Event');
  1212	    } else {
  1213	      notifyOfflineSaved('Event');
  1214	    }
  1215	
  1216	    // Always resolve and return the created event immediately!
  1217	    return newEvent;
  1218	  },
  1219	
  1220	  async updateEventStatus(eventId: string, newStatus: EventStatus): Promise<SupabaseEvent> {
  1221	    const normalized = (newStatus.toLowerCase() as EventStatus);
  1222	    return this.updateEvent(eventId, { status: normalized });
  1223	  },
  1224	
  1225	  async updateEvent(eventId: string, updates: Partial<SupabaseEvent>): Promise<SupabaseEvent> {
  1226	    const events = loadCache<SupabaseEvent>(STORAGE_KEYS.EVENTS, []);
  1227	    const idx = events.findIndex((e) => e.id === eventId);
  1228	    const existing = idx !== -1 ? events[idx] : null;
  1229	
  1230	    // PRE-CHECK: If locked and the call touches a roster column without setting is_locked: false, throw typed error BEFORE optimistic write
  1231	    const touchesRoster =
  1232	      updates.assigned_participants !== undefined ||
  1233	      updates.assigned_judges !== undefined ||
  1234	      updates.participant_sl_numbers !== undefined;
  1235	
  1236	    if (existing?.is_locked && updates.is_locked !== false && touchesRoster) {
  1237	      const err = new Error(`ROSTER_LOCKED: Cannot modify roster on locked event "${existing.name}". Unlock event first.`);
  1238	      (err as any).code = 'ROSTER_LOCKED';
  1239	      throw err;
  1240	    }
  1241	
  1242	    const updated: SupabaseEvent = normalizeEvent({
  1243	      ...(existing || {}),
  1244	      ...updates,
  1245	      id: eventId,
  1246	      updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
  1247	    });
  1248	
  1249	    // 1. OPTIMISTIC WRITE: Save to local cache immediately & notify UI
  1250	    if (idx !== -1) {
  1251	      events[idx] = updated;
  1252	    } else {
  1253	      events.unshift(updated);
  1254	    }
  1255	    saveCache(STORAGE_KEYS.EVENTS, events, false);
  1256	    if (updated.org_id) {
  1257	      invalidateCache(`${STORAGE_KEYS.EVENTS}_${updated.org_id.toUpperCase()}`);
  1258	    }
  1259	
  1260	    // 2. BACKGROUND CLOUD SYNC
  1261	    const dbUpdates: Record<string, any> = {
  1262	      ...updates,
  1263	      updated_at: updated.updated_at,
  1264	    };
  1265	    if (dbUpdates.rubric && !dbUpdates.rubric_schema) {
  1266	      dbUpdates.rubric_schema = dbUpdates.rubric;
  1267	    }
  1268	    delete dbUpdates.rubric;
  1269	
  1270	    if (isSupabaseConfigured() && isOnlineNavigator()) {
  1271	      try {
  1272	        const rpcRes = await callSecuredWriteRpc<SupabaseEvent>('event_update', {
  1273	          p_event_id: eventId,
  1274	          p_updates: dbUpdates,
  1275	        });
  1276	
  1277	        const syncedRow = rpcRes.data;
  1278	        if (syncedRow) {
  1279	          const synced = normalizeEvent({ ...syncedRow, is_archived: updated.is_archived });
  1280	          const currentEvents = loadCache<SupabaseEvent>(STORAGE_KEYS.EVENTS, []);
  1281	          saveCache(
  1282	            STORAGE_KEYS.EVENTS,
  1283	            currentEvents.map((e) => (e.id === synced.id ? synced : e)),
  1284	            true
  1285	          );
  1286	          notifyCloudSync('Event');
  1287	        }
  1288	      } catch (err: any) {
  1289	        const orgBlock = parseOrgEnforcementError(err);
  1290	        if (orgBlock) {
  1291	          eventBus.emit('org_enforcement', { code: orgBlock });
  1292	        }
  1293	        const isRosterLocked = Boolean(
  1294	          err?.code === 'ROSTER_LOCKED' ||
  1295	          err?.message?.includes('ROSTER_LOCKED') ||
  1296	          (err?.details && String(err.details).includes('ROSTER_LOCKED'))
  1297	        );
  1298	
  1299	        // If ROSTER_LOCKED, unauthorized, or any server-returned error: roll cache back and rethrow
  1300	        if (isServerRejectionError(err) || Boolean(err?.code) || isRosterLocked) {
  1301	          const currentEvents = loadCache<SupabaseEvent>(STORAGE_KEYS.EVENTS, []);
  1302	          if (existing) {
  1303	            const rollbackList = currentEvents.map((e) => (e.id === eventId ? existing : e));
  1304	            saveCache(STORAGE_KEYS.EVENTS, rollbackList, false);
  1305	          } else {
  1306	            const rollbackList = currentEvents.filter((e) => e.id !== eventId);
  1307	            saveCache(STORAGE_KEYS.EVENTS, rollbackList, false);
  1308	          }
  1309	          if (updated.org_id && err?.code !== 'unauthorized') {
  1310	            this.getEvents(updated.org_id, true).catch(() => {});
  1311	          }
  1312	          throw err;
  1313	        }
  1314	
  1315	        // Queue ONLY network errors
  1316	        console.warn('Offline mode: event update saved locally, deferring cloud sync:', err?.message || err);
  1317	        await queueMutationLocally('events', 'UPDATE', eventId, dbUpdates);
  1318	        notifyOfflineSaved('Event');
  1319	      }
  1320	    } else if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
  1321	      await queueMutationLocally('events', 'UPDATE', eventId, dbUpdates);
  1322	      notifyOfflineSaved('Event');
  1323	    } else {
  1324	      notifyOfflineSaved('Event');
  1325	    }
  1326	
  1327	    return updated;
  1328	  },
  1329	
  1330	  async updateEventParticipantSerial(
  1331	    eventId: string,
  1332	    participantId: string,
  1333	    newSlNo: string
  1334	  ): Promise<SupabaseEvent> {
  1335	    const event = await this.getEventById(eventId);
  1336	    if (!event) throw new Error('Event not found');
  1337	
  1338	    const updatedSerialMap = {
  1339	      ...(event.participant_sl_numbers || {}),
  1340	      [participantId]: newSlNo.trim(),
  1341	    };
  1342	
  1343	    return this.updateEvent(eventId, {
  1344	      participant_sl_numbers: updatedSerialMap,
  1345	    });
  1346	  },
  1347	
  1348	  async canDeleteEvent(eventId: string): Promise<boolean> {
  1349	    if (isSupabaseConfigured() && isOnlineNavigator()) {
  1350	      try {
  1351	        const res = await callSecuredReadRpc('read_can_delete_event', { p_event_id: eventId });
  1352	        return Boolean(res.can_delete);
  1353	      } catch {}
  1354	    }
  1355	    const scores = loadCache<SupabaseScore>(STORAGE_KEYS.SCORES, []);
  1356	    return !scores.some((s) => s.event_id === eventId);
  1357	  },
  1358	
  1359	  async deleteEvent(eventId: string): Promise<boolean> {
  1360	    const eventsSnapshot = loadCache<SupabaseEvent>(STORAGE_KEYS.EVENTS, []);
  1361	    const target = eventsSnapshot.find((e) => e.id === eventId);
  1362	    const remaining = eventsSnapshot.filter((e) => e.id !== eventId);
  1363	    saveCache(STORAGE_KEYS.EVENTS, remaining, false);
  1364	    if (target?.org_id) {
  1365	      invalidateCache(`${STORAGE_KEYS.EVENTS}_${target.org_id.toUpperCase()}`);
  1366	    }
  1367	
  1368	    if (isSupabaseConfigured() && isOnlineNavigator()) {
  1369	      try {
  1370	        await callSecuredWriteRpc('event_delete', { p_event_id: eventId });
  1371	        notifyCloudSync('Event');
  1372	      } catch (err: any) {
  1373	        if (isServerRejectionError(err)) {
  1374	          saveCache(STORAGE_KEYS.EVENTS, eventsSnapshot, false);
  1375	          throw err;
  1376	        }
  1377	        console.warn('Offline mode: event deletion saved locally, deferring cloud sync:', err?.message || err);
  1378	        await queueMutationLocally('events', 'DELETE', eventId, null);
  1379	        notifyOfflineSaved('Event');
  1380	      }
  1381	    } else if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
  1382	      await queueMutationLocally('events', 'DELETE', eventId, null);
  1383	      notifyOfflineSaved('Event');
  1384	    } else {
  1385	      notifyOfflineSaved('Event');
  1386	    }
  1387	
  1388	    return true;
  1389	  },
  1390	
  1391	  async deleteOrArchiveEvent(eventId: string): Promise<{ action: 'deleted' | 'archived' }> {
  1392	    const canDelete = await this.canDeleteEvent(eventId);
  1393	    if (canDelete) {
  1394	      await this.deleteEvent(eventId);
  1395	      return { action: 'deleted' };
  1396	    } else {
  1397	      await this.updateEvent(eventId, { status: 'archived' });
  1398	      return { action: 'archived' };
  1399	    }
  1400	  },
  1401	
  1402	  // ------------------------------------------
  1403	  // 4. JUDGES (Token-Checked; read_judges omits pin & current_session_token)
  1404	  // ------------------------------------------
  1405	  async getJudgesResult(orgId: string, force: boolean = false): Promise<ReadResult<SupabaseJudge[]>> {
  1406	    const cleanOrgId = orgId.trim().toUpperCase();
  1407	    const cacheKey = `${STORAGE_KEYS.JUDGES}_${cleanOrgId}`;
  1408	    const cachedJudges = this.getCachedJudges(cleanOrgId);
  1409	
  1410	    // 5-Minute SWR Cache Throttle
  1411	    if (!force && isCacheValid(cacheKey)) {
  1412	      return { ok: true, data: cachedJudges, fromCache: true, error: null, timedOut: false };
  1413	    }
  1414	
  1415	    return coalesceRead(cacheKey, force, async () => {
  1416	      const latestCached = this.getCachedJudges(cleanOrgId);
  1417	
  1418	      if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
  1419	        try {
  1420	          const res = await callSecuredReadRpc<any[]>('read_judges', {});
  1421	          const data = Array.isArray(res.data) ? res.data : [];
  1422	          const isOrganiser = getStoredSession()?.role === 'organiser';
  1423	          const prevPinById = new Map(latestCached.map((j) => [j.id, j.pin]));
  1424	          const normalized = data.map((row) => {
  1425	            const norm = normalizeJudge(row);
  1426	            if (isOrganiser && !norm.pin) {
  1427	              const cachedPin = prevPinById.get(norm.id);
  1428	              if (cachedPin) {
  1429	                return { ...norm, pin: cachedPin, judge_pin: cachedPin };
  1430	              }
  1431	            }
  1432	            return norm;
  1433	          });
  1434	          const currentJudges = loadCache<SupabaseJudge>(STORAGE_KEYS.JUDGES, []);
  1435	          const otherJudges = currentJudges.filter(
  1436	            (j) => (j.org_id || '').trim().toUpperCase() !== cleanOrgId
  1437	          );
  1438	          const changed = !areRecordsEqual(latestCached, normalized);
  1439	          saveCache(STORAGE_KEYS.JUDGES, [...otherJudges, ...normalized], !changed);
  1440	          updateCacheTimestamp(cacheKey);
  1441	          return { ok: true, data: normalized, fromCache: false, error: null, timedOut: false };
  1442	        } catch (err: any) {
  1443	          const info = classifyReadError(err);
  1444	          console.warn('Supabase getJudges fallback:', info.message);
  1445	          return {
  1446	            ok: false,
  1447	            data: latestCached,
  1448	            fromCache: latestCached.length > 0,
  1449	            error: info.message,
  1450	            errorCode: info.code,
  1451	            timedOut: info.timedOut,
  1452	            unauthorized: info.unauthorized,
  1453	          };
  1454	        }
  1455	      } else {
  1456	        updateCacheTimestamp(cacheKey);
  1457	        return { ok: true, data: latestCached, fromCache: true, error: null, timedOut: false };
  1458	      }
  1459	    });
  1460	  },
  1461	
  1462	  async getJudges(orgId: string, force: boolean = false): Promise<SupabaseJudge[]> {
  1463	    const res = await this.getJudgesResult(orgId, force);
  1464	    return res.data;
  1465	  },
  1466	
  1467	  async getJudgeByIdResult(judgeId: string): Promise<ReadResult<SupabaseJudge | null>> {
  1468	    return coalesceRead(`judge_by_id_${judgeId}`, false, async () => {
  1469	      const judges = loadCache<SupabaseJudge>(STORAGE_KEYS.JUDGES, []);
  1470	      const found = judges.find((j) => j.id === judgeId || j.judge_id === judgeId);
  1471	      const cachedJudge = found ? normalizeJudge(found) : null;
  1472	
  1473	      if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
  1474	        try {
  1475	          const res = await callSecuredReadRpc<any[]>('read_judges', { p_judge_id: judgeId });
  1476	          const rows = Array.isArray(res.data) ? res.data : [];
  1477	          if (rows[0]) {
  1478	            return {
  1479	              ok: true,
  1480	              data: normalizeJudge(rows[0]),
  1481	              fromCache: false,
  1482	              error: null,
  1483	              timedOut: false,
  1484	            };
  1485	          }
  1486	          return { ok: true, data: null, fromCache: false, error: null, timedOut: false, notFound: true };
  1487	        } catch (err: any) {
  1488	          const info = classifyReadError(err);
  1489	          console.warn('Supabase getJudgeById fallback:', info.message);
  1490	          return {
  1491	            ok: false,
  1492	            data: cachedJudge,
  1493	            fromCache: Boolean(cachedJudge),
  1494	            error: info.message,
  1495	            errorCode: info.code,
  1496	            timedOut: info.timedOut,
  1497	            unauthorized: info.unauthorized,
  1498	          };
  1499	        }
  1500	      }
  1501	
  1502	      return {
  1503	        ok: Boolean(cachedJudge),
  1504	        data: cachedJudge,
  1505	        fromCache: Boolean(cachedJudge),
  1506	        error: cachedJudge ? null : 'Judge not found locally',
  1507	        timedOut: false,
  1508	        notFound: !cachedJudge,
  1509	      };
  1510	    });
  1511	  },
  1512	
  1513	  async getJudgeById(judgeId: string): Promise<SupabaseJudge | null> {
  1514	    const res = await this.getJudgeByIdResult(judgeId);
  1515	    return res.data;
  1516	  },
  1517	
  1518	  async createJudge(
  1519	    orgId: string,
  1520	    name: string,
  1521	    pin?: string,
  1522	    customJudgeId?: string
  1523	  ): Promise<SupabaseJudge> {
  1524	    const cleanOrgId = orgId.trim().toUpperCase();
  1525	    const judges = loadCache<SupabaseJudge>(STORAGE_KEYS.JUDGES, []);
  1526	    const existingOrgJudges = judges.filter((j) => j.org_id.toUpperCase() === cleanOrgId);
  1527	    const assignedJudgeId =
  1528	      customJudgeId?.trim() || `J-${String(existingOrgJudges.length + 1).padStart(2, '0')}`;
  1529	    const generatedPin = pin?.trim() || Math.floor(1000 + Math.random() * 9000).toString();
  1530	    const newId = generateUUID();
  1531	
  1532	    const newJudge = normalizeJudge({
  1533	      id: newId,
  1534	      judge_id: assignedJudgeId,
  1535	      org_id: cleanOrgId,
  1536	      name: name.trim(),
  1537	      pin: generatedPin,
  1538	      assigned_events: [],
  1539	      current_session_token: null,
  1540	      created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
  1541	    });
  1542	
  1543	    // 1. OPTIMISTIC WRITE: Save to local cache immediately & notify UI
  1544	    const judgesSnapshot = [...judges];
  1545	    saveCache(STORAGE_KEYS.JUDGES, [newJudge, ...judges], false);
  1546	    invalidateCache(`${STORAGE_KEYS.JUDGES}_${cleanOrgId}`);
  1547	
  1548	    const payload = {
  1549	      id: newJudge.id,
  1550	      org_id: newJudge.org_id,
  1551	      name: newJudge.name,
  1552	      judge_id: newJudge.judge_id,
  1553	      pin: newJudge.pin,
  1554	      assigned_events: newJudge.assigned_events,
  1555	    };
  1556	
  1557	    // 2. BACKGROUND CLOUD SYNC
  1558	    if (isSupabaseConfigured() && isOnlineNavigator()) {
  1559	      try {
  1560	        const rpcRes = await callSecuredWriteRpc<SupabaseJudge>('judge_upsert', { p_record: payload });
  1561	        const createdRow = rpcRes.data;
  1562	        if (createdRow) {
  1563	          const synced = normalizeJudge(createdRow);
  1564	          const currentList = loadCache<SupabaseJudge>(STORAGE_KEYS.JUDGES, []);
  1565	          saveCache(
  1566	            STORAGE_KEYS.JUDGES,
  1567	            currentList.map((j) => (j.id === synced.id ? synced : j)),
  1568	            true
  1569	          );
  1570	        }
  1571	      } catch (err: any) {
  1572	        if (isServerRejectionError(err)) {
  1573	          saveCache(STORAGE_KEYS.JUDGES, judgesSnapshot, false);
  1574	          throw err;
  1575	        }
  1576	        console.warn('Offline mode: judge saved locally, deferring cloud sync:', err?.message || err);
  1577	        await queueMutationLocally('judges', 'INSERT', newJudge.id, payload);
  1578	      }
  1579	    } else if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
  1580	      await queueMutationLocally('judges', 'INSERT', newJudge.id, payload);
  1581	    }
  1582	
  1583	    return newJudge;
  1584	  },
  1585	
  1586	  async updateJudge(judgeId: string, updates: Partial<SupabaseJudge>): Promise<SupabaseJudge> {
  1587	    const cleanPin = updates.pin?.trim() || updates.judge_pin?.trim();
  1588	    const judgesSnapshot = loadCache<SupabaseJudge>(STORAGE_KEYS.JUDGES, []);
  1589	    const judges = [...judgesSnapshot];
  1590	    const idx = judges.findIndex((j) => j.id === judgeId || j.judge_id === judgeId);
  1591	    const existing = idx !== -1 ? judges[idx] : null;
  1592	
  1593	    const updated = normalizeJudge({
  1594	      ...(existing || {}),
  1595	      ...updates,
  1596	      pin: cleanPin || existing?.pin || '0000',
  1597	    });
  1598	
  1599	    // 1. OPTIMISTIC WRITE: Save to local cache & notify UI
  1600	    if (idx !== -1) {
  1601	      judges[idx] = updated;
  1602	    } else {
  1603	      judges.unshift(updated);
  1604	    }
  1605	    saveCache(STORAGE_KEYS.JUDGES, judges, false);
  1606	    if (updated.org_id) {
  1607	      invalidateCache(`${STORAGE_KEYS.JUDGES}_${updated.org_id.toUpperCase()}`);
  1608	    }
  1609	
  1610	    const dbUpdates: Record<string, any> = { ...updates };
  1611	    if (cleanPin) dbUpdates.pin = cleanPin;
  1612	    delete dbUpdates.judge_pin;
  1613	
  1614	    // 2. BACKGROUND CLOUD SYNC
  1615	    if (isSupabaseConfigured() && isOnlineNavigator()) {
  1616	      try {
  1617	        const rpcRes = await callSecuredWriteRpc<SupabaseJudge>('judge_update', {
  1618	          p_judge_id: judgeId,
  1619	          p_updates: dbUpdates,
  1620	        });
  1621	        const syncedRow = rpcRes.data;
  1622	        if (syncedRow) {
  1623	          const synced = normalizeJudge(syncedRow);
  1624	          const currentList = loadCache<SupabaseJudge>(STORAGE_KEYS.JUDGES, []);
  1625	          saveCache(
  1626	            STORAGE_KEYS.JUDGES,
  1627	            currentList.map((j) => (j.id === judgeId ? synced : j)),
  1628	            true
  1629	          );
  1630	        }
  1631	      } catch (err: any) {
  1632	        if (isServerRejectionError(err)) {
  1633	          saveCache(STORAGE_KEYS.JUDGES, judgesSnapshot, false);
  1634	          throw err;
  1635	        }
  1636	        console.warn('Offline mode: judge update saved locally, deferring cloud sync:', err?.message || err);
  1637	        await queueMutationLocally('judges', 'UPDATE', judgeId, dbUpdates);
  1638	      }
  1639	    } else if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
  1640	      await queueMutationLocally('judges', 'UPDATE', judgeId, dbUpdates);
  1641	    }
  1642	
  1643	    return updated;
  1644	  },
  1645	
  1646	  async deleteJudge(judgeId: string): Promise<void> {
  1647	    // PRE-FLIGHT LOCK CHECK: Check ALL events before any mutation
  1648	    const allEvents = loadCache<SupabaseEvent>(STORAGE_KEYS.EVENTS, []);
  1649	    const lockedEvent = allEvents.find(
  1650	      (e) => e.is_locked && (e.assigned_judges || []).some((j) => j === judgeId)
  1651	    );
  1652	    if (lockedEvent) {
  1653	      const err = new Error(`ROSTER_LOCKED: Cannot delete judge. Assigned to locked event "${lockedEvent.name}". Unlock event first.`);
  1654	      (err as any).code = 'ROSTER_LOCKED';
  1655	      throw err;
  1656	    }
  1657	
  1658	    const judgesSnapshot = loadCache<SupabaseJudge>(STORAGE_KEYS.JUDGES, []);
  1659	    const target = judgesSnapshot.find((j) => j.id === judgeId || j.judge_id === judgeId);
  1660	    const remaining = judgesSnapshot.filter((j) => j.id !== judgeId && j.judge_id !== judgeId);
  1661	    saveCache(STORAGE_KEYS.JUDGES, remaining, false);
  1662	    if (target?.org_id) {
  1663	      invalidateCache(`${STORAGE_KEYS.JUDGES}_${target.org_id.toUpperCase()}`);
  1664	    }
  1665	
  1666	    if (isSupabaseConfigured() && isOnlineNavigator()) {
  1667	      try {
  1668	        await callSecuredWriteRpc('judge_delete', { p_judge_id: judgeId });
  1669	      } catch (err: any) {
  1670	        if (isServerRejectionError(err)) {
  1671	          saveCache(STORAGE_KEYS.JUDGES, judgesSnapshot, false);
  1672	          throw err;
  1673	        }
  1674	        console.warn('Offline mode: judge deletion saved locally, deferring cloud sync:', err?.message || err);
  1675	        await queueMutationLocally('judges', 'DELETE', judgeId, null);
  1676	      }
  1677	    } else if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
  1678	      await queueMutationLocally('judges', 'DELETE', judgeId, null);
  1679	    }
  1680	  },
  1681	
  1682	  // ------------------------------------------
  1683	  // 5. PARTICIPANTS (Token-Checked; read_participants omits mobile_number)
  1684	  // ------------------------------------------
  1685	  async getParticipantsResult(
  1686	    orgId: string,
  1687	    force: boolean = false
  1688	  ): Promise<ReadResult<SupabaseParticipant[]>> {
  1689	    const cleanOrgId = orgId.trim().toUpperCase();
  1690	    const cacheKey = `${STORAGE_KEYS.PARTICIPANTS}_${cleanOrgId}`;
  1691	    const cached = this.getCachedParticipants(cleanOrgId);
  1692	
  1693	    // 5-Minute SWR Cache Throttle
  1694	    if (!force && isCacheValid(cacheKey)) {
  1695	      return { ok: true, data: cached, fromCache: true, error: null, timedOut: false };
  1696	    }
  1697	
  1698	    return coalesceRead(cacheKey, force, async () => {
  1699	      const latestCached = this.getCachedParticipants(cleanOrgId);
  1700	
  1701	      if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
  1702	        try {
  1703	          const res = await callSecuredReadRpc<any[]>('read_participants', {});
  1704	          const data = Array.isArray(res.data) ? (res.data as SupabaseParticipant[]) : [];
  1705	          const isOrganiser = getStoredSession()?.role === 'organiser';
  1706	          const prevMobileById = new Map(latestCached.map((p) => [p.id, p.mobile_number]));
  1707	          const mergedData = data.map((p) => {
  1708	            if (isOrganiser && !p.mobile_number) {
  1709	              const cachedMobile = prevMobileById.get(p.id);
  1710	              if (cachedMobile) {
  1711	                return { ...p, mobile_number: cachedMobile };
  1712	              }
  1713	            }
  1714	            return p;
  1715	          });
  1716	          const allCached = loadCache<SupabaseParticipant>(STORAGE_KEYS.PARTICIPANTS, []);
  1717	          const otherOrgParticipants = allCached.filter(
  1718	            (p) => (p.org_id || '').trim().toUpperCase() !== cleanOrgId
  1719	          );
  1720	          const changed = !areRecordsEqual(latestCached, mergedData);
  1721	          saveCache(STORAGE_KEYS.PARTICIPANTS, [...mergedData, ...otherOrgParticipants], !changed);
  1722	          updateCacheTimestamp(cacheKey);
  1723	          return { ok: true, data: mergedData, fromCache: false, error: null, timedOut: false };
  1724	        } catch (err: any) {
  1725	          const info = classifyReadError(err);
  1726	          console.warn('Supabase getParticipants fallback:', info.message);
  1727	          return {
  1728	            ok: false,
  1729	            data: latestCached,
  1730	            fromCache: latestCached.length > 0,
  1731	            error: info.message,
  1732	            errorCode: info.code,
  1733	            timedOut: info.timedOut,
  1734	            unauthorized: info.unauthorized,
  1735	          };
  1736	        }
  1737	      } else {
  1738	        updateCacheTimestamp(cacheKey);
  1739	        return { ok: true, data: latestCached, fromCache: true, error: null, timedOut: false };
  1740	      }
  1741	    });
  1742	  },
  1743	
  1744	  async getParticipants(orgId: string, force: boolean = false): Promise<SupabaseParticipant[]> {
  1745	    const res = await this.getParticipantsResult(orgId, force);
  1746	    return res.data;
  1747	  },
  1748	
  1749	  /**
  1750	   * Organiser-only roster read (PeopleTab & encrypted backups) returning Judge PINs and Participant contacts.
  1751	   * Strictly gated by org_sessions token on the server.
  1752	   */
  1753	  async getOrgRosterCredentialsResult(
  1754	    orgId: string,
  1755	    force: boolean = false
  1756	  ): Promise<ReadResult<{ judges: SupabaseJudge[]; participants: SupabaseParticipant[] }>> {
  1757	    const cleanOrgId = orgId.trim().toUpperCase();
  1758	    const cacheKey = `${STORAGE_KEYS.JUDGES}_roster_${cleanOrgId}`;
  1759	    const cachedJudges = this.getCachedJudges(cleanOrgId);
  1760	    const cachedParticipants = this.getCachedParticipants(cleanOrgId);
  1761	    const hasJudgePins = cachedJudges.length === 0 || cachedJudges.every((j) => Boolean(j.pin));
  1762	
  1763	    if (!force && isCacheValid(cacheKey) && hasJudgePins && (cachedJudges.length > 0 || cachedParticipants.length > 0)) {
  1764	      return {
  1765	        ok: true,
  1766	        data: { judges: cachedJudges, participants: cachedParticipants },
  1767	        fromCache: true,
  1768	        error: null,
  1769	        timedOut: false,
  1770	      };
  1771	    }
  1772	
  1773	    return coalesceRead(cacheKey, force, async () => {
  1774	      const latestJudges = this.getCachedJudges(cleanOrgId);
  1775	      const latestParticipants = this.getCachedParticipants(cleanOrgId);
  1776	
  1777	      if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
  1778	        try {
  1779	          let rawJudges: any[] = [];
  1780	          let rawParticipants: any[] = [];
  1781	
  1782	          try {
  1783	            const [judgesRes, partsRes] = await Promise.all([
  1784	              callSecuredReadRpc<any[]>('judges_list_with_pins', { p_org_id: cleanOrgId }),
  1785	              callSecuredReadRpc<any[]>('participants_list', { p_org_id: cleanOrgId }),
  1786	            ]);
  1787	            rawJudges = Array.isArray(judgesRes.data) ? judgesRes.data : [];
  1788	            rawParticipants = Array.isArray(partsRes.data) ? partsRes.data : [];
  1789	          } catch (phase3Err: any) {
  1790	            if (phase3Err?.code !== 'rpc_unavailable') {
  1791	              throw phase3Err;
  1792	            }
  1793	            const res = await callSecuredReadRpc('read_org_roster_credentials', {});
  1794	            rawJudges = Array.isArray(res.judges) ? res.judges : [];
  1795	            rawParticipants = Array.isArray(res.participants) ? res.participants : [];
  1796	          }
  1797	
  1798	          const judges = rawJudges.map(normalizeJudge);
  1799	          const participants = rawParticipants as SupabaseParticipant[];
  1800	
  1801	          const allCachedJudges = loadCache<SupabaseJudge>(STORAGE_KEYS.JUDGES, []);
  1802	          const otherJudges = allCachedJudges.filter(
  1803	            (j) => (j.org_id || '').trim().toUpperCase() !== cleanOrgId
  1804	          );
  1805	          const judgesChanged = !areRecordsEqual(latestJudges, judges);
  1806	          saveCache(STORAGE_KEYS.JUDGES, [...otherJudges, ...judges], !judgesChanged);
  1807	          updateCacheTimestamp(`${STORAGE_KEYS.JUDGES}_${cleanOrgId}`);
  1808	
  1809	          const allCachedParticipants = loadCache<SupabaseParticipant>(STORAGE_KEYS.PARTICIPANTS, []);
  1810	          const otherParticipants = allCachedParticipants.filter(
  1811	            (p) => (p.org_id || '').trim().toUpperCase() !== cleanOrgId
  1812	          );
  1813	          const participantsChanged = !areRecordsEqual(latestParticipants, participants);
  1814	          saveCache(
  1815	            STORAGE_KEYS.PARTICIPANTS,
  1816	            [...participants, ...otherParticipants],
  1817	            !participantsChanged
  1818	          );
  1819	          updateCacheTimestamp(`${STORAGE_KEYS.PARTICIPANTS}_${cleanOrgId}`);
  1820	          updateCacheTimestamp(cacheKey);
  1821	
  1822	          return {
  1823	            ok: true,
  1824	            data: { judges, participants },
  1825	            fromCache: false,
  1826	            error: null,
  1827	            timedOut: false,
  1828	          };
  1829	        } catch (err: any) {
  1830	          const info = classifyReadError(err);
  1831	          console.warn('Supabase getOrgRosterCredentials fallback:', info.message);
  1832	          return {
  1833	            ok: false,
  1834	            data: { judges: latestJudges, participants: latestParticipants },
  1835	            fromCache: latestJudges.length > 0 || latestParticipants.length > 0,
  1836	            error: info.message,
  1837	            errorCode: info.code,
  1838	            timedOut: info.timedOut,
  1839	            unauthorized: info.unauthorized,
  1840	          };
  1841	        }
  1842	      } else {
  1843	        updateCacheTimestamp(cacheKey);
  1844	        return {
  1845	          ok: true,
  1846	          data: { judges: latestJudges, participants: latestParticipants },
  1847	          fromCache: true,
  1848	          error: null,
  1849	          timedOut: false,
  1850	        };
  1851	      }
  1852	    });
  1853	  },
  1854	
  1855	  async createParticipant(
  1856	    orgId: string,
  1857	    data: {
  1858	      identity_id?: string;
  1859	      sl_no?: string;
  1860	      name: string;
  1861	      school?: string;
  1862	      class_or_age?: string;
  1863	      mobile_number?: string;
  1864	      custom_values?: Record<string, string>;
  1865	    }
  1866	  ): Promise<SupabaseParticipant> {
  1867	    const cleanOrgId = orgId.trim().toUpperCase();
  1868	    const participants = loadCache<SupabaseParticipant>(STORAGE_KEYS.PARTICIPANTS, []);
  1869	    const existingOrgParticipants = participants.filter(
  1870	      (p) => p.org_id.toUpperCase() === cleanOrgId
  1871	    );
  1872	    const assignedSl =
  1873	      data.sl_no?.trim() || String(existingOrgParticipants.length + 1).padStart(2, '0');
  1874	    const newId = generateUUID();
  1875	    const now = new Date().toISOString();
  1876	
  1877	    const newParticipant: SupabaseParticipant = {
  1878	      id: newId,
  1879	      org_id: cleanOrgId,
  1880	      identity_id: data.identity_id?.trim() || generateIdentityId(),
  1881	      sl_no: assignedSl,
  1882	      name: data.name.trim(),
  1883	      school: data.school?.trim() || 'General',
  1884	      class_or_age: data.class_or_age?.trim() || '',
  1885	      mobile_number: data.mobile_number?.trim() || '',
  1886	      custom_values: data.custom_values || {},
  1887	      created_at: now,
  1888	    };
  1889	
  1890	    // 1. OPTIMISTIC WRITE: Save to local cache immediately & notify UI
  1891	    const participantsSnapshot = [...participants];
  1892	    saveCache(STORAGE_KEYS.PARTICIPANTS, [newParticipant, ...participants], false);
  1893	    invalidateCache(`${STORAGE_KEYS.PARTICIPANTS}_${cleanOrgId}`);
  1894	
  1895	    const payload = {
  1896	      id: newParticipant.id,
  1897	      org_id: newParticipant.org_id,
  1898	      identity_id: newParticipant.identity_id,
  1899	      sl_no: newParticipant.sl_no,
  1900	      name: newParticipant.name,
  1901	      school: newParticipant.school,
  1902	      class_or_age: newParticipant.class_or_age,
  1903	      mobile_number: newParticipant.mobile_number,
  1904	      custom_values: newParticipant.custom_values,
  1905	    };
  1906	
  1907	    // 2. BACKGROUND CLOUD SYNC
  1908	    if (isSupabaseConfigured() && isOnlineNavigator()) {
  1909	      try {
  1910	        const rpcRes = await callSecuredWriteRpc<SupabaseParticipant[]>('participant_upsert_batch', {
  1911	          p_records: [payload],
  1912	        });
  1913	        const createdRow = Array.isArray(rpcRes.data) ? rpcRes.data[0] : null;
  1914	        if (createdRow) {
  1915	          const currentList = loadCache<SupabaseParticipant>(STORAGE_KEYS.PARTICIPANTS, []);
  1916	          saveCache(
  1917	            STORAGE_KEYS.PARTICIPANTS,
  1918	            currentList.map((p) => (p.id === createdRow.id ? createdRow : p)),
  1919	            true
  1920	          );
  1921	        }
  1922	      } catch (err: any) {
  1923	        if (isServerRejectionError(err)) {
  1924	          saveCache(STORAGE_KEYS.PARTICIPANTS, participantsSnapshot, false);
  1925	          throw err;
  1926	        }
  1927	        console.warn('Offline mode: participant saved locally, deferring cloud sync:', err?.message || err);
  1928	        await queueMutationLocally('participants', 'INSERT', newParticipant.id, payload);
  1929	      }
  1930	    } else if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
  1931	      await queueMutationLocally('participants', 'INSERT', newParticipant.id, payload);
  1932	    }
  1933	
  1934	    return newParticipant;
  1935	  },
  1936	
  1937	  async bulkCreateParticipants(
  1938	    orgId: string,
  1939	    rows: Array<{
  1940	      identity_id?: string;
  1941	      sl_no?: string;
  1942	      name: string;
  1943	      school?: string;
  1944	      class_or_age?: string;
  1945	      mobile_number?: string;
  1946	      custom_values?: Record<string, string>;
  1947	    }>
  1948	  ): Promise<number> {
  1949	    const cleanOrgId = orgId.trim().toUpperCase();
  1950	    const participants = loadCache<SupabaseParticipant>(STORAGE_KEYS.PARTICIPANTS, []);
  1951	    const existingOrgCount = participants.filter(
  1952	      (p) => p.org_id.toUpperCase() === cleanOrgId
  1953	    ).length;
  1954	
  1955	    const usedIds = new Set<string>(participants.map((p) => p.identity_id));
  1956	    const makeUniqueIdentityId = (provided?: string) => {
  1957	      let candidate = provided?.trim() || generateIdentityId();
  1958	      while (usedIds.has(candidate)) {
  1959	        candidate = generateIdentityId();
  1960	      }
  1961	      usedIds.add(candidate);
  1962	      return candidate;
  1963	    };
  1964	
  1965	    const newItems: SupabaseParticipant[] = rows.map((r, i) => ({
  1966	      id: generateUUID(),
  1967	      org_id: cleanOrgId,
  1968	      identity_id: makeUniqueIdentityId(r.identity_id),
  1969	      sl_no: r.sl_no ? r.sl_no.trim() : String(existingOrgCount + i + 1).padStart(2, '0'),
  1970	      name: r.name.trim(),
  1971	      school: r.school ? r.school.trim() : 'General',
  1972	      class_or_age: r.class_or_age ? r.class_or_age.trim() : '',
  1973	      mobile_number: r.mobile_number ? r.mobile_number.trim() : '',
  1974	      custom_values: r.custom_values || {},
  1975	      created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
  1976	    }));
  1977	
  1978	    // 1. OPTIMISTIC WRITE: Save to local cache immediately & notify UI
  1979	    const participantsSnapshot = [...participants];
  1980	    saveCache(STORAGE_KEYS.PARTICIPANTS, [...newItems, ...participants], false);
  1981	    invalidateCache(`${STORAGE_KEYS.PARTICIPANTS}_${cleanOrgId}`);
  1982	
  1983	    const payloads = newItems.map((item) => ({
  1984	      id: item.id,
  1985	      org_id: item.org_id,
  1986	      identity_id: item.identity_id,
  1987	      sl_no: item.sl_no,
  1988	      name: item.name,
  1989	      school: item.school,
  1990	      class_or_age: item.class_or_age,
  1991	      mobile_number: item.mobile_number,
  1992	      custom_values: item.custom_values,
  1993	    }));
  1994	
  1995	    // 2. BACKGROUND CLOUD SYNC
  1996	    if (isSupabaseConfigured() && isOnlineNavigator()) {
  1997	      try {
  1998	        const rpcRes = await callSecuredWriteRpc<SupabaseParticipant[]>('participant_upsert_batch', {
  1999	          p_records: payloads,
  2000	        });
  2001	        const createdRows = rpcRes.data;
  2002	        if (createdRows) {
  2003	          const currentList = loadCache<SupabaseParticipant>(STORAGE_KEYS.PARTICIPANTS, []);
  2004	          const dataMap = new Map(createdRows.map((d: any) => [d.id, d]));
  2005	          saveCache(
  2006	            STORAGE_KEYS.PARTICIPANTS,
  2007	            currentList.map((p) => (dataMap.has(p.id) ? dataMap.get(p.id)! : p)),
  2008	            true
  2009	          );
  2010	        }
  2011	      } catch (err: any) {
  2012	        if (isServerRejectionError(err)) {
  2013	          saveCache(STORAGE_KEYS.PARTICIPANTS, participantsSnapshot, false);
  2014	          throw err;
  2015	        }
  2016	        console.warn('Offline mode: bulk participants saved locally, deferring cloud sync:', err?.message || err);
  2017	        await queueMutationLocally('participants', 'INSERT', `bulk_${Date.now()}`, payloads);
  2018	      }
  2019	    } else if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
  2020	      await queueMutationLocally('participants', 'INSERT', `bulk_${Date.now()}`, payloads);
  2021	    }
  2022	
  2023	    return newItems.length;
  2024	  },
  2025	
  2026	  async updateParticipant(
  2027	    participantId: string,
  2028	    updates: Partial<SupabaseParticipant>
  2029	  ): Promise<SupabaseParticipant> {
  2030	    const safeUpdates = { ...updates };
  2031	    delete safeUpdates.identity_id;
  2032	
  2033	    const participantsSnapshot = loadCache<SupabaseParticipant>(STORAGE_KEYS.PARTICIPANTS, []);
  2034	    const participants = [...participantsSnapshot];
  2035	    const idx = participants.findIndex((p) => p.id === participantId);
  2036	    const existing = idx !== -1 ? participants[idx] : null;
  2037	
  2038	    const updated = {
  2039	      ...(existing || { id: participantId, name: 'Participant', org_id: '' }),
  2040	      ...safeUpdates,
  2041	    } as SupabaseParticipant;
  2042	
  2043	    // 1. OPTIMISTIC WRITE: Update cache & notify UI
  2044	    if (idx !== -1) {
  2045	      participants[idx] = updated;
  2046	    } else {
  2047	      participants.unshift(updated);
  2048	    }
  2049	    saveCache(STORAGE_KEYS.PARTICIPANTS, participants, false);
  2050	    if (updated.org_id) {
  2051	      invalidateCache(`${STORAGE_KEYS.PARTICIPANTS}_${updated.org_id.toUpperCase()}`);
  2052	    }
  2053	
  2054	    // 2. BACKGROUND CLOUD SYNC
  2055	    if (isSupabaseConfigured() && isOnlineNavigator()) {
  2056	      try {
  2057	        const rpcRes = await callSecuredWriteRpc<SupabaseParticipant>('participant_update', {
  2058	          p_participant_id: participantId,
  2059	          p_updates: safeUpdates,
  2060	        });
  2061	        const updatedRow = rpcRes.data;
  2062	        if (updatedRow) {
  2063	          const currentList = loadCache<SupabaseParticipant>(STORAGE_KEYS.PARTICIPANTS, []);
  2064	          saveCache(
  2065	            STORAGE_KEYS.PARTICIPANTS,
  2066	            currentList.map((p) => (p.id === participantId ? updatedRow : p)),
  2067	            true
  2068	          );
  2069	        }
  2070	      } catch (err: any) {
  2071	        if (isServerRejectionError(err)) {
  2072	          saveCache(STORAGE_KEYS.PARTICIPANTS, participantsSnapshot, false);
  2073	          throw err;
  2074	        }
  2075	        console.warn('Offline mode: participant update saved locally, deferring cloud sync:', err?.message || err);
  2076	        await queueMutationLocally('participants', 'UPDATE', participantId, safeUpdates);
  2077	      }
  2078	    } else if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
  2079	      await queueMutationLocally('participants', 'UPDATE', participantId, safeUpdates);
  2080	    }
  2081	
  2082	    return updated;
  2083	  },
  2084	
  2085	  /**
  2086	   * Smart Deletion & Ledger Integrity Guardrail (Directive 3)
  2087	   * 1. Ended Events (Hard Block): If participant has finalized scores in ended/archived event, block deletion.
  2088	   * 2. Ongoing Events (Warning): If participant is in active/draft events, require user confirmation.
  2089	   * 3. No Events (Safe): Immediate clean deletion.
  2090	   */
  2091	  async checkParticipantDeletion(participantId: string): Promise<{
  2092	    tier: 'blocked' | 'warning' | 'safe';
  2093	    reason: string;
  2094	    eventNames: string[];
  2095	  }> {
  2096	    const allEvents = loadCache<SupabaseEvent>(STORAGE_KEYS.EVENTS, []);
  2097	    const allScores = this.getCachedScores();
  2098	
  2099	    // Tier 1: Ended Events Check (The Hard Block)
  2100	    const endedEventsWithParticipant = allEvents.filter((e) => {
  2101	      const isEnded = e.status === 'ended' || e.status === 'archived';
  2102	      if (!isEnded) return false;
  2103	      const isAssigned = (e.assigned_participants || []).includes(participantId);
  2104	      const hasScores = allScores.some((s) => s.participant_id === participantId && s.event_id === e.id);
  2105	      return isAssigned || hasScores;
  2106	    });
  2107	
  2108	    if (endedEventsWithParticipant.length > 0) {
  2109	      return {
  2110	        tier: 'blocked',
  2111	        reason: 'ended_events',
  2112	        eventNames: endedEventsWithParticipant.map((e) => e.name),
  2113	      };
  2114	    }
  2115	
  2116	    // Tier 2: Ongoing/Draft Events Check (Warning & Override)
  2117	    const ongoingEvents = allEvents.filter((e) => {
  2118	      const isOngoing = e.status !== 'ended' && e.status !== 'archived';
  2119	      return isOngoing && (e.assigned_participants || []).includes(participantId);
  2120	    });
  2121	
  2122	    if (ongoingEvents.length > 0) {
  2123	      return {
  2124	        tier: 'warning',
  2125	        reason: 'ongoing_events',
  2126	        eventNames: ongoingEvents.map((e) => e.name),
  2127	      };
  2128	    }
  2129	
  2130	    // Tier 3: No Events (Safe)
  2131	    return {
  2132	      tier: 'safe',
  2133	      reason: 'no_events',
  2134	      eventNames: [],
  2135	    };
  2136	  },
  2137	
  2138	  async deleteParticipant(participantId: string, forceOverrideActive: boolean = false): Promise<void> {
  2139	    // PRE-FLIGHT LOCK CHECK: Check ALL events before any mutation or forceOverrideActive branch
  2140	    const allEvents = loadCache<SupabaseEvent>(STORAGE_KEYS.EVENTS, []);
  2141	    const lockedEvent = allEvents.find(
  2142	      (e) => e.is_locked && (e.assigned_participants || []).includes(participantId)
  2143	    );
  2144	    if (lockedEvent) {
  2145	      const err = new Error(`ROSTER_LOCKED: Cannot delete participant. Enrolled in locked event "${lockedEvent.name}". Unlock event first.`);
  2146	      (err as any).code = 'ROSTER_LOCKED';
  2147	      throw err;
  2148	    }
  2149	
  2150	    const safety = await this.checkParticipantDeletion(participantId);
  2151	
  2152	    if (safety.tier === 'blocked') {
  2153	      throw new Error('Cannot delete: Participant has finalized scores in a completed event.');
  2154	    }
  2155	
  2156	    if (safety.tier === 'warning' && !forceOverrideActive) {
  2157	      throw new Error('This participant is currently enrolled in active events. Deleting them will pull them from all active rosters. Proceed?');
  2158	    }
  2159	
  2160	    // If enrolled in active/draft events, clean from rosters
  2161	    if (safety.tier === 'warning' && forceOverrideActive) {
  2162	      const currentEvents = loadCache<SupabaseEvent>(STORAGE_KEYS.EVENTS, []);
  2163	      for (const evt of currentEvents) {
  2164	        if ((evt.assigned_participants || []).includes(participantId)) {
  2165	          const updatedAssigned = (evt.assigned_participants || []).filter((id) => id !== participantId);
  2166	          const updatedSl = { ...(evt.participant_sl_numbers || {}) };
  2167	          delete updatedSl[participantId];
  2168	          await this.updateEvent(evt.id, {
  2169	            assigned_participants: updatedAssigned,
  2170	            participant_sl_numbers: updatedSl,
  2171	          });
  2172	        }
  2173	      }
  2174	    }
  2175
