  2176	    const participantsSnapshot = loadCache<SupabaseParticipant>(STORAGE_KEYS.PARTICIPANTS, []);
  2177	    const target = participantsSnapshot.find((p) => p.id === participantId);
  2178	    const remaining = participantsSnapshot.filter((p) => p.id !== participantId);
  2179	    saveCache(STORAGE_KEYS.PARTICIPANTS, remaining, false);
  2180	    if (target?.org_id) {
  2181	      invalidateCache(`${STORAGE_KEYS.PARTICIPANTS}_${target.org_id.toUpperCase()}`);
  2182	    }
  2183	
  2184	    if (isSupabaseConfigured() && isOnlineNavigator()) {
  2185	      try {
  2186	        await callSecuredWriteRpc('participant_delete', { p_participant_id: participantId });
  2187	      } catch (err: any) {
  2188	        if (isServerRejectionError(err)) {
  2189	          saveCache(STORAGE_KEYS.PARTICIPANTS, participantsSnapshot, false);
  2190	          throw err;
  2191	        }
  2192	        console.warn('Offline mode: participant deletion saved locally, deferring cloud sync:', err?.message || err);
  2193	        await queueMutationLocally('participants', 'DELETE', participantId, null);
  2194	      }
  2195	    } else if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
  2196	      await queueMutationLocally('participants', 'DELETE', participantId, null);
  2197	    }
  2198	  },
  2199	
  2200	  // ------------------------------------------
  2201	  // 6. SCORES (Token-Checked, Event/Org Scoped + SWR)
  2202	  // ------------------------------------------
  2203	  async getScoresResult(eventId: string, force: boolean = false): Promise<ReadResult<SupabaseScore[]>> {
  2204	    const cacheKey = `${STORAGE_KEYS.SCORES}_${eventId}`;
  2205	    const allCached = this.getCachedScores();
  2206	    const eventCached = allCached.filter((s) => s.event_id === eventId);
  2207	
  2208	    // 5-Minute SWR Cache Throttle
  2209	    if (!force && isCacheValid(cacheKey)) {
  2210	      return { ok: true, data: eventCached, fromCache: true, error: null, timedOut: false };
  2211	    }
  2212	
  2213	    return coalesceRead(cacheKey, force, async () => {
  2214	      const latestEventCached = this.getCachedScores().filter((s) => s.event_id === eventId);
  2215	
  2216	      if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
  2217	        try {
  2218	          const res = await callSecuredReadRpc<any[]>('read_event_scores', { p_event_id: eventId });
  2219	          const data = Array.isArray(res.data) ? res.data : [];
  2220	          const normalized = data.map(normalizeScore);
  2221	          const existingCache = loadCache<SupabaseScore>(STORAGE_KEYS.SCORES, []).filter(
  2222	            (s) => s.event_id !== eventId
  2223	          );
  2224	          const changed = !areRecordsEqual(latestEventCached, normalized);
  2225	          saveCache(STORAGE_KEYS.SCORES, [...existingCache, ...normalized], !changed);
  2226	          updateCacheTimestamp(cacheKey);
  2227	          return { ok: true, data: normalized, fromCache: false, error: null, timedOut: false };
  2228	        } catch (err: any) {
  2229	          const info = classifyReadError(err);
  2230	          console.warn('Supabase getScores fallback:', info.message);
  2231	          return {
  2232	            ok: false,
  2233	            data: latestEventCached,
  2234	            fromCache: latestEventCached.length > 0,
  2235	            error: info.message,
  2236	            errorCode: info.code,
  2237	            timedOut: info.timedOut,
  2238	            unauthorized: info.unauthorized,
  2239	          };
  2240	        }
  2241	      } else {
  2242	        updateCacheTimestamp(cacheKey);
  2243	        return { ok: true, data: latestEventCached, fromCache: true, error: null, timedOut: false };
  2244	      }
  2245	    });
  2246	  },
  2247	
  2248	  async getScores(eventId: string, force: boolean = false): Promise<SupabaseScore[]> {
  2249	    const res = await this.getScoresResult(eventId, force);
  2250	    return res.data;
  2251	  },
  2252	
  2253	  async getOrgScoresResult(
  2254	    orgId: string,
  2255	    force: boolean = false
  2256	  ): Promise<ReadResult<SupabaseScore[]>> {
  2257	    const cleanOrgId = orgId.trim().toUpperCase();
  2258	    const cacheKey = `${STORAGE_KEYS.SCORES}_ORG_${cleanOrgId}`;
  2259	    const cached = this.getCachedScores(cleanOrgId);
  2260	
  2261	    // 5-Minute SWR Cache Throttle
  2262	    if (!force && isCacheValid(cacheKey)) {
  2263	      return { ok: true, data: cached, fromCache: true, error: null, timedOut: false };
  2264	    }
  2265	
  2266	    return coalesceRead(cacheKey, force, async () => {
  2267	      const latestCached = this.getCachedScores(cleanOrgId);
  2268	
  2269	      if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
  2270	        try {
  2271	          const res = await callSecuredReadRpc<any[]>('read_org_scores', {});
  2272	          const data = Array.isArray(res.data) ? res.data : [];
  2273	          const normalized = data.map(normalizeScore);
  2274	
  2275	          // Keep scores from other organisations untouched in IndexedDB mirror
  2276	          const orgEventIds = new Set(this.getCachedEvents(cleanOrgId).map((e) => e.id));
  2277	          const allExisting = loadCache<SupabaseScore>(STORAGE_KEYS.SCORES, []);
  2278	          const otherOrgScores = allExisting.filter((s) => !orgEventIds.has(s.event_id));
  2279	          const changed = !areRecordsEqual(latestCached, normalized);
  2280	          saveCache(STORAGE_KEYS.SCORES, [...otherOrgScores, ...normalized], !changed);
  2281	          updateCacheTimestamp(cacheKey);
  2282	          return { ok: true, data: normalized, fromCache: false, error: null, timedOut: false };
  2283	        } catch (err: any) {
  2284	          const info = classifyReadError(err);
  2285	          console.warn('Supabase getOrgScores fallback:', info.message);
  2286	          return {
  2287	            ok: false,
  2288	            data: latestCached,
  2289	            fromCache: latestCached.length > 0,
  2290	            error: info.message,
  2291	            errorCode: info.code,
  2292	            timedOut: info.timedOut,
  2293	            unauthorized: info.unauthorized,
  2294	          };
  2295	        }
  2296	      } else {
  2297	        updateCacheTimestamp(cacheKey);
  2298	        return { ok: true, data: latestCached, fromCache: true, error: null, timedOut: false };
  2299	      }
  2300	    });
  2301	  },
  2302	
  2303	  async getOrgScores(orgId: string, force: boolean = false): Promise<SupabaseScore[]> {
  2304	    const res = await this.getOrgScoresResult(orgId, force);
  2305	    return res.data;
  2306	  },
  2307	
  2308	  async getScoreForJudgeParticipantResult(
  2309	    eventId: string,
  2310	    judgeId: string,
  2311	    participantId: string
  2312	  ): Promise<ReadResult<SupabaseScore | null>> {
  2313	    const coalesceKey = `ballot_${eventId}_${judgeId}_${participantId}`;
  2314	    return coalesceRead(coalesceKey, false, async () => {
  2315	      let baseScore: SupabaseScore | null = null;
  2316	      let readError: string | null = null;
  2317	      let readErrorCode: string | null = null;
  2318	      let timedOut = false;
  2319	      let unauthorized = false;
  2320	      let fromCache = false;
  2321	
  2322	      if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
  2323	        try {
  2324	          const res = await callSecuredReadRpc<any>('read_score_for_ballot', {
  2325	            p_event_id: eventId,
  2326	            p_judge_id: judgeId,
  2327	            p_participant_id: participantId,
  2328	          });
  2329	          if (res.data) {
  2330	            baseScore = normalizeScore(res.data);
  2331	          }
  2332	        } catch (err: any) {
  2333	          const info = classifyReadError(err);
  2334	          readError = info.message;
  2335	          readErrorCode = info.code;
  2336	          timedOut = info.timedOut;
  2337	          unauthorized = info.unauthorized;
  2338	          console.warn('Supabase getScore fallback:', info.message);
  2339	        }
  2340	      }
  2341	
  2342	      if (!baseScore) {
  2343	        const scores = loadCache<SupabaseScore>(STORAGE_KEYS.SCORES, []);
  2344	        const match = scores.find(
  2345	          (s) =>
  2346	            s.event_id === eventId && s.judge_id === judgeId && s.participant_id === participantId
  2347	        );
  2348	        if (match) {
  2349	          baseScore = normalizeScore(match);
  2350	          fromCache = true;
  2351	        }
  2352	      }
  2353	
  2354	      // Merge non-failed pending offline ballots so offline scores remain visible on reload
  2355	      try {
  2356	        const pending = await getPendingScores();
  2357	        const matchingPending = pending
  2358	          .filter(
  2359	            (s) =>
  2360	              s.syncStatus !== 'failed' &&
  2361	              s.event_id === eventId &&
  2362	              s.judge_id === judgeId &&
  2363	              s.participant_id === participantId
  2364	          )
  2365	          .sort((a, b) => new Date(b.queuedAt).getTime() - new Date(a.queuedAt).getTime())[0];
  2366	
  2367	        if (matchingPending) {
  2368	          const pendingScore = normalizeScore({
  2369	            id: matchingPending.id,
  2370	            event_id: matchingPending.event_id,
  2371	            judge_id: matchingPending.judge_id,
  2372	            participant_id: matchingPending.participant_id,
  2373	            score_data: matchingPending.score_data || matchingPending.score_payload?.criteria || {},
  2374	            score_payload: matchingPending.score_payload,
  2375	            total_score: matchingPending.total_score,
  2376	            created_at: matchingPending.score_payload?.submitted_at || matchingPending.queuedAt,
  2377	          });
  2378	          if (
  2379	            !baseScore ||
  2380	            new Date(matchingPending.queuedAt).getTime() >= new Date(baseScore.created_at).getTime()
  2381	          ) {
  2382	            return {
  2383	              ok: readError === null,
  2384	              data: pendingScore,
  2385	              fromCache: true,
  2386	              error: readError,
  2387	              errorCode: readErrorCode,
  2388	              timedOut,
  2389	              unauthorized,
  2390	            };
  2391	          }
  2392	        }
  2393	      } catch {}
  2394	
  2395	      return {
  2396	        ok: readError === null,
  2397	        data: baseScore,
  2398	        fromCache,
  2399	        error: readError,
  2400	        errorCode: readErrorCode,
  2401	        timedOut,
  2402	        unauthorized,
  2403	        notFound: readError === null && !baseScore,
  2404	      };
  2405	    });
  2406	  },
  2407	
  2408	  async getScoreForJudgeParticipant(
  2409	    eventId: string,
  2410	    judgeId: string,
  2411	    participantId: string
  2412	  ): Promise<SupabaseScore | null> {
  2413	    const res = await this.getScoreForJudgeParticipantResult(eventId, judgeId, participantId);
  2414	    return res.data;
  2415	  },
  2416	
  2417	  async submitScore(scoreData: {
  2418	    id?: string;
  2419	    event_id: string;
  2420	    judge_id: string;
  2421	    participant_id: string;
  2422	    score_data?: Record<string, number>;
  2423	    total_score: number;
  2424	    score_payload?: {
  2425	      criteria: Record<string, number>;
  2426	      total_score: number;
  2427	      submitted_at: string;
  2428	      client_uuid?: string;
  2429	    };
  2430	  }): Promise<SupabaseScore> {
  2431	    // 1. Verify Event exists & is active
  2432	    const event = await this.getEventById(scoreData.event_id);
  2433	    if (!event) throw new Error('Event not found.');
  2434	    if (event.status !== 'active') {
  2435	      const statusLabel =
  2436	        event.status === 'ended' || event.status === 'archived'
  2437	          ? 'Event has ended.'
  2438	          : event.status === 'paused'
  2439	          ? 'Event is paused.'
  2440	          : `Event is ${event.status}.`;
  2441	      throw new Error(`${statusLabel} Ballot submission rejected.`);
  2442	    }
  2443	
  2444	    // 2. Verify Judge assigned
  2445	    const judge = await this.getJudgeById(scoreData.judge_id);
  2446	    if (!judge) throw new Error('Judge not found.');
  2447	
  2448	    const isAssigned =
  2449	      event.assigned_judges.includes(judge.id) ||
  2450	      (judge.assigned_events || []).includes(event.id) ||
  2451	      event.status === 'active';
  2452	    if (!isAssigned) {
  2453	      throw new Error('Judge is not assigned to evaluate this event.');
  2454	    }
  2455	
  2456	    // 3. Check if score already exists (Strict Immutability with 5-minute server-clock grace period support)
  2457	    const existing = await this.getScoreForJudgeParticipant(
  2458	      scoreData.event_id,
  2459	      scoreData.judge_id,
  2460	      scoreData.participant_id
  2461	    );
  2462	
  2463	    let isWithinGrace = false;
  2464	    if (existing) {
  2465	      const org = await this.getOrganizationByOrgId(event.org_id);
  2466	      const isGraceAllowed = Boolean(org?.grace_period_enabled);
  2467	      const diffMs = getServerNowMs() - new Date(existing.created_at).getTime();
  2468	      isWithinGrace = isGraceAllowed && diffMs <= 5 * 60 * 1000;
  2469	
  2470	      if (!isWithinGrace) {
  2471	        throw new Error(
  2472	          'RLS Permission Denied: Scores are immutable and cannot be updated once inserted.'
  2473	        );
  2474	      }
  2475	    }
  2476	
  2477	    const clientScoreUuid = (existing && isWithinGrace ? existing.id : scoreData.id) || generateUUID();
  2478	    const criteriaMap = scoreData.score_data || scoreData.score_payload?.criteria || {};
  2479	    const timestamp = new Date(getServerNowMs()).toISOString();
  2480	    const payloadJson = {
  2481	      ...(scoreData.score_payload || {
  2482	        criteria: criteriaMap,
  2483	        total_score: scoreData.total_score,
  2484	        submitted_at: timestamp,
  2485	      }),
  2486	      client_uuid: scoreData.score_payload?.client_uuid || clientScoreUuid,
  2487	    };
  2488	
  2489	    // 4. OPTIMISTIC WRITE: Keep snapshot so server rejections roll back cleanly
  2490	    const scoresSnapshot = loadCache<SupabaseScore>(STORAGE_KEYS.SCORES, []);
  2491	    let optimisticScore: SupabaseScore;
  2492	
  2493	    if (existing && isWithinGrace) {
  2494	      optimisticScore = normalizeScore({
  2495	        ...existing,
  2496	        score_data: criteriaMap,
  2497	        total_score: scoreData.total_score,
  2498	        score_payload: payloadJson,
  2499	      });
  2500	      const updated = scoresSnapshot.some((s) => s.id === existing.id)
  2501	        ? scoresSnapshot.map((s) => (s.id === existing.id ? optimisticScore : s))
  2502	        : [...scoresSnapshot, optimisticScore];
  2503	      saveCache(STORAGE_KEYS.SCORES, updated, false);
  2504	    } else {
  2505	      optimisticScore = normalizeScore({
  2506	        id: clientScoreUuid,
  2507	        event_id: scoreData.event_id,
  2508	        judge_id: scoreData.judge_id,
  2509	        participant_id: scoreData.participant_id,
  2510	        score_data: criteriaMap,
  2511	        total_score: scoreData.total_score,
  2512	        score_payload: payloadJson,
  2513	        created_at: timestamp,
  2514	      });
  2515	      saveCache(STORAGE_KEYS.SCORES, [...scoresSnapshot, optimisticScore], false);
  2516	    }
  2517	
  2518	    invalidateCache(STORAGE_KEYS.SCORES);
  2519	
  2520	    // 5. CLOUD SYNC
  2521	    if (isSupabaseConfigured() && isOnlineNavigator()) {
  2522	      try {
  2523	        const scorePayload = {
  2524	          id: existing && isWithinGrace ? existing.id : optimisticScore.id,
  2525	          event_id: scoreData.event_id,
  2526	          judge_id: scoreData.judge_id,
  2527	          participant_id: scoreData.participant_id,
  2528	          total_score: scoreData.total_score,
  2529	          score_payload: payloadJson,
  2530	        };
  2531	
  2532	        const rpcRes = await callSecuredWriteRpc<SupabaseScore[]>('score_upsert_batch', {
  2533	          p_records: [scorePayload],
  2534	        });
  2535	
  2536	        const syncedRow = Array.isArray(rpcRes.data) ? rpcRes.data[0] : null;
  2537	        if (syncedRow) {
  2538	          const synced = normalizeScore(syncedRow);
  2539	          const currentScores = loadCache<SupabaseScore>(STORAGE_KEYS.SCORES, []);
  2540	          const existsInCache = currentScores.some((s) => s.id === synced.id);
  2541	          saveCache(
  2542	            STORAGE_KEYS.SCORES,
  2543	            existsInCache
  2544	              ? currentScores.map((s) => (s.id === synced.id ? synced : s))
  2545	              : [...currentScores, synced],
  2546	            true
  2547	          );
  2548	          notifyCloudSync('Score ballot');
  2549	          return synced;
  2550	        }
  2551	      } catch (err: any) {
  2552	        const orgBlock = parseOrgEnforcementError(err);
  2553	        if (orgBlock) {
  2554	          saveCache(STORAGE_KEYS.SCORES, scoresSnapshot, false);
  2555	          eventBus.emit('org_enforcement', { code: orgBlock });
  2556	          throw err;
  2557	        }
  2558	        if (isServerRejectionError(err)) {
  2559	          saveCache(STORAGE_KEYS.SCORES, scoresSnapshot, false);
  2560	          throw err;
  2561	        }
  2562	        console.warn('Offline mode: score ballot saved locally, deferring cloud sync:', err?.message || err);
  2563	        await queueScoreLocally(optimisticScore);
  2564	        notifyOfflineSaved('Score ballot');
  2565	      }
  2566	    } else if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
  2567	      await queueScoreLocally(optimisticScore);
  2568	      notifyOfflineSaved('Score ballot');
  2569	    } else {
  2570	      notifyOfflineSaved('Score ballot');
  2571	    }
  2572	
  2573	    return optimisticScore;
  2574	  },
  2575	
  2576	  async deleteScore(scoreId: string): Promise<boolean> {
  2577	    const scoresSnapshot = loadCache<SupabaseScore>(STORAGE_KEYS.SCORES, []);
  2578	    saveCache(
  2579	      STORAGE_KEYS.SCORES,
  2580	      scoresSnapshot.filter((s) => s.id !== scoreId),
  2581	      true
  2582	    );
  2583	    invalidateCache(STORAGE_KEYS.SCORES);
  2584	    if (isSupabaseConfigured() && isOnlineNavigator()) {
  2585	      try {
  2586	        await callSecuredWriteRpc('score_delete', { p_score_id: scoreId });
  2587	        notifyCloudSync('Score ballot removed');
  2588	        return true;
  2589	      } catch (err: any) {
  2590	        if (isServerRejectionError(err)) {
  2591	          saveCache(STORAGE_KEYS.SCORES, scoresSnapshot, false);
  2592	          throw err;
  2593	        }
  2594	        console.warn('deleteScore cloud sync note:', err?.message || err);
  2595	      }
  2596	    }
  2597	    return true;
  2598	  },
  2599	
  2600	  // ------------------------------------------
  2601	  // 7. TIE-BREAKER ENGINE LOGIC (Multithreaded Web Worker & Fast Fallback)
  2602	  // ------------------------------------------
  2603	  calculateRankings(
  2604	    participants: SupabaseParticipant[],
  2605	    scores: SupabaseScore[],
  2606	    assignedJudgesCount: number = 0,
  2607	    method: TieBreakerMethod = 'olympic',
  2608	    tieBreakerEnabled: boolean = false
  2609	  ) {
  2610	    return engineCalculateRankings(
  2611	      participants,
  2612	      scores,
  2613	      assignedJudgesCount,
  2614	      method,
  2615	      tieBreakerEnabled
  2616	    );
  2617	  },
  2618	
  2619	  async calculateRankingsAsync(
  2620	    participants: SupabaseParticipant[],
  2621	    scores: SupabaseScore[],
  2622	    assignedJudgesCount: number = 0,
  2623	    method: TieBreakerMethod = 'olympic',
  2624	    tieBreakerEnabled: boolean = false
  2625	  ) {
  2626	    return engineCalculateRankingsAsync(
  2627	      participants,
  2628	      scores,
  2629	      assignedJudgesCount,
  2630	      method,
  2631	      tieBreakerEnabled
  2632	    );
  2633	  },
  2634	};

==================== FILE: src/lib/backup/index.ts ====================
     1	import { Organization } from '../supabaseTypes';
     2	import { supabase, isSupabaseConfigured } from '../supabase';
     3	import { APP_VERSION } from '../../config/version';
     4	import { BackupManifest, BackupPayload, RawBackupFile, RestoreMode, RestoreSummary } from './format';
     5	import { collectOrganizationData } from './collect';
     6	import { encryptPayload, decryptPayload } from './crypto';
     7	import { getIndexedDBCache, setIndexedDBCache } from '../db/indexedDB';
     8	
     9	const SAFETY_SNAPSHOT_KEY = 'fj_backup_safety_snapshot';
    10	
    11	export async function createBackup(
    12	  org: Organization,
    13	  pin?: string
    14	): Promise<{ filename: string; blob: Blob; manifest: BackupManifest }> {
    15	  const data = await collectOrganizationData(org.org_id);
    16	  const isEncrypted = Boolean(pin && pin.trim().length > 0);
    17	
    18	  let payloadOut: BackupPayload | string = data;
    19	  let salt: string | undefined;
    20	  let iv: string | undefined;
    21	
    22	  if (isEncrypted && pin) {
    23	    const rawJson = JSON.stringify(data);
    24	    const enc = await encryptPayload(rawJson, pin.trim());
    25	    payloadOut = enc.ciphertext;
    26	    salt = enc.salt;
    27	    iv = enc.iv;
    28	  }
    29	
    30	  const manifest: BackupManifest = {
    31	    app: 'Fair Judge',
    32	    format_version: '1.0.0',
    33	    app_version: APP_VERSION,
    34	    org_id: org.org_id,
    35	    org_name: org.name,
    36	    exported_at: new Date().toISOString(),
    37	    counts: {
    38	      events: data.events.length,
    39	      judges: data.judges.length,
    40	      participants: data.participants.length,
    41	      scores: data.scores.length,
    42	    },
    43	    encrypted: isEncrypted,
    44	    salt,
    45	    iv,
    46	  };
    47	
    48	  const fileData: RawBackupFile = {
    49	    manifest,
    50	    payload: payloadOut,
    51	  };
    52	
    53	  const jsonStr = JSON.stringify(fileData, null, 2);
    54	  const blob = new Blob([jsonStr], { type: 'application/json' });
    55	  const dateStr = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
    56	  const cleanOrgCode = org.org_id.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, '-');
    57	  const filename = `fairjudge-backup-${cleanOrgCode}-${dateStr}.json`;
    58	
    59	  return {
    60	    filename,
    61	    blob,
    62	    manifest,
    63	  };
    64	}
    65	
    66	export async function parseBackupFile(
    67	  fileText: string,
    68	  pin?: string
    69	): Promise<{ manifest: BackupManifest; payload: BackupPayload }> {
    70	  let parsed: RawBackupFile;
    71	  try {
    72	    parsed = JSON.parse(fileText);
    73	  } catch {
    74	    throw new Error('Invalid JSON backup file format.');
    75	  }
    76	
    77	  if (parsed.manifest?.app !== 'Fair Judge') {
    78	    throw new Error('Unrecognized backup format. File must be created by Fair Judge.');
    79	  }
    80	
    81	  const { manifest, payload } = parsed;
    82	
    83	  if (manifest.encrypted) {
    84	    if (!pin) {
    85	      throw new Error('ENCRYPTED_BACKUP: This backup is encrypted. Please enter your 4-digit PIN.');
    86	    }
    87	    if (!manifest.salt || !manifest.iv || typeof payload !== 'string') {
    88	      throw new Error('Corrupted encrypted backup file structure.');
    89	    }
    90	    const decryptedJson = await decryptPayload(payload, pin.trim(), manifest.salt, manifest.iv);
    91	    const decryptedPayload = JSON.parse(decryptedJson) as BackupPayload;
    92	    return { manifest, payload: decryptedPayload };
    93	  }
    94	
    95	  return {
    96	    manifest,
    97	    payload: payload as BackupPayload,
    98	  };
    99	}
   100	
   101	export async function previewRestore(
   102	  orgId: string,
   103	  password: string,
   104	  payload: BackupPayload,
   105	  mode: RestoreMode
   106	): Promise<RestoreSummary> {
   107	  if (!isSupabaseConfigured() || (typeof navigator !== 'undefined' && !navigator.onLine)) {
   108	    throw new Error('Restore simulation requires an internet connection.');
   109	  }
   110	
   111	  const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc('org_restore', {
   112	    p_org_id: orgId,
   113	    p_password: password,
   114	    p_mode: mode,
   115	    p_payload: payload,
   116	    p_dry_run: true,
   117	  });
   118	
   119	  if (error) {
   120	    throw new Error(`Dry run failed: ${error.message}`);
   121	  }
   122	
   123	  const res = data as any;
   124	  if (!res || !res.ok) {
   125	    const reason = res?.message || res?.error || res?.reason || 'Unknown error';
   126	    if (res?.error === 'invalid_credentials' || res?.reason === 'invalid_credentials') {
   127	      throw new Error('Invalid master password. Please verify credentials.');
   128	    }
   129	    if (res?.error === 'replace_blocked_locked_events' || res?.reason === 'replace_blocked_locked_events') {
   130	      throw new Error(res.message || 'Cannot replace competition: locked events present. Use Merge mode instead.');
   131	    }
   132	    throw new Error(`Dry run rejected: ${reason}`);
   133	  }
   134	
   135	  return res.summary as RestoreSummary;
   136	}
   137	
   138	export async function applyRestore(
   139	  orgId: string,
   140	  password: string,
   141	  payload: BackupPayload,
   142	  mode: RestoreMode
   143	): Promise<RestoreSummary> {
   144	  if (!isSupabaseConfigured() || (typeof navigator !== 'undefined' && !navigator.onLine)) {
   145	    throw new Error('Restoration requires an internet connection.');
   146	  }
   147	
   148	  const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc('org_restore', {
   149	    p_org_id: orgId,
   150	    p_password: password,
   151	    p_mode: mode,
   152	    p_payload: payload,
   153	    p_dry_run: false,
   154	  });
   155	
   156	  if (error) {
   157	    throw new Error(`Restore failed: ${error.message}`);
   158	  }
   159	
   160	  const res = data as any;
   161	  if (!res || !res.ok) {
   162	    const reason = res?.message || res?.error || res?.reason || 'Unknown error';
   163	    if (res?.error === 'invalid_credentials' || res?.reason === 'invalid_credentials') {
   164	      throw new Error('Invalid master password. Please verify credentials.');
   165	    }
   166	    if (res?.error === 'replace_blocked_locked_events' || res?.reason === 'replace_blocked_locked_events') {
   167	      throw new Error(res.message || 'Cannot replace competition: locked events present. Use Merge mode instead.');
   168	    }
   169	    throw new Error(`Restore failed: ${reason}`);
   170	  }
   171	
   172	  return res.summary as RestoreSummary;
   173	}
   174	
   175	export async function createSafetySnapshot(org: Organization): Promise<void> {
   176	  try {
   177	    const current = await collectOrganizationData(org.org_id);
   178	    await setIndexedDBCache(
   179	      `${SAFETY_SNAPSHOT_KEY}_${org.org_id}`,
   180	      {
   181	        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
   182	        data: current,
   183	      },
   184	      true
   185	    );
   186	  } catch (err) {
   187	    console.warn('Safety snapshot creation notice:', err);
   188	  }
   189	}
   190	
   191	export async function hasSafetySnapshot(orgId: string): Promise<boolean> {
   192	  try {
   193	    const snap = await getIndexedDBCache<any>(`${SAFETY_SNAPSHOT_KEY}_${orgId}`, null);
   194	    return Boolean(snap && snap.data);
   195	  } catch {
   196	    return false;
   197	  }
   198	}
   199	
   200	export async function restoreSafetySnapshot(orgId: string, password: string): Promise<RestoreSummary> {
   201	  const snap = await getIndexedDBCache<any>(`${SAFETY_SNAPSHOT_KEY}_${orgId}`, null);
   202	  if (!snap || !snap.data) {
   203	    throw new Error('No safety snapshot found to undo.');
   204	  }
   205	  return await applyRestore(orgId, password, snap.data, 'replace');
   206	}

   210	export * from './crypto';

==================== FILE: src/lib/backup/collect.ts ====================
     1	import { isSupabaseConfigured, dbService } from '../supabase';
     2	import { BackupPayload } from './format';
     3	import { SupabaseEvent, SupabaseJudge, SupabaseParticipant, SupabaseScore } from '../supabaseTypes';
     4	
     5	export async function collectOrganizationData(orgId: string): Promise<BackupPayload> {
     6	  const cleanOrgId = orgId.trim().toUpperCase();
     7	
     8	  let events: SupabaseEvent[] = [];
     9	  let judges: SupabaseJudge[] = [];
    10	  let participants: SupabaseParticipant[] = [];
    11	  let scores: SupabaseScore[] = [];
    12	
    13	  // Try live token-checked RPCs first if online
    14	  if (isSupabaseConfigured() && typeof navigator !== 'undefined' && navigator.onLine) {
    15	    try {
    16	      const [eventsRes, rosterRes, scoresRes] = await Promise.all([
    17	        dbService.getEventsResult(cleanOrgId, true),
    18	        dbService.getOrgRosterCredentialsResult(cleanOrgId, true),
    19	        dbService.getOrgScoresResult(cleanOrgId, true),
    20	      ]);
    21	
    22	      if (eventsRes.ok || eventsRes.data.length > 0) events = eventsRes.data;
    23	      if (rosterRes.ok || rosterRes.data.judges.length > 0) judges = rosterRes.data.judges;
    24	      if (rosterRes.ok || rosterRes.data.participants.length > 0) {
    25	        participants = rosterRes.data.participants;
    26	      }
    27	      if (scoresRes.ok || scoresRes.data.length > 0) scores = scoresRes.data;
    28	    } catch (err) {
    29	      console.warn('Cloud collection notice, falling back to local mirror:', err);
    30	    }
    31	  }
    32	
    33	  // Fallback to local org-scoped cache if cloud returned 0 rows or was offline
    34	  if (events.length === 0) {
    35	    events = dbService.getCachedEvents(cleanOrgId);
    36	  }
    37	  if (judges.length === 0) {
    38	    judges = dbService.getCachedJudges(cleanOrgId);
    39	  }
    40	  if (participants.length === 0) {
    41	    participants = dbService.getCachedParticipants(cleanOrgId);
    42	  }
    43	  if (scores.length === 0) {
    44	    const eventIds = new Set(events.map((e) => e.id));
    45	    const orgCachedScores = dbService.getCachedScores(cleanOrgId);
    46	    scores = eventIds.size > 0
    47	      ? orgCachedScores.filter((s) => eventIds.has(s.event_id))
    48	      : orgCachedScores;
    49	  }
    50	
    51	  // Defense in depth: Sanitize any sensitive tokens from export
    52	  const cleanJudges = judges.map((j) => {
    53	    const { current_session_token: _t, ...rest } = j as any;
    54	    return rest as SupabaseJudge;
    55	  });
    56	
    57	  return {
    58	    events,
    59	    judges: cleanJudges,
    60	    participants,
    61	    scores,
    62	  };
    63	}

==================== FILE: src/lib/backup/crypto.ts ====================
     1	/**
     2	 * Web Crypto API PBKDF2 + AES-GCM 256-bit Encryption
     3	 * Zero external npm dependencies.
     4	 */
     5	
     6	function bufferToHex(buffer: ArrayBuffer): string {
     7	  return Array.from(new Uint8Array(buffer))
     8	    .map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0'))
     9	    .join('');
    10	}
    11	
    12	function hexToBuffer(hex: string): ArrayBuffer {
    13	  const bytes = new Uint8Array(hex.length / 2);
    14	  for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i++) {
    15	    bytes[i] = parseInt(hex.substr(i * 2, 2), 16);
    16	  }
    17	  return bytes.buffer;
    18	}
    19	
    20	function bufferToBase64(buffer: ArrayBuffer): string {
    21	  const bytes = new Uint8Array(buffer);
    22	  let binary = '';
    23	  for (let i = 0; i < bytes.byteLength; i++) {
    24	    binary += String.fromCharCode(bytes[i]);
    25	  }
    26	  return btoa(binary);
    27	}
    28	
    29	function base64ToBuffer(base64: string): ArrayBuffer {
    30	  const binary = atob(base64);
    31	  const bytes = new Uint8Array(binary.length);
    32	  for (let i = 0; i < binary.length; i++) {
    33	    bytes[i] = binary.charCodeAt(i);
    34	  }
    35	  return bytes.buffer;
    36	}
    37	
    38	async function deriveKey(pinOrPass: string, saltBuffer: ArrayBuffer): Promise<CryptoKey> {
    39	  const enc = new TextEncoder();
    40	  const baseKey = await crypto.subtle.importKey(
    41	    'raw',
    42	    enc.encode(pinOrPass),
    43	    { name: 'PBKDF2' },
    44	    false,
    45	    ['deriveKey']
    46	  );
    47	
    48	  return crypto.subtle.deriveKey(
    49	    {
    50	      name: 'PBKDF2',
    51	      salt: saltBuffer,
    52	      iterations: 100000,
    53	      hash: 'SHA-256',
    54	    },
    55	    baseKey,
    56	    { name: 'AES-GCM', length: 256 },
    57	    false,
    58	    ['encrypt', 'decrypt']
    59	  );
    60	}
    61	
    62	export async function encryptPayload(
    63	  payloadText: string,
    64	  pinOrPass: string
    65	): Promise<{ ciphertext: string; salt: string; iv: string }> {
    66	  const salt = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(16));
    67	  const iv = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(12));
    68	  const key = await deriveKey(pinOrPass, salt.buffer);
    69	
    70	  const enc = new TextEncoder();
    71	  const cipherBuffer = await crypto.subtle.encrypt(
    72	    { name: 'AES-GCM', iv },
    73	    key,
    74	    enc.encode(payloadText)
    75	  );
    76	
    77	  return {
    78	    ciphertext: bufferToBase64(cipherBuffer),
    79	    salt: bufferToHex(salt.buffer),
    80	    iv: bufferToHex(iv.buffer),
    81	  };
    82	}
    83	
    84	export async function decryptPayload(
    85	  ciphertextBase64: string,
    86	  pinOrPass: string,
    87	  saltHex: string,
    88	  ivHex: string
    89	): Promise<string> {
    90	  const salt = hexToBuffer(saltHex);
    91	  const iv = hexToBuffer(ivHex);
    92	  const key = await deriveKey(pinOrPass, salt);
    93	  const cipherBuffer = base64ToBuffer(ciphertextBase64);
    94	
    95	  try {
    96	    const plainBuffer = await crypto.subtle.decrypt(
    97	      { name: 'AES-GCM', iv: new Uint8Array(iv) },
    98	      key,
    99	      cipherBuffer
   100	    );
   101	    const dec = new TextDecoder();
   102	    return dec.decode(plainBuffer);
   103	  } catch {
   104	    throw new Error('Incorrect decryption PIN or corrupted backup file.');
   105	  }
   106	}

==================== FILE: src/components/organiser/backup/BackupSheet.tsx ====================
     1	import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
     2	import { X, Download, ShieldCheck, Lock, Database, Check } from 'lucide-react';
     3	import { Organization } from '../../../lib/supabaseTypes';
     4	import { createBackup } from '../../../lib/backup';
     5	import { useToast } from '../../common/ToastProvider';
     6	
     7	interface BackupSheetProps {
     8	  isOpen: boolean;
     9	  onClose: () => void;
    10	  organization: Organization;
    11	}
    12	
    13	export function BackupSheet({ isOpen, onClose, organization }: BackupSheetProps) {
    14	  const { showToast } = useToast();
    15	  const [pin, setPin] = useState('');
    16	  const [isEncrypted, setIsEncrypted] = useState(false);
    17	  const [isExporting, setIsExporting] = useState(false);
    18	  const [downloadSuccess, setDownloadSuccess] = useState(false);
    19	
    20	  // Modal Scroll Lock and Escape handling
    21	  useEffect(() => {
    22	    if (!isOpen) return;
    23	
    24	    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
    25	      if (e.key === 'Escape') onClose();
    26	    };
    27	    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    28	
    29	    const originalOverflow = document.body.style.overflow;
    30	    document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
    31	
    32	    return () => {
    33	      window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    34	      document.body.style.overflow = originalOverflow;
    35	    };
    36	  }, [isOpen, onClose]);
    37	
    38	  if (!isOpen) return null;
    39	
    40	  const handleDownload = async () => {
    41	    if (isEncrypted && pin.trim().length < 4) {
    42	      showToast('PIN must be at least 4 digits.', 'error');
    43	      return;
    44	    }
    45	
    46	    setIsExporting(true);
    47	    try {
    48	      const { filename, blob } = await createBackup(organization, isEncrypted ? pin.trim() : undefined);
    49	      
    50	      const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    51	      const a = document.createElement('a');
    52	      a.href = url;
    53	      a.download = filename;
    54	      document.body.appendChild(a);
    55	      a.click();
    56	      document.body.removeChild(a);
    57	      URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    58	
    59	      setDownloadSuccess(true);
    60	      showToast('Backup downloaded successfully.', 'success');
    61	      setTimeout(() => {
    62	        setDownloadSuccess(false);
    63	        onClose();
    64	      }, 1500);
    65	    } catch (err: any) {
    66	      showToast(`Export failed: ${err.message || 'Unknown error'}`, 'error');
    67	    } finally {
    68	      setIsExporting(false);
    69	    }
    70	  };
    71	
    72	  return (
    73	    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[110] flex items-center justify-center p-3 sm:p-4 bg-black/65 animate-in fade-in duration-150">
    74	      <div 
    75	        className="relative w-full max-w-md max-h-[calc(100dvh-2rem)] flex flex-col bg-white dark:bg-zinc-950 border border-stone-200 dark:border-zinc-800 rounded-3xl shadow-2xl overflow-hidden animate-in zoom-in-95 duration-150"
    76	        role="dialog"
    77	        aria-modal="true"
    78	        aria-labelledby="backup-title"
    79	      >
    80	        {/* Pinned Header */}
    81	        <div className="shrink-0 px-5 py-4 border-b border-stone-100 dark:border-zinc-800/80 flex items-center justify-between">
    82	          <div className="flex items-center space-x-2.5">
    83	            <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-xl bg-emerald-500/10 border border-emerald-500/20 flex items-center justify-center text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400">
    84	              <Database className="w-4 h-4" />
    85	            </div>
    86	            <div>
    87	              <h3 id="backup-title" className="text-sm font-bold text-zinc-900 dark:text-white">
    88	                Create Backup
    89	              </h3>
    90	              <p className="text-[11px] text-zinc-500">
    91	                Export events, roster, judges and scores
    92	              </p>
    93	            </div>
    94	          </div>
    95	          <button
    96	            type="button"
    97	            onClick={onClose}
    98	            aria-label="Close backup modal"
    99	            className="w-11 h-11 rounded-xl text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-600 dark:hover:text-zinc-200 hover:bg-stone-100 dark:hover:bg-zinc-900 flex items-center justify-center transition-colors cursor-pointer"
   100	          >
   101	            <X className="w-4 h-4" />
   102	          </button>
   103	        </div>
   104	
   105	        {/* Scrollable Content */}
   106	        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto overscroll-contain p-5 space-y-4">
   107	          <div className="p-3.5 rounded-2xl bg-stone-50 dark:bg-zinc-900/60 border border-stone-200 dark:border-zinc-800 text-xs text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-300 leading-relaxed">
   108	            <p className="font-semibold text-zinc-900 dark:text-white mb-1">
   109	              Zero-Trust Offline Storage
   110	            </p>
   111	            <p>
   112	              Your backup contains full competition archives. It does not contain passwords or auth keys and can be restored anytime on any device.
   113	            </p>
   114	          </div>
   115	
   116	          {/* Encryption Option */}
   117	          <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl border border-stone-200 dark:border-zinc-800 space-y-3">
   118	            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
   119	              <div className="space-y-0.5">
   120	                <span className="text-xs font-bold text-zinc-900 dark:text-white block">
   121	                  PIN Encryption
   122	                </span>
   123	                <span className="text-[11px] text-zinc-500 block">
   124	                  Protect backup with AES-256 GCM
   125	                </span>
   126	              </div>
   127	              <input
   128	                type="checkbox"
   129	                checked={isEncrypted}
   130	                onChange={(e) => setIsEncrypted(e.target.checked)}
   131	                className="w-4 h-4 rounded text-emerald-600 focus:ring-emerald-500 cursor-pointer"
   132	                id="encrypt-toggle"
   133	              />
   134	            </div>
   135	
   136	            {isEncrypted && (
   137	              <div className="pt-2 animate-in fade-in duration-150 space-y-2">
   138	                <label className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-zinc-500 block">
   139	                  Set 4-Digit Security PIN
   140	                </label>
   141	                <div className="relative">
   142	                  <Lock className="w-3.5 h-3.5 absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-zinc-400" />
   143	                  <input
   144	                    type="password"
   145	                    inputMode="numeric"
   146	                    maxLength={12}
   147	                    placeholder="Enter 4 to 8 digits"
   148	                    value={pin}
   149	                    onChange={(e) => setPin(e.target.value)}
   150	                    className="w-full pl-9 pr-3.5 py-2.5 bg-stone-50 dark:bg-zinc-900 border border-stone-200 dark:border-zinc-800 rounded-xl text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-emerald-500"
   151	                  />
   152	                </div>
   153	              </div>
   154	            )}
   155	          </div>
   156	        </div>
   157	
   158	        {/* Pinned Footer */}
   159	        <div className="shrink-0 p-4 border-t border-stone-100 dark:border-zinc-800/80 bg-stone-50/50 dark:bg-zinc-900/30 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
   160	          <button
   161	            type="button"
   162	            onClick={onClose}
   163	            disabled={isExporting}
   164	            className="min-h-[44px] px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-stone-200 dark:border-zinc-800 text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 hover:bg-stone-100 dark:hover:bg-zinc-900 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
   165	          >
   166	            Cancel
   167	          </button>
   168	
   169	          <button
   170	            type="button"
   171	            onClick={handleDownload}
   172	            disabled={isExporting || (isEncrypted && pin.trim().length < 4)}
   173	            className="min-h-[44px] px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 disabled:opacity-50 text-white text-xs font-bold transition-colors shadow-sm flex items-center space-x-2 cursor-pointer active:scale-95 motion-reduce:transition-none"
   174	          >
   175	            {downloadSuccess ? (
   176	              <>
   177	                <Check className="w-4 h-4 text-white" />
   178	                <span>Saved</span>
   179	              </>
   180	            ) : isExporting ? (
   181	              <span>Preparing...</span>
   182	            ) : (
   183	              <>
   184	                <Download className="w-4 h-4" />
   185	                <span>Download Backup</span>
   186	              </>
   187	            )}
   188	          </button>
   189	        </div>
   190	      </div>
   191	    </div>
   192	  );
   193	}

==================== FILE: src/components/organiser/backup/RestoreSheet.tsx ====================
     1	import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
     2	import { 
     3	  X, 
     4	  Upload, 
     5	  Check, 
     6	  RefreshCw, 
     7	  AlertTriangle, 
     8	  Undo2, 
     9	  Lock, 
    10	  KeyRound, 
    11	  Eye, 
    12	  EyeOff, 
    13	  CheckCircle2, 
    14	  ShieldCheck 
    15	} from 'lucide-react';
    16	import { Organization } from '../../../lib/supabaseTypes';
    17	import { 
    18	  parseBackupFile, 
    19	  previewRestore, 
    20	  applyRestore, 
    21	  createSafetySnapshot,
    22	  hasSafetySnapshot,
    23	  restoreSafetySnapshot,
    24	  BackupManifest, 
    25	  BackupPayload, 
    26	  RestoreMode, 
    27	  RestoreSummary 
    28	} from '../../../lib/backup';
    29	import { useToast } from '../../common/ToastProvider';
    30	
    31	interface RestoreSheetProps {
    32	  isOpen: boolean;
    33	  onClose: () => void;
    34	  organization: Organization;
    35	  onRestoreComplete: () => void;
    36	}
    37	
    38	export function RestoreSheet({ isOpen, onClose, organization, onRestoreComplete }: RestoreSheetProps) {
    39	  const { showToast } = useToast();
    40	  const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
    41	
    42	  const [step, setStep] = useState<'upload' | 'pin' | 'auth' | 'preview' | 'done'>('upload');
    43	  const [rawText, setRawText] = useState('');
    44	  const [pin, setPin] = useState('');
    45	  const [pinError, setPinError] = useState('');
    46	  
    47	  // Master Password state — kept purely in memory, cleared on close
    48	  const [masterPassword, setMasterPassword] = useState('');
    49	  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
    50	  const [passwordError, setPasswordError] = useState('');
    51	  const [lockedEventsWarning, setLockedEventsWarning] = useState<string | null>(null);
    52	
    53	  const [manifest, setManifest] = useState<BackupManifest | null>(null);
    54	  const [payload, setPayload] = useState<BackupPayload | null>(null);
    55	  const [mode, setMode] = useState<RestoreMode>('merge');
    56	  const [summary, setSummary] = useState<RestoreSummary | null>(null);
    57	  const [isSimulating, setIsSimulating] = useState(false);
    58	  const [isApplying, setIsApplying] = useState(false);
    59	  
    60	  // Undo safety snapshot state
    61	  const [canUndo, setCanUndo] = useState(false);
    62	  const [isUndoing, setIsUndoing] = useState(false);
    63	  const [showUndoPrompt, setShowUndoPrompt] = useState(false);
    64	  const [undoPassword, setUndoPassword] = useState('');
    65	  const [undoError, setUndoError] = useState('');
    66	
    67	  // Clear memory and sensitive state whenever modal is opened or closed
    68	  const resetAllState = () => {
    69	    setMasterPassword('');
    70	    setShowPassword(false);
    71	    setPasswordError('');
    72	    setLockedEventsWarning(null);
    73	    setPin('');
    74	    setPinError('');
    75	    setRawText('');
    76	    setManifest(null);
    77	    setPayload(null);
    78	    setSummary(null);
    79	    setStep('upload');
    80	    setShowUndoPrompt(false);
    81	    setUndoPassword('');
    82	    setUndoError('');
    83	  };
    84	
    85	  const handleClose = () => {
    86	    resetAllState();
    87	    onClose();
    88	  };
    89	
    90	  // Check safety snapshot availability on open, wipe on close
    91	  useEffect(() => {
    92	    if (isOpen) {
    93	      hasSafetySnapshot(organization.org_id).then(setCanUndo).catch(() => setCanUndo(false));
    94	    } else {
    95	      resetAllState();
    96	    }
    97	  }, [isOpen, organization.org_id]);
    98	
    99	  // Modal Scroll Lock and Escape handling
   100	  useEffect(() => {
   101	    if (!isOpen) return;
   102	
   103	    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
   104	      if (e.key === 'Escape') handleClose();
   105	    };
   106	    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
   107	
   108	    const originalOverflow = document.body.style.overflow;
   109	    document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
   110	
   111	    return () => {
   112	      window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
   113	      document.body.style.overflow = originalOverflow;
   114	    };
   115	  }, [isOpen]);
   116	
   117	  if (!isOpen) return null;
   118	
   119	  const handleFileSelect = async (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
   120	    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
   121	    if (!file) return;
   122	
   123	    try {
   124	      const text = await file.text();
   125	      setRawText(text);
   126	
   127	      // Try parsing unencrypted
   128	      try {
   129	        const parsed = await parseBackupFile(text);
   130	        setManifest(parsed.manifest);
   131	        setPayload(parsed.payload);
   132	        setStep('auth');
   133	      } catch (parseErr: any) {
   134	        if (parseErr.message?.includes('ENCRYPTED_BACKUP')) {
   135	          setStep('pin');
   136	        } else {
   137	          showToast(parseErr.message || 'Invalid backup file', 'error');
   138	        }
   139	      }
   140	    } catch {
   141	      showToast('Could not read selected file.', 'error');
   142	    }
   143	  };
   144	
   145	  const handleDecryptWithPin = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
   146	    e.preventDefault();
   147	    setPinError('');
   148	    if (!pin.trim()) {
   149	      setPinError('Please enter your backup PIN.');
   150	      return;
   151	    }
   152	
   153	    try {
   154	      const parsed = await parseBackupFile(rawText, pin.trim());
   155	      setManifest(parsed.manifest);
   156	      setPayload(parsed.payload);
   157	      setStep('auth');
   158	    } catch (err: any) {
   159	      setPinError(err.message || 'Incorrect PIN.');
   160	    }
   161	  };
   162	
   163	  const runSimulation = async (targetPayload: BackupPayload, selectedMode: RestoreMode, pwd?: string) => {
   164	    const effectivePwd = (pwd ?? masterPassword).trim();
   165	    if (!effectivePwd) {
   166	      setPasswordError('Master password is required.');
   167	      return;
   168	    }
   169	
   170	    setIsSimulating(true);
   171	    setPasswordError('');
   172	    setLockedEventsWarning(null);
   173	
   174	    try {
   175	      const preview = await previewRestore(organization.org_id, effectivePwd, targetPayload, selectedMode);
   176	      setSummary(preview);
   177	      setStep('preview');
   178	    } catch (err: any) {
   179	      const msg: string = err.message || '';
   180	      if (msg.includes('Invalid master password') || msg.includes('invalid_credentials')) {
   181	        setPasswordError('Invalid master password. Please verify credentials.');
   182	        showToast('Authentication failed: Invalid master password.', 'error');
   183	      } else if (msg.includes('replace_blocked_locked_events') || msg.includes('locked event(s)')) {
   184	        setLockedEventsWarning(msg);
   185	        showToast(msg, 'error');
   186	      } else {
   187	        showToast(`Simulation notice: ${msg}`, 'error');
   188	      }
   189	    } finally {
   190	      setIsSimulating(false);
   191	    }
   192	  };
   193	
   194	  const handleAuthSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
   195	    e.preventDefault();
   196	    if (!payload) return;
   197	    if (!masterPassword.trim()) {
   198	      setPasswordError('Please enter your organiser master password.');
   199	      return;
   200	    }
   201	    runSimulation(payload, mode, masterPassword);
   202	  };
   203	
   204	  const handleModeChange = (newMode: RestoreMode) => {
   205	    setMode(newMode);
   206	    setLockedEventsWarning(null);
   207	    if (payload && masterPassword.trim()) {
   208	      runSimulation(payload, newMode, masterPassword);
   209	    }
   210	  };
   211	
   212	  const handleExecuteRestore = async () => {
   213	    if (!payload || !masterPassword.trim()) {
   214	      showToast('Master password missing. Please re-authenticate.', 'error');
   215	      setStep('auth');
   216	      return;
   217	    }
   218	    setIsApplying(true);
   219	    try {
   220	      // 1. Create immediate local safety snapshot before applying restore
   221	      await createSafetySnapshot(organization);
   222	
   223	      // 2. Apply restore atomically via database RPC with master password
   224	      const result = await applyRestore(organization.org_id, masterPassword.trim(), payload, mode);
   225	      setSummary(result);
   226	      setStep('done');
   227	      showToast('Backup restored successfully!', 'success');
   228	      onRestoreComplete();
   229	    } catch (err: any) {
   230	      const msg: string = err.message || '';
   231	      if (msg.includes('Invalid master password') || msg.includes('invalid_credentials')) {
   232	        setPasswordError('Invalid master password.');
   233	        setStep('auth');
   234	      } else if (msg.includes('replace_blocked_locked_events') || msg.includes('locked event(s)')) {
   235	        setLockedEventsWarning(msg);
   236	      }
   237	      showToast(`Restore failed: ${msg}`, 'error');
   238	    } finally {
   239	      setIsApplying(false);
   240	    }
   241	  };
   242	
   243	  const handleExecuteUndo = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
   244	    e.preventDefault();
   245	    const cleanPwd = undoPassword.trim();
   246	    if (!cleanPwd) {
   247	      setUndoError('Master password is required.');
   248	      return;
   249	    }
   250	    setIsUndoing(true);
   251	    setUndoError('');
   252	    try {
   253	      await restoreSafetySnapshot(organization.org_id, cleanPwd);
   254	      showToast('Restoration undone. Snapshot restored.', 'success');
   255	      onRestoreComplete();
   256	      handleClose();
   257	    } catch (err: any) {
   258	      const msg: string = err.message || '';
   259	      if (msg.includes('Invalid master password') || msg.includes('invalid_credentials')) {
   260	        setUndoError('Invalid master password. Revert refused.');
   261	      } else {
   262	        setUndoError(`Undo failed: ${msg}`);
   263	      }
   264	    } finally {
   265	      setIsUndoing(false);
   266	    }
   267	  };
   268	
   269	  return (
   270	    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[110] flex items-center justify-center p-3 sm:p-4 bg-black/65 animate-in fade-in duration-150">
   271	      <div 
   272	        className="relative w-full max-w-lg max-h-[calc(100dvh-2rem)] flex flex-col bg-white dark:bg-zinc-950 border border-stone-200 dark:border-zinc-800 rounded-3xl shadow-2xl overflow-hidden animate-in zoom-in-95 duration-150"
   273	        role="dialog"
   274	        aria-modal="true"
   275	        aria-labelledby="restore-title"
   276	      >
   277	        {/* Pinned Header */}
   278	        <div className="shrink-0 px-5 py-4 border-b border-stone-100 dark:border-zinc-800/80 flex items-center justify-between">
   279	          <div className="flex items-center space-x-2.5">
   280	            <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-xl bg-blue-500/10 border border-blue-500/20 flex items-center justify-center text-blue-600 dark:text-blue-400">
   281	              <Upload className="w-4 h-4" />
   282	            </div>
   283	            <div>
   284	              <h3 id="restore-title" className="text-sm font-bold text-zinc-900 dark:text-white">
   285	                Restore Competition
   286	              </h3>
   287	              <p className="text-[11px] text-zinc-500">
   288	                Verified database restore with locked event protection
   289	              </p>
   290	            </div>
   291	          </div>
   292	          <button
   293	            type="button"
   294	            onClick={handleClose}
   295	            aria-label="Close restore modal"
   296	            className="w-11 h-11 rounded-xl text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-600 dark:hover:text-zinc-200 hover:bg-stone-100 dark:hover:bg-zinc-900 flex items-center justify-center transition-colors cursor-pointer"
   297	          >
   298	            <X className="w-4 h-4" />
   299	          </button>
   300	        </div>
   301	
   302	        {/* Scrollable Content */}
   303	        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto overscroll-contain p-5 space-y-4">
   304	          {/* Step 1: Upload File */}
   305	          {step === 'upload' && !showUndoPrompt && (
   306	            <div className="space-y-4">
   307	              <input
   308	                type="file"
   309	                ref={fileInputRef}
   310	                accept=".json,application/json"
   311	                onChange={handleFileSelect}
   312	                className="hidden"
   313	              />
   314	
   315	              <button
   316	                type="button"
   317	                onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}
   318	                className="w-full p-8 rounded-2xl border-2 border-dashed border-stone-300 dark:border-zinc-700 hover:border-emerald-500 dark:hover:border-emerald-500 flex flex-col items-center justify-center space-y-2 text-center transition-colors cursor-pointer group"
   319	              >
   320	                <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl bg-stone-100 dark:bg-zinc-900 group-hover:bg-emerald-500/10 flex items-center justify-center text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400 group-hover:text-emerald-500 transition-colors">
   321	                  <Upload className="w-6 h-6" />
   322	                </div>
   323	                <div>
   324	                  <span className="text-xs font-bold text-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-200 block">
   325	                    Choose Backup File (.json)
   326	                  </span>
   327	                  <span className="text-[11px] text-zinc-400 block mt-0.5">
   328	                    Click to browse or drop file here
   329	                  </span>
   330	                </div>
   331	              </button>
   332	
   333	              {canUndo && (
   334	                <div className="p-3.5 rounded-2xl bg-stone-50 dark:bg-zinc-900/40 border border-stone-200 dark:border-zinc-800 flex items-center justify-between">
   335	                  <div className="space-y-0.5">
   336	                    <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-200 block">
   337	                      Safety Snapshot Available
   338	                    </span>
   339	                    <span className="text-[11px] text-zinc-500 block">
   340	                      Revert changes from your last restoration
   341	                    </span>
   342	                  </div>
   343	                  <button
   344	                    type="button"
   345	                    onClick={() => setShowUndoPrompt(true)}
   346	                    className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl border border-stone-200 dark:border-zinc-800 text-xs font-bold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 hover:bg-stone-100 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 transition-colors flex items-center space-x-1.5 cursor-pointer"
   347	                  >
   348	                    <Undo2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
   349	                    <span>Undo Restore</span>
   350	                  </button>
   351	                </div>
   352	              )}
   353	            </div>
   354	          )}
   355	
   356	          {/* Undo Master Password Prompt */}
   357	          {showUndoPrompt && (
   358	            <form onSubmit={handleExecuteUndo} className="space-y-4 animate-in fade-in duration-150">
   359	              <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-amber-500/10 border border-amber-500/20 text-xs text-amber-800 dark:text-amber-300 flex items-start space-x-2.5">
   360	                <AlertTriangle className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-500 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
   361	                <span>
   362	                  Reverting to the safety snapshot requires master password authentication to protect competition integrity.
   363	                </span>
   364	              </div>
   365	
   366	              <div className="space-y-1.5">
   367	                <label className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-500 block">
   368	                  Master Password
   369	                </label>
   370	                <input
   371	                  type="password"
   372	                  autoFocus
   373	                  placeholder="Enter organiser master password"
   374	                  value={undoPassword}
   375	                  onChange={(e) => setUndoPassword(e.target.value)}
   376	                  className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 bg-stone-50 dark:bg-zinc-900 border border-stone-200 dark:border-zinc-800 rounded-xl text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-emerald-500"
   377	                />
   378	                {undoError && <p className="text-[11px] text-red-500 font-medium">{undoError}</p>}
   379	              </div>
   380	
   381	              <div className="flex items-center justify-end space-x-2 pt-2">
   382	                <button
   383	                  type="button"
   384	                  onClick={() => {
   385	                    setShowUndoPrompt(false);
   386	                    setUndoPassword('');
   387	                    setUndoError('');
   388	                  }}
   389	                  className="px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl border border-stone-200 dark:border-zinc-800 text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 hover:bg-stone-100 dark:hover:bg-zinc-900 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
   390	                >
   391	                  Cancel
   392	                </button>
   393	                <button
   394	                  type="submit"
   395	                  disabled={isUndoing}
   396	                  className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-amber-600 hover:bg-amber-500 text-white text-xs font-bold transition-colors shadow-sm cursor-pointer flex items-center space-x-1.5"
   397	                >
   398	                  <RefreshCw className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 ${isUndoing ? 'animate-spin' : ''}`} />
   399	                  <span>{isUndoing ? 'Reverting...' : 'Confirm Revert'}</span>
   400	                </button>
   401	              </div>
   402	            </form>
   403	          )}
   404	
   405	          {/* Step 2: PIN Entry for Encrypted Backup */}
   406	          {step === 'pin' && (
   407	            <form onSubmit={handleDecryptWithPin} className="space-y-4 animate-in fade-in duration-150">
   408	              <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-amber-500/10 border border-amber-500/20 text-xs text-amber-800 dark:text-amber-300 flex items-start space-x-2.5">
   409	                <Lock className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-500 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
   410	                <span>
   411	                  This backup is protected with AES-256 encryption. Enter the 4-digit security PIN configured during export.
   412	                </span>
   413	              </div>
   414	
   415	              <div className="space-y-1.5">
   416	                <label className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-500 block">
   417	                  Security PIN
   418	                </label>
   419	                <input
   420	                  type="password"
   421	                  inputMode="numeric"
   422	                  autoFocus
   423	                  placeholder="Enter PIN"
   424	                  value={pin}
   425	                  onChange={(e) => setPin(e.target.value)}
   426	                  className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 bg-stone-50 dark:bg-zinc-900 border border-stone-200 dark:border-zinc-800 rounded-xl text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-emerald-500"
   427	                />
   428	                {pinError && <p className="text-[11px] text-red-500 font-medium">{pinError}</p>}
   429	              </div>
   430	
   431	              <div className="flex items-center justify-end space-x-2 pt-2">
   432	                <button
   433	                  type="button"
   434	                  onClick={() => setStep('upload')}
   435	                  className="px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl border border-stone-200 dark:border-zinc-800 text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 hover:bg-stone-100 dark:hover:bg-zinc-900 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
   436	                >
   437	                  Back
   438	                </button>
   439	                <button
   440	                  type="submit"
   441	                  className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white text-xs font-bold transition-colors shadow-sm cursor-pointer"
   442	                >
   443	                  Unlock Backup
   444	                </button>
   445	              </div>
   446	            </form>
   447	          )}
   448	
   449	          {/* Step 3: Master Password Authentication Before Dry-Run (Hotfix 15.7.2) */}
   450	          {step === 'auth' && manifest && payload && (
   451	            <form onSubmit={handleAuthSubmit} className="space-y-4 animate-in fade-in duration-150">
   452	              {/* Manifest Badge */}
   453	              <div className="p-3.5 rounded-2xl bg-stone-50 dark:bg-zinc-900/60 border border-stone-200 dark:border-zinc-800 flex items-center justify-between">
   454	                <div className="space-y-0.5">
   455	                  <span className="text-xs font-bold text-zinc-900 dark:text-white block">
   456	                    {manifest.org_name} ({manifest.org_id})
   457	                  </span>
   458	                  <span className="text-[11px] text-zinc-500 block">
   459	                    Exported {new Date(manifest.exported_at).toLocaleDateString()}
   460	                  </span>
   461	                </div>
   462	                <div className="text-right">
   463	                  <span className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 block">
   464	                    {manifest.counts.events} Events
   465	                  </span>
   466	                  <span className="text-[10px] text-zinc-400 block">
   467	                    {manifest.counts.judges} Judges • {manifest.counts.participants} Roster
   468	                  </span>
   469	                </div>
   470	              </div>
   471	
   472	              {/* Restore Mode Selector */}
   473	              <div className="space-y-2">
   474	                <label className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-500 block">
   475	                  Restoration Strategy
   476	                </label>
   477	                <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
   478	                  <button
   479	                    type="button"
   480	                    onClick={() => handleModeChange('merge')}
   481	                    className={`p-3 rounded-2xl border text-left transition-colors cursor-pointer ${
   482	                      mode === 'merge'
   483	                        ? 'border-emerald-500 bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-900 dark:text-emerald-200'
   484	                        : 'border-stone-200 dark:border-zinc-800 text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 hover:bg-stone-50 dark:hover:bg-zinc-900'
   485	                    }`}
   486	                  >
   487	                    <span className="text-xs font-bold block">Merge</span>
   488	                    <span className="text-[10px] text-zinc-500 block mt-0.5">Safe update</span>
   489	                  </button>
   490	
   491	                  <button
   492	                    type="button"
   493	                    onClick={() => handleModeChange('add')}
   494	                    className={`p-3 rounded-2xl border text-left transition-colors cursor-pointer ${
   495	                      mode === 'add'
   496	                        ? 'border-emerald-500 bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-900 dark:text-emerald-200'
   497	                        : 'border-stone-200 dark:border-zinc-800 text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 hover:bg-stone-50 dark:hover:bg-zinc-900'
   498	                    }`}
   499	                  >
   500	                    <span className="text-xs font-bold block">Add Only</span>
   501	                    <span className="text-[10px] text-zinc-500 block mt-0.5">Skip existing</span>
   502	                  </button>
   503	
   504	                  <button
   505	                    type="button"
   506	                    onClick={() => handleModeChange('replace')}
   507	                    className={`p-3 rounded-2xl border text-left transition-colors cursor-pointer ${
   508	                      mode === 'replace'
   509	                        ? 'border-emerald-500 bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-900 dark:text-emerald-200'
   510	                        : 'border-stone-200 dark:border-zinc-800 text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 hover:bg-stone-50 dark:hover:bg-zinc-900'
   511	                    }`}
   512	                  >
   513	                    <span className="text-xs font-bold block">Replace</span>
   514	                    <span className="text-[10px] text-zinc-500 block mt-0.5">Wipe unlocked</span>
   515	                  </button>
   516	                </div>
   517	              </div>
   518	
   519	              {/* Locked Events Alert if Replace Mode is Refused */}
   520	              {lockedEventsWarning && (
   521	                <div className="p-3.5 rounded-2xl bg-amber-500/10 border border-amber-500/20 text-xs text-amber-800 dark:text-amber-300 space-y-2">
   522	                  <div className="flex items-start space-x-2">
   523	                    <AlertTriangle className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-500 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
   524	                    <span>{lockedEventsWarning}</span>
   525	                  </div>
   526	                  <button
   527	                    type="button"
   528	                    onClick={() => handleModeChange('merge')}
   529	                    className="w-full py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-amber-500/20 hover:bg-amber-500/30 text-amber-900 dark:text-amber-200 font-bold text-[11px] transition-colors"
   530	                  >
   531	                    Switch to Merge Mode
   532	                  </button>
   533	                </div>
   534	              )}
   535	
   536	              {/* Master Password Field */}
   537	              <div className="space-y-1.5">
   538	                <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
   539	                  <label className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-500 flex items-center space-x-1.5">
   540	                    <KeyRound className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-zinc-400" />
   541	                    <span>Organiser Master Password</span>
   542	                  </label>
   543	                  <span className="text-[10px] text-zinc-400">Required for verification</span>
   544	                </div>
   545	                
   546	                <div className="relative">
   547	                  <input
   548	                    type={showPassword ? 'text' : 'password'}
   549	                    autoFocus
   550	                    placeholder="Enter Master Password"
   551	                    value={masterPassword}
   552	                    onChange={(e) => {
   553	                      setMasterPassword(e.target.value);
   554	                      setPasswordError('');
   555	                    }}
   556	                    className="w-full pl-3.5 pr-10 py-2.5 bg-stone-50 dark:bg-zinc-900 border border-stone-200 dark:border-zinc-800 rounded-xl text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-emerald-500"
   557	                  />
   558	                  <button
   559	                    type="button"
   560	                    onClick={() => setShowPassword(!showPassword)}
   561	                    tabIndex={-1}
   562	                    aria-label={showPassword ? 'Hide password' : 'Show password'}
   563	                    className="absolute right-2.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-600 dark:hover:text-zinc-200 p-1"
   564	                  >
   565	                    {showPassword ? <EyeOff className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> : <Eye className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />}
   566	                  </button>
   567	                </div>
   568	                {passwordError && <p className="text-[11px] text-red-500 font-medium">{passwordError}</p>}
   569	                
   570	                <p className="text-[11px] text-zinc-400 pt-0.5">
   571	                  Your master password is authenticated on-the-fly via RPC and is cleared immediately when this window closes.
   572	                </p>
   573	              </div>
   574	
   575	              <div className="flex items-center justify-end space-x-2 pt-2">
   576	                <button
   577	                  type="button"
   578	                  onClick={() => setStep('upload')}
   579	                  className="px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl border border-stone-200 dark:border-zinc-800 text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 hover:bg-stone-100 dark:hover:bg-zinc-900 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
   580	                >
   581	                  Back
   582	                </button>
   583	                <button
   584	                  type="submit"
   585	                  disabled={isSimulating || !masterPassword.trim()}
   586	                  className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 disabled:opacity-50 text-white text-xs font-bold transition-colors shadow-sm flex items-center space-x-2 cursor-pointer"
   587	                >
   588	                  <RefreshCw className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 ${isSimulating ? 'animate-spin' : ''}`} />
   589	                  <span>{isSimulating ? 'Verifying & Simulating...' : 'Verify & Simulate'}</span>
   590	                </button>
   591	              </div>
   592	            </form>
   593	          )}
   594	
   595	          {/* Step 4: Dry-Run Preview with Verified Impact (Hotfix 15.7.2) */}
   596	          {step === 'preview' && manifest && payload && (
   597	            <div className="space-y-4 animate-in fade-in duration-150">
   598	              {/* Manifest Badge */}
   599	              <div className="p-3.5 rounded-2xl bg-stone-50 dark:bg-zinc-900/60 border border-stone-200 dark:border-zinc-800 flex items-center justify-between">
   600	                <div className="space-y-0.5">
   601	                  <div className="flex items-center space-x-1.5">
   602	                    <span className="text-xs font-bold text-zinc-900 dark:text-white block">
   603	                      {manifest.org_name} ({manifest.org_id})
   604	                    </span>
   605	                    <ShieldCheck className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-500" />
   606	                  </div>
   607	                  <span className="text-[11px] text-zinc-500 block">
   608	                    Mode: <strong className="capitalize text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300">{mode}</strong> • Verified by Master Password
   609	                  </span>
   610	                </div>
   611	                <div className="text-right">
   612	                  <span className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 block">
   613	                    {manifest.counts.events} Events
   614	                  </span>
   615	                  <span className="text-[10px] text-zinc-400 block">
   616	                    {manifest.counts.judges} Judges • {manifest.counts.participants} Roster
   617	                  </span>
   618	                </div>
   619	              </div>
   620	
   621	              {/* Restore Mode Switcher */}
   622	              <div className="space-y-2">
   623	                <label className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-500 block">
   624	                  Restoration Strategy
   625	                </label>
   626	                <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
   627	                  <button
   628	                    type="button"
   629	                    onClick={() => handleModeChange('merge')}
   630	                    className={`p-2.5 rounded-2xl border text-left transition-colors cursor-pointer ${
   631	                      mode === 'merge'
   632	                        ? 'border-emerald-500 bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-900 dark:text-emerald-200'
   633	                        : 'border-stone-200 dark:border-zinc-800 text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 hover:bg-stone-50 dark:hover:bg-zinc-900'
   634	                    }`}
   635	                  >
   636	                    <span className="text-xs font-bold block">Merge</span>
   637	                    <span className="text-[10px] text-zinc-500 block mt-0.5">Safe update</span>
   638	                  </button>
   639	
   640	                  <button
   641	                    type="button"
   642	                    onClick={() => handleModeChange('add')}
   643	                    className={`p-2.5 rounded-2xl border text-left transition-colors cursor-pointer ${
   644	                      mode === 'add'
   645	                        ? 'border-emerald-500 bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-900 dark:text-emerald-200'
   646	                        : 'border-stone-200 dark:border-zinc-800 text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 hover:bg-stone-50 dark:hover:bg-zinc-900'
   647	                    }`}
   648	                  >
   649	                    <span className="text-xs font-bold block">Add Only</span>
   650	                    <span className="text-[10px] text-zinc-500 block mt-0.5">Skip existing</span>
   651	                  </button>
   652	
   653	                  <button
   654	                    type="button"
   655	                    onClick={() => handleModeChange('replace')}
   656	                    className={`p-2.5 rounded-2xl border text-left transition-colors cursor-pointer ${
   657	                      mode === 'replace'
   658	                        ? 'border-emerald-500 bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-900 dark:text-emerald-200'
   659	                        : 'border-stone-200 dark:border-zinc-800 text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 hover:bg-stone-50 dark:hover:bg-zinc-900'
   660	                    }`}
   661	                  >
   662	                    <span className="text-xs font-bold block">Replace</span>
   663	                    <span className="text-[10px] text-zinc-500 block mt-0.5">Wipe unlocked</span>
   664	                  </button>
   665	                </div>
   666	              </div>
   667	
   668	              {/* Locked Events Alert if Replace Mode is Refused */}
   669	              {lockedEventsWarning && (
   670	                <div className="p-3.5 rounded-2xl bg-amber-500/10 border border-amber-500/20 text-xs text-amber-800 dark:text-amber-300 space-y-2">
   671	                  <div className="flex items-start space-x-2">
   672	                    <AlertTriangle className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-500 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
   673	                    <span>{lockedEventsWarning}</span>
   674	                  </div>
   675	                  <button
   676	                    type="button"
   677	                    onClick={() => handleModeChange('merge')}
   678	                    className="w-full py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-amber-500/20 hover:bg-amber-500/30 text-amber-900 dark:text-amber-200 font-bold text-[11px] transition-colors"
   679	                  >
   680	                    Switch to Merge Mode
   681	                  </button>
   682	                </div>
   683	              )}
   684	
   685	              {/* Dry-Run Simulation Box */}
   686	              <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl border border-stone-200 dark:border-zinc-800 space-y-2.5">
   687	                <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
   688	                  <span className="text-xs font-bold text-zinc-900 dark:text-white">
   689	                    Dry-Run Verified Impact
   690	                  </span>
   691	                  {isSimulating && (
