   692	                    <span className="text-[11px] text-zinc-400 flex items-center space-x-1">
   693	                      <RefreshCw className="w-3 h-3 animate-spin" />
   694	                      <span>Re-simulating...</span>
   695	                    </span>
   696	                  )}
   697	                </div>
   698	
   699	                {summary ? (
   700	                  <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-2 pt-1 text-center">
   701	                    <div className="p-2 rounded-xl bg-stone-50 dark:bg-zinc-900/60">
   702	                      <span className="text-sm font-mono font-bold text-zinc-900 dark:text-white block">
   703	                        {(summary.events_created || 0) + (summary.events_updated || 0)}
   704	                      </span>
   705	                      <span className="text-[10px] text-zinc-500 uppercase block">Events</span>
   706	                    </div>
   707	
   708	                    <div className="p-2 rounded-xl bg-stone-50 dark:bg-zinc-900/60">
   709	                      <span className="text-sm font-mono font-bold text-zinc-900 dark:text-white block">
   710	                        {(summary.judges_created || 0) + (summary.judges_updated || 0)}
   711	                      </span>
   712	                      <span className="text-[10px] text-zinc-500 uppercase block">Judges</span>
   713	                    </div>
   714	
   715	                    <div className="p-2 rounded-xl bg-stone-50 dark:bg-zinc-900/60">
   716	                      <span className="text-sm font-mono font-bold text-zinc-900 dark:text-white block">
   717	                        {(summary.participants_created || 0) + (summary.participants_updated || 0)}
   718	                      </span>
   719	                      <span className="text-[10px] text-zinc-500 uppercase block">Roster</span>
   720	                    </div>
   721	
   722	                    <div className="p-2 rounded-xl bg-stone-50 dark:bg-zinc-900/60">
   723	                      <span className="text-sm font-mono font-bold text-zinc-900 dark:text-white block">
   724	                        {(summary.scores_created || 0) + (summary.scores_updated || 0)}
   725	                      </span>
   726	                      <span className="text-[10px] text-zinc-500 uppercase block">Scores</span>
   727	                    </div>
   728	                  </div>
   729	                ) : (
   730	                  <p className="text-[11px] text-zinc-400 italic">Calculating impact...</p>
   731	                )}
   732	
   733	                {summary && (summary.events_protected || 0) > 0 && (
   734	                  <div className="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-emerald-500/10 border border-emerald-500/20 text-[11px] text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-300 flex items-center space-x-2">
   735	                    <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-500 shrink-0" />
   736	                    <span>{summary.events_protected} locked event(s) immune from deletion or modification.</span>
   737	                  </div>
   738	                )}
   739	              </div>
   740	            </div>
   741	          )}
   742	
   743	          {/* Step 5: Done */}
   744	          {step === 'done' && (
   745	            <div className="py-6 text-center space-y-3 animate-in zoom-in-95 duration-150">
   746	              <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-full bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-500 mx-auto flex items-center justify-center">
   747	                <Check className="w-6 h-6 stroke-[3]" />
   748	              </div>
   749	              <h4 className="text-base font-bold text-zinc-900 dark:text-white">
   750	                Restoration Complete
   751	              </h4>
   752	              <p className="text-xs text-zinc-500 max-w-xs mx-auto">
   753	                All records were synchronized. A safety snapshot was saved to local storage should you need to undo.
   754	              </p>
   755	            </div>
   756	          )}
   757	        </div>
   758	
   759	        {/* Pinned Footer */}
   760	        <div className="shrink-0 p-4 border-t border-stone-100 dark:border-zinc-800/80 bg-stone-50/50 dark:bg-zinc-900/30 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
   761	          {step === 'done' ? (
   762	            <button
   763	              type="button"
   764	              onClick={handleClose}
   765	              className="w-full py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white text-xs font-bold transition-colors shadow-sm cursor-pointer"
   766	            >
   767	              Done
   768	            </button>
   769	          ) : (
   770	            <>
   771	              <button
   772	                type="button"
   773	                onClick={handleClose}
   774	                disabled={isApplying}
   775	                className="min-h-[44px] px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-stone-200 dark:border-zinc-800 text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 hover:bg-stone-100 dark:hover:bg-zinc-900 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
   776	              >
   777	                Cancel
   778	              </button>
   779	
   780	              {step === 'preview' && (
   781	                <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
   782	                  <button
   783	                    type="button"
   784	                    onClick={() => setStep('auth')}
   785	                    disabled={isApplying}
   786	                    className="min-h-[44px] px-3 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-stone-200 dark:border-zinc-800 text-xs font-medium text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400 hover:bg-stone-100 dark:hover:bg-zinc-900 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
   787	                  >
   788	                    Change Credentials
   789	                  </button>
   790	                  <button
   791	                    type="button"
   792	                    onClick={handleExecuteRestore}
   793	                    disabled={isApplying || isSimulating || Boolean(lockedEventsWarning && mode === 'replace')}
   794	                    className="min-h-[44px] px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 disabled:opacity-50 text-white text-xs font-bold transition-colors shadow-sm flex items-center space-x-2 cursor-pointer active:scale-95 motion-reduce:transition-none"
   795	                  >
   796	                    <RefreshCw className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 ${isApplying ? 'animate-spin' : ''}`} />
   797	                    <span>{isApplying ? 'Restoring...' : 'Apply Restoration'}</span>
   798	                  </button>
   799	                </div>
   800	              )}
   801	            </>
   802	          )}
   803	        </div>
   804	      </div>
   805	    </div>
   806	  );
   807	}

==================== FILE: sql/15.7-stage-b1.sql ====================
     1	-- ====================================================================
     2	-- EPIC 15.7 STAGE B1: Password Hashing, Secret Table & Brute-Force Lockout
     3	-- Safe additive migration: existing password column remains intact until B3.
     4	-- ====================================================================
     5	
     6	-- 1. Crypto Extension
     7	create extension if not exists pgcrypto with schema extensions;
     8	
     9	-- Ensure organizations.org_id has a unique constraint if not already unique
    10	do $$
    11	begin
    12	  if not exists (
    13	    select 1 from pg_constraint
    14	    where conrelid = 'public.organizations'::regclass
    15	      and contype in ('p', 'u')
    16	      and conkey = array[(select attnum from pg_attribute where attrelid = 'public.organizations'::regclass and attname = 'org_id')]
    17	  ) then
    18	    alter table public.organizations add constraint organizations_org_id_key unique (org_id);
    19	  end if;
    20	end $$;
    21	
    22	-- 2. Secret Tables (Never accessible to public anon key)
    23	create table if not exists public.org_secrets (
    24	  org_id text primary key references public.organizations(org_id) on delete cascade,
    25	  password_hash text not null,
    26	  updated_at timestamptz not null default now()
    27	);
    28	
    29	create table if not exists public.org_login_throttle (
    30	  key text primary key,
    31	  fails int not null default 0,
    32	  first_fail_at timestamptz,
    33	  locked_until timestamptz
    34	);
    35	
    36	-- 3. Row Level Security & Explicit Revocation
    37	alter table public.org_secrets enable row level security;
    38	alter table public.org_login_throttle enable row level security;
    39	
    40	revoke all on public.org_secrets from anon, authenticated, public;
    41	revoke all on public.org_login_throttle from anon, authenticated, public;
    42	
    43	-- 4. Initial Migration of Existing Plaintext Passwords into bcrypt Hashes
    44	insert into public.org_secrets(org_id, password_hash)
    45	select org_id, extensions.crypt(btrim(password), extensions.gen_salt('bf', 10))
    46	from public.organizations
    47	where password is not null and btrim(password) <> ''
    48	on conflict (org_id) do nothing;
    49	
    50	-- Audit notice
    51	do $$
    52	declare
    53	  org_count int;
    54	  secret_count int;
    55	  unmigrated_list text;
    56	begin
    57	  select count(*) into org_count from public.organizations;
    58	  select count(*) into secret_count from public.org_secrets;
    59	  select string_agg(org_id, ', ') into unmigrated_list 
    60	  from public.organizations 
    61	  where org_id not in (select org_id from public.org_secrets);
    62	  
    63	  raise notice 'MIGRATION AUDIT: % organizations, % org_secrets rows created.', org_count, secret_count;
    64	  if unmigrated_list is not null then
    65	    raise notice 'WARNING: The following org_ids do not have a password secret: %', unmigrated_list;
    66	  else
    67	    raise notice 'SUCCESS: All organizations successfully migrated to org_secrets.';
    68	  end if;
    69	end $$;
    70	
    71	-- 5. Internal Verification Helper (never accessible to public/anon)
    72	create or replace function public._fj_verify_org(p_identifier text, p_password text)
    73	returns jsonb
    74	language plpgsql
    75	security definer
    76	set search_path = public, extensions
    77	as $$
    78	declare
    79	  v_key text;
    80	  v_pwd text;
    81	  v_org_id text;
    82	  v_tkey text;
    83	  v_locked_until timestamptz;
    84	  v_hash text;
    85	  v_fails int;
    86	begin
    87	  v_key := lower(btrim(coalesce(p_identifier, '')));
    88	  v_pwd := btrim(coalesce(p_password, ''));
    89	
    90	  if v_key = '' or v_pwd = '' then
    91	    return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'invalid_credentials');
    92	  end if;
    93	
    94	  -- Resolve org_id by org_id or email
    95	  select o.org_id into v_org_id
    96	  from public.organizations o
    97	  where lower(o.org_id) = v_key or lower(coalesce(o.email, '')) = v_key
    98	  limit 1;
    99	
   100	  v_tkey := lower(coalesce(v_org_id, v_key));
   101	
   102	  -- Check brute-force lockout
   103	  select locked_until into v_locked_until
   104	  from public.org_login_throttle
   105	  where key = v_tkey;
   106	
   107	  if v_locked_until is not null and v_locked_until > now() then
   108	    return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'too_many_attempts');
   109	  end if;
   110	
   111	  -- Load hash from org_secrets
   112	  if v_org_id is not null then
   113	    select password_hash into v_hash
   114	    from public.org_secrets
   115	    where org_id = v_org_id;
   116	  end if;
   117	
   118	  -- Verify bcrypt hash
   119	  if v_hash is not null and extensions.crypt(v_pwd, v_hash) = v_hash then
   120	    delete from public.org_login_throttle where key = v_tkey;
   121	    return jsonb_build_object('ok', true, 'org_id', v_org_id);
   122	  else
   123	    -- Record failed attempt (lock after 5 fails for 15 minutes)
   124	    insert into public.org_login_throttle (key, fails, first_fail_at, locked_until)
   125	    values (v_tkey, 1, now(), null)
   126	    on conflict (key) do update set
   127	      fails = case
   128	        when public.org_login_throttle.first_fail_at < now() - interval '15 minutes' then 1
   129	        else public.org_login_throttle.fails + 1
   130	      end,
   131	      first_fail_at = case
   132	        when public.org_login_throttle.first_fail_at < now() - interval '15 minutes' then now()
   133	        else public.org_login_throttle.first_fail_at
   134	      end,
   135	      locked_until = case
   136	        when (case when public.org_login_throttle.first_fail_at < now() - interval '15 minutes' then 1 else public.org_login_throttle.fails + 1 end) >= 5
   137	        then now() + interval '15 minutes'
   138	        else null
   139	      end
   140	    returning fails into v_fails;
   141	
   142	    -- 2% probability background maintenance
   143	    if random() < 0.02 then
   144	      delete from public.org_login_throttle
   145	      where first_fail_at < now() - interval '1 day'
   146	        and (locked_until is null or locked_until < now());
   147	    end if;
   148	
   149	    return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'invalid_credentials');
   150	  end if;
   151	end;
   152	$$;
   153	
   154	revoke all on function public._fj_verify_org(text, text) from public, anon, authenticated;
   155	
   156	-- 6. Public API Functions (SECURITY DEFINER, sanitize password output)
   157	
   158	-- Login
   159	create or replace function public.org_login(p_identifier text, p_password text)
   160	returns jsonb
   161	language plpgsql
   162	security definer
   163	set search_path = public, extensions
   164	as $$
   165	declare
   166	  v jsonb;
   167	  v_org jsonb;
   168	begin
   169	  v := public._fj_verify_org(p_identifier, p_password);
   170	  if not (v->>'ok')::boolean then
   171	    return v;
   172	  end if;
   173	
   174	  select to_jsonb(o) - 'password' into v_org
   175	  from public.organizations o
   176	  where o.org_id = v->>'org_id';
   177	
   178	  return jsonb_build_object('ok', true, 'org', v_org);
   179	end;
   180	$$;
   181	
   182	-- Verify Password (for sensitive confirmations such as deleting ended events)
   183	create or replace function public.org_verify_password(p_org_id text, p_password text)
   184	returns jsonb
   185	language plpgsql
   186	security definer
   187	set search_path = public, extensions
   188	as $$
   189	declare
   190	  v jsonb;
   191	begin
   192	  v := public._fj_verify_org(p_org_id, p_password);
   193	  if (v->>'ok')::boolean then
   194	    return jsonb_build_object('ok', true);
   195	  else
   196	    return v;
   197	  end if;
   198	end;
   199	$$;
   200	
   201	-- Change Password
   202	create or replace function public.org_change_password(p_org_id text, p_old text, p_new text)
   203	returns jsonb
   204	language plpgsql
   205	security definer
   206	set search_path = public, extensions
   207	as $$
   208	declare
   209	  v jsonb;
   210	  clean_new text;
   211	begin
   212	  v := public._fj_verify_org(p_org_id, p_old);
   213	  if not (v->>'ok')::boolean then
   214	    return v;
   215	  end if;
   216	
   217	  clean_new := btrim(coalesce(p_new, ''));
   218	  if length(clean_new) < 8 or length(clean_new) > 72 then
   219	    return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'weak_password');
   220	  end if;
   221	
   222	  update public.org_secrets
   223	  set password_hash = extensions.crypt(clean_new, extensions.gen_salt('bf', 10)),
   224	      updated_at = now()
   225	  where org_id = v->>'org_id';
   226	
   227	  delete from public.org_login_throttle where key = lower(v->>'org_id');
   228	
   229	  return jsonb_build_object('ok', true);
   230	end;
   231	$$;
   232	
   233	-- Register New Organization
   234	create or replace function public.org_register(p_org_id text, p_name text, p_email text, p_password text)
   235	returns jsonb
   236	language plpgsql
   237	security definer
   238	set search_path = public, extensions
   239	as $$
   240	declare
   241	  clean_org_id text;
   242	  clean_name text;
   243	  clean_email text;
   244	  clean_pass text;
   245	  existing_id text;
   246	  v_org_row public.organizations;
   247	begin
   248	  clean_org_id := upper(btrim(coalesce(p_org_id, '')));
   249	  clean_name := btrim(coalesce(p_name, ''));
   250	  clean_email := lower(btrim(coalesce(p_email, '')));
   251	  if clean_email = '' then
   252	    clean_email := null;
   253	  end if;
   254	  clean_pass := btrim(coalesce(p_password, ''));
   255	
   256	  if clean_org_id = '' or clean_name = '' then
   257	    return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'invalid_input');
   258	  end if;
   259	
   260	  if length(clean_pass) < 8 or length(clean_pass) > 72 then
   261	    return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'weak_password');
   262	  end if;
   263	
   264	  -- Duplicate check: org_id or email
   265	  select org_id into existing_id
   266	  from public.organizations
   267	  where lower(org_id) = lower(clean_org_id)
   268	     or (clean_email is not null and lower(coalesce(email, '')) = clean_email)
   269	  limit 1;
   270	
   271	  if existing_id is not null then
   272	    return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'reason', 'duplicate');
   273	  end if;
   274	
   275	  -- Insert organizations row (atomic with org_secrets)
   276	  insert into public.organizations (
   277	    id,
   278	    org_id,
   279	    name,
   280	    email,
   281	    verification_status,
   282	    tie_breaker_enabled,
   283	    tie_breaker_method,
   284	    custom_participant_fields,
   285	    blind_judging_enabled,
   286	    auto_advance_enabled,
   287	    grace_period_enabled,
   288	    isolated_jury_enabled,
   289	    created_at
   290	  ) values (
   291	    gen_random_uuid(),
   292	    clean_org_id,
   293	    clean_name,
   294	    clean_email,
   295	    'pending',
   296	    false,
   297	    'olympic',
   298	    '[]'::jsonb,
   299	    false,
   300	    true,
   301	    false,
   302	    false,
   303	    now()
   304	  )
   305	  returning * into v_org_row;
   306	
   307	  -- Insert secret hash
   308	  insert into public.org_secrets (org_id, password_hash, updated_at)
   309	  values (clean_org_id, extensions.crypt(clean_pass, extensions.gen_salt('bf', 10)), now());
   310	
   311	  return jsonb_build_object('ok', true, 'org', to_jsonb(v_org_row) - 'password');
   312	end;
   313	$$;
   314	
   315	-- 7. Grant execution permissions to anon and authenticated
   316	revoke all on function public.org_login(text, text) from public;
   317	grant execute on function public.org_login(text, text) to anon, authenticated;
   318	
   319	revoke all on function public.org_verify_password(text, text) from public;
   320	grant execute on function public.org_verify_password(text, text) to anon, authenticated;
   321	
   322	revoke all on function public.org_change_password(text, text, text) from public;
   323	grant execute on function public.org_change_password(text, text, text) to anon, authenticated;
   324	
   325	revoke all on function public.org_register(text, text, text, text) from public;
   326	grant execute on function public.org_register(text, text, text, text) to anon, authenticated;

==================== FILE: sql/15.10-stage1-security-lock.sql ====================
     1	-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
     2	-- EPIC 15.10 — Stage 1: Security Lock (Idempotent Verification & Hardening)
     3	-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
     4	-- Safe to run regardless of whether 15.7 D1/D2/D3 were partially applied:
     5	-- 1. Ensures events.is_archived column exists.
     6	-- 2. Ensures org_sessions & judge_sessions + token verification & logout RPCs exist.
     7	-- 3. Ensures all 14 Stage D3 token-authenticated write RPCs exist.
     8	-- 4. Fixes event_delete to delete child scores before deleting the event row (prevents FK 23503).
     9	-- 5. Revokes all direct INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE grants on core tables from anon & authenticated
    10	--    and enforces SELECT-only RLS policies.
    11	-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    12	
    13	create extension if not exists pgcrypto with schema extensions;
    14	
    15	-- 1. Ensure archive column exists on events
    16	alter table public.events
    17	  add column if not exists is_archived boolean not null default false;
    18	
    19	-- 2. Ensure Server-Only Session Tables exist (RLS ON, zero public access)
    20	create table if not exists public.org_sessions (
    21	  token text primary key,
    22	  org_id text not null references public.organizations(org_id) on delete cascade,
    23	  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
    24	  expires_at timestamptz not null default (now() + interval '30 days')
    25	);
    26	
    27	create index if not exists idx_org_sessions_org_id on public.org_sessions(org_id);
    28	alter table public.org_sessions enable row level security;
    29	revoke all on table public.org_sessions from anon, authenticated, public;
    30	
    31	create table if not exists public.judge_sessions (
    32	  token text primary key,
    33	  judge_id uuid not null references public.judges(id) on delete cascade,
    34	  org_id text not null references public.organizations(org_id) on delete cascade,
    35	  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
    36	  expires_at timestamptz not null default (now() + interval '30 days')
    37	);
    38	
    39	create index if not exists idx_judge_sessions_judge_id on public.judge_sessions(judge_id);
    40	create index if not exists idx_judge_sessions_org_id on public.judge_sessions(org_id);
    41	alter table public.judge_sessions enable row level security;
    42	revoke all on table public.judge_sessions from anon, authenticated, public;
    43	
    44	-- 3. Internal Token Verification Helpers (SECURITY DEFINER RPC use only)
    45	create or replace function public._fj_verify_org_token(p_token text, p_org_id text default null)
    46	returns text
    47	language plpgsql
    48	security definer
    49	set search_path = public, extensions
    50	as $$
    51	declare
    52	  v_tok text;
    53	  v_org_id text;
    54	begin
    55	  v_tok := btrim(coalesce(p_token, ''));
    56	  if v_tok = '' then
    57	    return null;
    58	  end if;
    59	
    60	  select s.org_id into v_org_id
    61	  from public.org_sessions s
    62	  where s.token = v_tok
    63	    and s.expires_at > now()
    64	  limit 1;
    65	
    66	  if v_org_id is null then
    67	    return null;
    68	  end if;
    69	
    70	  if p_org_id is not null and btrim(p_org_id) <> '' and upper(v_org_id) <> upper(btrim(p_org_id)) then
    71	    return null;
    72	  end if;
    73	
    74	  return v_org_id;
    75	end;
    76	$$;
    77	
    78	revoke all on function public._fj_verify_org_token(text, text) from public, anon, authenticated;
    79	
    80	create or replace function public._fj_verify_judge_token(p_token text)
    81	returns jsonb
    82	language plpgsql
    83	security definer
    84	set search_path = public, extensions
    85	as $$
    86	declare
    87	  v_tok text;
    88	  v_rec record;
    89	begin
    90	  v_tok := btrim(coalesce(p_token, ''));
    91	  if v_tok = '' then
    92	    return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'unauthorized');
    93	  end if;
    94	
    95	  select s.judge_id, s.org_id into v_rec
    96	  from public.judge_sessions s
    97	  where s.token = v_tok
    98	    and s.expires_at > now()
    99	  limit 1;
   100	
   101	  if v_rec.judge_id is null then
   102	    return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'unauthorized');
   103	  end if;
   104	
   105	  return jsonb_build_object('ok', true, 'judge_id', v_rec.judge_id, 'org_id', v_rec.org_id);
   106	end;
   107	$$;
   108	
   109	revoke all on function public._fj_verify_judge_token(text) from public, anon, authenticated;
   110	
   111	-- 4. Logout RPCs
   112	create or replace function public.org_logout(p_token text)
   113	returns jsonb
   114	language plpgsql
   115	security definer
   116	set search_path = public, extensions
   117	as $$
   118	begin
   119	  if btrim(coalesce(p_token, '')) <> '' then
   120	    delete from public.org_sessions where token = btrim(p_token);
   121	  end if;
   122	  return jsonb_build_object('ok', true);
   123	end;
   124	$$;
   125	
   126	revoke all on function public.org_logout(text) from public;
   127	grant execute on function public.org_logout(text) to anon, authenticated;
   128	
   129	create or replace function public.judge_logout(p_token text)
   130	returns jsonb
   131	language plpgsql
   132	security definer
   133	set search_path = public, extensions
   134	as $$
   135	begin
   136	  if btrim(coalesce(p_token, '')) <> '' then
   137	    delete from public.judge_sessions where token = btrim(p_token);
   138	  end if;
   139	  return jsonb_build_object('ok', true);
   140	end;
   141	$$;
   142	
   143	revoke all on function public.judge_logout(text) from public;
   144	grant execute on function public.judge_logout(text) to anon, authenticated;
   145	
   146	-- 5. Organization Write RPCs
   147	create or replace function public.org_update(
   148	  p_token text,
   149	  p_org_id text,
   150	  p_updates jsonb
   151	)
   152	returns jsonb
   153	language plpgsql
   154	security definer
   155	set search_path = public, extensions
   156	as $$
   157	declare
   158	  v_org_id text;
   159	  v_row public.organizations;
   160	begin
   161	  v_org_id := public._fj_verify_org_token(p_token, p_org_id);
   162	  if v_org_id is null then
   163	    return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'unauthorized', 'message', 'Session expired or invalid. Please sign in again.');
   164	  end if;
   165	
   166	  update public.organizations
   167	  set
   168	    name = case when p_updates ? 'name' then coalesce(nullif(btrim(p_updates->>'name'), ''), name) else name end,
   169	    email = case when p_updates ? 'email' then nullif(btrim(p_updates->>'email'), '') else email end,
   170	    verification_status = case when p_updates ? 'verification_status' then coalesce(p_updates->>'verification_status', verification_status) else verification_status end,
   171	    tie_breaker_enabled = case when p_updates ? 'tie_breaker_enabled' then coalesce((p_updates->>'tie_breaker_enabled')::boolean, tie_breaker_enabled) else tie_breaker_enabled end,
   172	    tie_breaker_method = case when p_updates ? 'tie_breaker_method' then coalesce(p_updates->>'tie_breaker_method', tie_breaker_method) else tie_breaker_method end,
   173	    custom_participant_fields = case when p_updates ? 'custom_participant_fields' then coalesce(p_updates->'custom_participant_fields', custom_participant_fields) else custom_participant_fields end,
   174	    blind_judging_enabled = case when p_updates ? 'blind_judging_enabled' then coalesce((p_updates->>'blind_judging_enabled')::boolean, blind_judging_enabled) else blind_judging_enabled end,
   175	    auto_advance_enabled = case when p_updates ? 'auto_advance_enabled' then coalesce((p_updates->>'auto_advance_enabled')::boolean, auto_advance_enabled) else auto_advance_enabled end,
   176	    grace_period_enabled = case when p_updates ? 'grace_period_enabled' then coalesce((p_updates->>'grace_period_enabled')::boolean, grace_period_enabled) else grace_period_enabled end,
   177	    isolated_jury_enabled = case when p_updates ? 'isolated_jury_enabled' then coalesce((p_updates->>'isolated_jury_enabled')::boolean, isolated_jury_enabled) else isolated_jury_enabled end
   178	  where upper(org_id) = upper(v_org_id)
   179	  returning * into v_row;
   180	
   181	  if v_row.id is null then
   182	    return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'not_found', 'message', 'Organization not found.');
   183	  end if;
   184	
   185	  return jsonb_build_object('ok', true, 'data', to_jsonb(v_row) - 'password');
   186	end;
   187	$$;
   188	
   189	revoke all on function public.org_update(text, text, jsonb) from public;
   190	grant execute on function public.org_update(text, text, jsonb) to anon, authenticated;
   191	
   192	create or replace function public.org_reset_data(
   193	  p_token text,
   194	  p_org_id text
   195	)
   196	returns jsonb
   197	language plpgsql
   198	security definer
   199	set search_path = public, extensions
   200	as $$
   201	declare
   202	  v_org_id text;
   203	begin
   204	  v_org_id := public._fj_verify_org_token(p_token, p_org_id);
   205	  if v_org_id is null then
   206	    return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'unauthorized', 'message', 'Session expired or invalid. Please sign in again.');
   207	  end if;
   208	
   209	  if exists (select 1 from public.events where upper(org_id) = upper(v_org_id) and is_locked = true) then
   210	    return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'ROSTER_LOCKED', 'message', 'ROSTER_LOCKED: Unlock your events first.');
   211	  end if;
   212	
   213	  delete from public.scores where event_id in (select id from public.events where upper(org_id) = upper(v_org_id));
   214	  delete from public.events where upper(org_id) = upper(v_org_id);
   215	  delete from public.participants where upper(org_id) = upper(v_org_id);
   216	  delete from public.judges where upper(org_id) = upper(v_org_id);
   217	
   218	  return jsonb_build_object('ok', true);
   219	end;
   220	$$;
   221	
   222	revoke all on function public.org_reset_data(text, text) from public;
   223	grant execute on function public.org_reset_data(text, text) to anon, authenticated;
   224	
   225	create or replace function public.org_delete(
   226	  p_token text,
   227	  p_org_id text
   228	)
   229	returns jsonb
   230	language plpgsql
   231	security definer
   232	set search_path = public, extensions
   233	as $$
   234	declare
   235	  v_org_id text;
   236	begin
   237	  v_org_id := public._fj_verify_org_token(p_token, p_org_id);
   238	  if v_org_id is null then
   239	    return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'unauthorized', 'message', 'Session expired or invalid. Please sign in again.');
   240	  end if;
   241	
   242	  if exists (select 1 from public.events where upper(org_id) = upper(v_org_id) and is_locked = true) then
   243	    return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'ROSTER_LOCKED', 'message', 'ROSTER_LOCKED: Unlock your events first.');
   244	  end if;
   245	
   246	  delete from public.scores where event_id in (select id from public.events where upper(org_id) = upper(v_org_id));
   247	  delete from public.events where upper(org_id) = upper(v_org_id);
   248	  delete from public.participants where upper(org_id) = upper(v_org_id);
   249	  delete from public.judges where upper(org_id) = upper(v_org_id);
   250	  delete from public.organizations where upper(org_id) = upper(v_org_id);
   251	
   252	  return jsonb_build_object('ok', true);
   253	end;
   254	$$;
   255

   257	grant execute on function public.org_delete(text, text) to anon, authenticated;

   259	-- 6. Event Write RPCs (with fixed event_delete child-score cleanup)
   260	create or replace function public.event_upsert(
   261	  p_token text,
   262	  p_record jsonb
   263	)
   264	returns jsonb
   265	language plpgsql
   266	security definer
   267	set search_path = public, extensions
   268	as $$
   269	declare
   270	  v_org_id text;
   271	  v_id uuid;
   272	  v_existing_org text;
   273	  v_row public.events;
   274	begin
   275	  v_org_id := public._fj_verify_org_token(p_token, p_record->>'org_id');
   276	  if v_org_id is null then
   277	    return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'unauthorized', 'message', 'Session expired or invalid. Please sign in again.');
   278	  end if;
   279	
   280	  v_id := coalesce(nullif(p_record->>'id', '')::uuid, gen_random_uuid());
   281	
   282	  select org_id into v_existing_org from public.events where id = v_id;
   283	  if v_existing_org is not null and upper(v_existing_org) <> upper(v_org_id) then
   284	    return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'forbidden', 'message', 'Cannot overwrite event from another organization.');
   285	  end if;
   286	
   287	  insert into public.events (
   288	    id, org_id, name, category, date, status, started_at, elapsed_seconds,
   289	    rubric_schema, assigned_judges, assigned_participants, participant_sl_numbers,
   290	    is_locked, is_archived, created_at, updated_at
   291	  ) values (
   292	    v_id,
   293	    v_org_id,
   294	    coalesce(nullif(btrim(p_record->>'name'), ''), 'Event'),
   295	    coalesce(p_record->>'category', 'General'),
   296	    p_record->>'date',
   297	    coalesce(lower(p_record->>'status'), 'draft'),
   298	    case when nullif(p_record->>'started_at', '') is not null then (p_record->>'started_at')::timestamptz else null end,
   299	    coalesce((p_record->>'elapsed_seconds')::integer, 0),
   300	    coalesce(p_record->'rubric_schema', '[]'::jsonb),
   301	    case when jsonb_typeof(p_record->'assigned_judges') = 'array' then array(select jsonb_array_elements_text(p_record->'assigned_judges')) else '{}'::text[] end,
   302	    case when jsonb_typeof(p_record->'assigned_participants') = 'array' then array(select jsonb_array_elements_text(p_record->'assigned_participants')) else '{}'::text[] end,
   303	    coalesce(p_record->'participant_sl_numbers', '{}'::jsonb),
   304	    coalesce((p_record->>'is_locked')::boolean, false),
   305	    coalesce((p_record->>'is_archived')::boolean, false),
   306	    coalesce(nullif(p_record->>'created_at', '')::timestamptz, now()),
   307	    now()
   308	  )
   309	  on conflict (id) do update set
   310	    name = excluded.name,
   311	    category = excluded.category,
   312	    date = excluded.date,
   313	    status = excluded.status,
   314	    started_at = excluded.started_at,
   315	    elapsed_seconds = excluded.elapsed_seconds,
   316	    rubric_schema = excluded.rubric_schema,
   317	    assigned_judges = excluded.assigned_judges,
   318	    assigned_participants = excluded.assigned_participants,
   319	    participant_sl_numbers = excluded.participant_sl_numbers,
   320	    is_locked = excluded.is_locked,
   321	    is_archived = excluded.is_archived,
   322	    updated_at = now()
   323	  returning * into v_row;
   324	
   325	  return jsonb_build_object('ok', true, 'data', to_jsonb(v_row));
   326	end;
   327	$$;
   328	
   329	revoke all on function public.event_upsert(text, jsonb) from public;
   330	grant execute on function public.event_upsert(text, jsonb) to anon, authenticated;
   331	
   332	create or replace function public.event_update(
   333	  p_token text,
   334	  p_event_id uuid,
   335	  p_updates jsonb
   336	)
   337	returns jsonb
   338	language plpgsql
   339	security definer
   340	set search_path = public, extensions
   341	as $$
   342	declare
   343	  v_org_id text;
   344	  v_row public.events;
   345	begin
   346	  v_org_id := public._fj_verify_org_token(p_token, null);
   347	  if v_org_id is null then
   348	    return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'unauthorized', 'message', 'Session expired or invalid. Please sign in again.');
   349	  end if;
   350	
   351	  update public.events
   352	  set
   353	    name = case when p_updates ? 'name' then coalesce(nullif(btrim(p_updates->>'name'), ''), name) else name end,
   354	    category = case when p_updates ? 'category' then p_updates->>'category' else category end,
   355	    date = case when p_updates ? 'date' then p_updates->>'date' else date end,
   356	    status = case when p_updates ? 'status' then coalesce(lower(p_updates->>'status'), status) else status end,
   357	    started_at = case when p_updates ? 'started_at' then (nullif(p_updates->>'started_at', ''))::timestamptz else started_at end,
   358	    elapsed_seconds = case when p_updates ? 'elapsed_seconds' then coalesce((p_updates->>'elapsed_seconds')::integer, elapsed_seconds) else elapsed_seconds end,
   359	    rubric_schema = case when p_updates ? 'rubric_schema' then coalesce(p_updates->'rubric_schema', rubric_schema) else rubric_schema end,
   360	    assigned_judges = case when p_updates ? 'assigned_judges' then (case when jsonb_typeof(p_updates->'assigned_judges') = 'array' then array(select jsonb_array_elements_text(p_updates->'assigned_judges')) else '{}'::text[] end) else assigned_judges end,
   361	    assigned_participants = case when p_updates ? 'assigned_participants' then (case when jsonb_typeof(p_updates->'assigned_participants') = 'array' then array(select jsonb_array_elements_text(p_updates->'assigned_participants')) else '{}'::text[] end) else assigned_participants end,
   362	    participant_sl_numbers = case when p_updates ? 'participant_sl_numbers' then coalesce(p_updates->'participant_sl_numbers', participant_sl_numbers) else participant_sl_numbers end,
   363	    is_locked = case when p_updates ? 'is_locked' then coalesce((p_updates->>'is_locked')::boolean, is_locked) else is_locked end,
   364	    is_archived = case when p_updates ? 'is_archived' then coalesce((p_updates->>'is_archived')::boolean, is_archived) else is_archived end,
   365	    updated_at = now()
   366	  where id = p_event_id and upper(org_id) = upper(v_org_id)
   367	  returning * into v_row;
   368	
   369	  if v_row.id is null then
   370	    return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'not_found', 'message', 'Event not found or access denied.');
   371	  end if;
   372	
   373	  return jsonb_build_object('ok', true, 'data', to_jsonb(v_row));
   374	end;
   375	$$;
   376	
   377	revoke all on function public.event_update(text, uuid, jsonb) from public;
   378	grant execute on function public.event_update(text, uuid, jsonb) to anon, authenticated;
   379	
   380	create or replace function public.event_delete(
   381	  p_token text,
   382	  p_event_id uuid
   383	)
   384	returns jsonb
   385	language plpgsql
   386	security definer
   387	set search_path = public, extensions
   388	as $$
   389	declare
   390	  v_org_id text;
   391	  v_locked boolean;
   392	begin
   393	  v_org_id := public._fj_verify_org_token(p_token, null);
   394	  if v_org_id is null then
   395	    return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'unauthorized', 'message', 'Session expired or invalid. Please sign in again.');
   396	  end if;
   397	
   398	  select is_locked into v_locked from public.events where id = p_event_id and upper(org_id) = upper(v_org_id);
   399	  if v_locked is true then
   400	    return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'ROSTER_LOCKED', 'message', 'ROSTER_LOCKED: Unlock event before deleting.');
   401	  end if;
   402	
   403	  delete from public.scores
   404	  where event_id = p_event_id
   405	    and exists (
   406	      select 1 from public.events e
   407	      where e.id = p_event_id and upper(e.org_id) = upper(v_org_id)
   408	    );
   409	
   410	  delete from public.events where id = p_event_id and upper(org_id) = upper(v_org_id);
   411	  return jsonb_build_object('ok', true);
   412	end;
   413	$$;
   414	
   415	revoke all on function public.event_delete(text, uuid) from public;
   416	grant execute on function public.event_delete(text, uuid) to anon, authenticated;
   417	
   418	-- 7. Judge Write RPCs
   419	create or replace function public.judge_upsert(
   420	  p_token text,
   421	  p_record jsonb
   422	)
   423	returns jsonb
   424	language plpgsql
   425	security definer
   426	set search_path = public, extensions
   427	as $$
   428	declare
   429	  v_org_id text;
   430	  v_id uuid;
   431	  v_existing_org text;
   432	  v_row public.judges;
   433	begin
   434	  v_org_id := public._fj_verify_org_token(p_token, p_record->>'org_id');
   435	  if v_org_id is null then
   436	    return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'unauthorized', 'message', 'Session expired or invalid. Please sign in again.');
   437	  end if;
   438	
   439	  v_id := coalesce(nullif(p_record->>'id', '')::uuid, gen_random_uuid());
   440	
   441	  select org_id into v_existing_org from public.judges where id = v_id;
   442	  if v_existing_org is not null and upper(v_existing_org) <> upper(v_org_id) then
   443	    return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'forbidden', 'message', 'Cannot overwrite judge from another organization.');
   444	  end if;
   445	
   446	  insert into public.judges (
   447	    id, org_id, name, judge_id, pin, assigned_events, current_session_token, created_at
   448	  ) values (
   449	    v_id,
   450	    v_org_id,
   451	    coalesce(nullif(btrim(p_record->>'name'), ''), 'Judge'),
   452	    p_record->>'judge_id',
   453	    coalesce(nullif(btrim(p_record->>'pin'), ''), '0000'),
   454	    case when jsonb_typeof(p_record->'assigned_events') = 'array' then array(select jsonb_array_elements_text(p_record->'assigned_events')) else '{}'::text[] end,
   455	    p_record->>'current_session_token',
   456	    coalesce(nullif(p_record->>'created_at', '')::timestamptz, now())
   457	  )
   458	  on conflict (id) do update set
   459	    name = excluded.name,
   460	    judge_id = excluded.judge_id,
   461	    pin = excluded.pin,
   462	    assigned_events = excluded.assigned_events,
   463	    current_session_token = excluded.current_session_token
   464	  returning * into v_row;
   465	
   466	  return jsonb_build_object('ok', true, 'data', to_jsonb(v_row));
   467	end;
   468	$$;
   469	
   470	revoke all on function public.judge_upsert(text, jsonb) from public;
   471	grant execute on function public.judge_upsert(text, jsonb) to anon, authenticated;

   475	  p_judge_id uuid,
   476	  p_updates jsonb
   477	)
   478	returns jsonb
   479	language plpgsql
   480	security definer
   481	set search_path = public, extensions

   483	declare
   484	  v_org_id text;

   490	  end if;

   495	    judge_id = case when p_updates ? 'judge_id' then p_updates->>'judge_id' else judge_id end,

   497	    assigned_events = case when p_updates ? 'assigned_events' then (case when jsonb_typeof(p_updates->'assigned_events') = 'array' then array(select jsonb_array_elements_text(p_updates->'assigned_events')) else '{}'::text[] end) else assigned_events end,
   498	    current_session_token = case when p_updates ? 'current_session_token' then p_updates->>'current_session_token' else current_session_token end

   538	-- 8. Participant Write RPCs
   539	create or replace function public.participant_upsert_batch(
   540	  p_token text,
   541	  p_records jsonb
   542	)
   543	returns jsonb
   544	language plpgsql
   545	security definer
   546	set search_path = public, extensions
   547	as $$
   548	declare
   549	  v_org_id text;
   550	  rec jsonb;
   551	  v_id uuid;
   552	  v_existing_org text;
   553	  v_row public.participants;
   554	  v_out jsonb := '[]'::jsonb;
   555	begin
   556	  v_org_id := public._fj_verify_org_token(p_token, null);
   557	  if v_org_id is null then
   558	    return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'unauthorized', 'message', 'Session expired or invalid. Please sign in again.');
   559	  end if;
   560	
   561	  for rec in select * from jsonb_array_elements(coalesce(p_records, '[]'::jsonb)) loop
   562	    if rec->>'org_id' is not null and upper(rec->>'org_id') <> upper(v_org_id) then
   563	      return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'forbidden', 'message', 'Cannot insert participants for another organization.');
   564	    end if;
   565	
   566	    v_id := coalesce(nullif(rec->>'id', '')::uuid, gen_random_uuid());
   567	    select org_id into v_existing_org from public.participants where id = v_id;
   568	    if v_existing_org is not null and upper(v_existing_org) <> upper(v_org_id) then
   569	      return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'forbidden', 'message', 'Cannot overwrite participant from another organization.');
   570	    end if;
   571	
   572	    insert into public.participants (
   573	      id, org_id, identity_id, sl_no, name, school, class_or_age, mobile_number, custom_values, created_at
   574	    ) values (
   575	      v_id,
   576	      v_org_id,
   577	      coalesce(nullif(btrim(rec->>'identity_id'), ''), 'P-' || substring(v_id::text from 1 for 6)),
   578	      rec->>'sl_no',
   579	      coalesce(nullif(btrim(rec->>'name'), ''), 'Participant'),
   580	      rec->>'school',
   581	      rec->>'class_or_age',
   582	      rec->>'mobile_number',
   583	      coalesce(rec->'custom_values', '{}'::jsonb),
   584	      coalesce(nullif(rec->>'created_at', '')::timestamptz, now())
   585	    )
   586	    on conflict (id) do update set
   587	      sl_no = excluded.sl_no,
   588	      name = excluded.name,
   589	      school = excluded.school,
   590	      class_or_age = excluded.class_or_age,
   591	      mobile_number = excluded.mobile_number,
   592	      custom_values = excluded.custom_values
   593	    returning * into v_row;
   594	
   595	    v_out := v_out || jsonb_build_array(to_jsonb(v_row));
   596	  end loop;
   597	
   598	  return jsonb_build_object('ok', true, 'data', v_out);
   599	end;
   600	$$;
   601	
   602	revoke all on function public.participant_upsert_batch(text, jsonb) from public;
   603	grant execute on function public.participant_upsert_batch(text, jsonb) to anon, authenticated;
   604	
   605	create or replace function public.participant_update(
   606	  p_token text,
   607	  p_participant_id uuid,
   608	  p_updates jsonb
   609	)
   610	returns jsonb
   611	language plpgsql
   612	security definer
   613	set search_path = public, extensions
   614	as $$
   615	declare
   616	  v_org_id text;
   617	  v_row public.participants;
   618	begin
   619	  v_org_id := public._fj_verify_org_token(p_token, null);
   620	  if v_org_id is null then
   621	    return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'unauthorized', 'message', 'Session expired or invalid. Please sign in again.');
   622	  end if;
   623	
   624	  update public.participants
   625	  set
   626	    sl_no = case when p_updates ? 'sl_no' then p_updates->>'sl_no' else sl_no end,
   627	    name = case when p_updates ? 'name' then coalesce(nullif(btrim(p_updates->>'name'), ''), name) else name end,
   628	    school = case when p_updates ? 'school' then p_updates->>'school' else school end,
   629	    class_or_age = case when p_updates ? 'class_or_age' then p_updates->>'class_or_age' else class_or_age end,
   630	    mobile_number = case when p_updates ? 'mobile_number' then p_updates->>'mobile_number' else mobile_number end,
   631	    custom_values = case when p_updates ? 'custom_values' then coalesce(p_updates->'custom_values', custom_values) else custom_values end
   632	  where id = p_participant_id and upper(org_id) = upper(v_org_id)
   633	  returning * into v_row;
   634	
   635	  if v_row.id is null then
   636	    return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'not_found', 'message', 'Participant not found or access denied.');
   637	  end if;
   638	
   639	  return jsonb_build_object('ok', true, 'data', to_jsonb(v_row));
   640	end;
   641	$$;
   642	
   643	revoke all on function public.participant_update(text, uuid, jsonb) from public;
   644	grant execute on function public.participant_update(text, uuid, jsonb) to anon, authenticated;
   645	
   646	create or replace function public.participant_delete(
   647	  p_token text,
   648	  p_participant_id uuid
   649	)
   650	returns jsonb
   651	language plpgsql
   652	security definer
   653	set search_path = public, extensions
   654	as $$
   655	declare
   656	  v_org_id text;
   657	begin
   658	  v_org_id := public._fj_verify_org_token(p_token, null);
   659	  if v_org_id is null then
   660	    return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'unauthorized', 'message', 'Session expired or invalid. Please sign in again.');
   661	  end if;
   662	
   663	  delete from public.participants where id = p_participant_id and upper(org_id) = upper(v_org_id);
   664	  return jsonb_build_object('ok', true);
   665	end;
   666	$$;
   667	
   668	revoke all on function public.participant_delete(text, uuid) from public;
   669	grant execute on function public.participant_delete(text, uuid) to anon, authenticated;
   670	
   671	-- 9. Score Write RPCs
   672	create or replace function public.score_upsert_batch(
   673	  p_token text,
   674	  p_records jsonb
   675	)
   676	returns jsonb
   677	language plpgsql
   678	security definer
   679	set search_path = public, extensions
   680	as $$
   681	declare
   682	  v_org_id text;
   683	  v_judge_auth jsonb;
   684	  v_judge_id uuid;
   685	  v_judge_org text;
   686	  rec jsonb;
   687	  v_id uuid;
   688	  v_event_id uuid;
   689	  v_rec_judge_id uuid;
   690	  v_participant_id uuid;
   691	  v_event_org text;
   692	  v_event_status text;
   693	  v_assigned_judges text[];
   694	  v_grace_enabled boolean;
   695	  v_existing public.scores;
   696	  v_row public.scores;
   697	  v_out jsonb := '[]'::jsonb;
   698	begin
   699	  v_org_id := public._fj_verify_org_token(p_token, null);
   700	  if v_org_id is null then
   701	    v_judge_auth := public._fj_verify_judge_token(p_token);
   702	    if not coalesce((v_judge_auth->>'ok')::boolean, false) then
   703	      return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'unauthorized', 'message', 'Session expired or invalid. Please sign in again.');
   704	    end if;
   705	    v_judge_id := (v_judge_auth->>'judge_id')::uuid;
   706	    v_judge_org := v_judge_auth->>'org_id';
   707	  end if;
   708	
   709	  for rec in select * from jsonb_array_elements(coalesce(p_records, '[]'::jsonb)) loop
   710	    v_id := coalesce(nullif(rec->>'id', '')::uuid, gen_random_uuid());
   711	    v_event_id := (rec->>'event_id')::uuid;
   712	    v_rec_judge_id := (rec->>'judge_id')::uuid;
   713	    v_participant_id := (rec->>'participant_id')::uuid;
   714	
   715	    select e.org_id, lower(coalesce(e.status, '')), coalesce(e.assigned_judges, '{}'::text[])
   716	    into v_event_org, v_event_status, v_assigned_judges
   717	    from public.events e
   718	    where e.id = v_event_id;
   719	
   720	    if v_event_org is null then
   721	      return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'not_found', 'message', 'Target event not found.');
   722	    end if;
   723	
   724	    if v_org_id is not null then
   725	      if upper(v_event_org) <> upper(v_org_id) then
   726	        return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'forbidden', 'message', 'Cannot write scores for another organization.');
   727	      end if;
   728	    else
   729	      if upper(v_event_org) <> upper(v_judge_org) or v_rec_judge_id <> v_judge_id then
   730	        return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'forbidden', 'message', 'Judge token does not match score owner or organization.');
   731	      end if;
   732	      if not (v_judge_id::text = any(v_assigned_judges) or v_event_status = 'active') then
   733	        return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'forbidden', 'message', 'Judge is not assigned to this event.');
   734	      end if;
   735	    end if;
   736	
   737	    select * into v_existing
   738	    from public.scores s
   739	    where s.id = v_id
   740	       or (s.event_id = v_event_id and s.judge_id = v_rec_judge_id and s.participant_id = v_participant_id)
   741	    limit 1;
   742	
   743	    if v_existing.id is not null and v_org_id is null then
   744	      select coalesce(o.grace_period_enabled, false) into v_grace_enabled
   745	      from public.organizations o
   746	      where upper(o.org_id) = upper(v_event_org);
   747	
   748	      if not coalesce(v_grace_enabled, false) or (now() - v_existing.created_at) > interval '5 minutes' then
   749	        return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'RLS Permission Denied', 'message', 'RLS Permission Denied: Scores are immutable and cannot be updated once inserted.');
   750	      end if;
   751	    end if;
   752	
   753	    if v_existing.id is not null then
   754	      update public.scores
   755	      set
   756	        score_payload = coalesce(rec->'score_payload', score_payload),
   757	        total_score = coalesce((rec->>'total_score')::numeric, total_score)
   758	      where id = v_existing.id
   759	      returning * into v_row;
   760	    else
   761	      insert into public.scores (
   762	        id, event_id, judge_id, participant_id, score_payload, total_score, created_at
   763	      ) values (
   764	        v_id,
   765	        v_event_id,
   766	        v_rec_judge_id,
   767	        v_participant_id,
   768	        coalesce(rec->'score_payload', '{}'::jsonb),
   769	        coalesce((rec->>'total_score')::numeric, 0),
   770	        now()
   771	      )
   772	      returning * into v_row;
   773	    end if;
   774	
   775	    v_out := v_out || jsonb_build_array(to_jsonb(v_row));
   776	  end loop;
   777	
   778	  return jsonb_build_object('ok', true, 'data', v_out);
   779	end;
   780	$$;
   781	
   782	revoke all on function public.score_upsert_batch(text, jsonb) from public;
   783	grant execute on function public.score_upsert_batch(text, jsonb) to anon, authenticated;
   784	
   785	create or replace function public.score_delete(
   786	  p_token text,
   787	  p_score_id uuid
   788	)
   789	returns jsonb
   790	language plpgsql
   791	security definer
   792	set search_path = public, extensions
   793	as $$
   794	declare
   795	  v_org_id text;
   796	begin
   797	  v_org_id := public._fj_verify_org_token(p_token, null);
   798	  if v_org_id is null then
   799	    return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'unauthorized', 'message', 'Session expired or invalid. Please sign in again.');
   800	  end if;
   801	
   802	  delete from public.scores s
   803	  using public.events e
   804	  where s.id = p_score_id
   805	    and s.event_id = e.id
   806	    and upper(e.org_id) = upper(v_org_id);
   807	
   808	  return jsonb_build_object('ok', true);
   809	end;
   810	$$;
   811	
   812	revoke all on function public.score_delete(text, uuid) from public;
   813	grant execute on function public.score_delete(text, uuid) to anon, authenticated;
   814	
   815	-- 10. Lock Down Direct Table Writes (SELECT-Only on Public REST API)
   816	alter table public.organizations enable row level security;
   817	alter table public.events enable row level security;
   818	alter table public.judges enable row level security;
   819	alter table public.participants enable row level security;
   820	alter table public.scores enable row level security;
   821	
   822	drop policy if exists "organizations_insert" on public.organizations;
   823	drop policy if exists "organizations_update" on public.organizations;
   824	drop policy if exists "organizations_delete" on public.organizations;
   825	revoke insert, update, delete on table public.organizations from anon, authenticated;
   826	
   827	drop policy if exists "Enable read/write for events" on public.events;
   828	drop policy if exists "events_select" on public.events;
   829	create policy "events_select" on public.events for select to public using (true);
   830	revoke insert, update, delete on table public.events from anon, authenticated;
   831	
   832	drop policy if exists "Enable read/write for judges" on public.judges;
   833	drop policy if exists "judges_select" on public.judges;
   834	create policy "judges_select" on public.judges for select to public using (true);
   835	revoke insert, update, delete on table public.judges from anon, authenticated;
   836	
   837	drop policy if exists "Enable read/write for participants" on public.participants;
   838	drop policy if exists "participants_select" on public.participants;
   839	create policy "participants_select" on public.participants for select to public using (true);
   840	revoke insert, update, delete on table public.participants from anon, authenticated;
   841	
   842	drop policy if exists "Enable insert for scores" on public.scores;
   843	drop policy if exists "Enable update for scores" on public.scores;
   844	drop policy if exists "Enable read for scores" on public.scores;
   845	drop policy if exists "scores_select" on public.scores;
   846	create policy "scores_select" on public.scores for select to public using (true);
   847	revoke insert, update, delete on table public.scores from anon, authenticated;

==================== FILE: sql/15.12-phase3-pii-lock.sql ====================
     1	-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
     2	-- EPIC 15.12 — PHASE 3: PII LOCK & JUDGE SECRETS HARDENING
     3	-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
     4	-- What this migration does:
     5	--   3.2 PARTICIPANT MOBILE NUMBER LOCK
     6	--     - Explicit column-level REVOKE SELECT (mobile_number) on public.participants
     7	--     - Updates read_participants to return explicit non-PII columns only
     8	--     - Adds organiser-only participants_list(p_token, p_org_id)
     9	--
    10	--   3.3 JUDGE PIN HASHING + ENCRYPTION + THROTTLE
    11	--     - Creates server-only _fj_internal_secrets (RLS ON, REVOKE ALL) for symmetric key
    12	--     - Creates public.judge_secrets (judge_id PK, org_id, pin_hash bcrypt, pin_encrypted, updated_at)
    13	--     - Creates public.judge_login_throttle (brute-force lockout: 5 fails -> 15 min lock)
    14	--     - Migrates existing judges.pin into judge_secrets, verifies 100% coverage,
    15	--       and clears plaintext pin & current_session_token on public.judges
    16	--     - Explicit column-level REVOKE SELECT (pin, current_session_token) on public.judges
    17	--     - Adds organiser-only judges_list_with_pins(p_token, p_org_id)
    18	--     - Updates read_org_roster_credentials, judge_upsert, judge_update, judge_login, org_restore
    19	--     - Updates fj_health() with Phase 3 secret tables & column security status
    20	--
    21	-- Ends with a single read-only verification query returning ONE jsonb cell.
    22	-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    23	
    24	create extension if not exists pgcrypto with schema extensions;
    25	
    26	-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    27	-- 1. SERVER-ONLY INTERNAL KEY STORE & JUDGE SECRETS / THROTTLE TABLES
    28	-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    29	create table if not exists public._fj_internal_secrets (
    30	  key text primary key,
    31	  value text not null,
    32	  created_at timestamptz not null default now()
    33	);
    34	
    35	alter table public._fj_internal_secrets enable row level security;
    36	revoke all on table public._fj_internal_secrets from anon, authenticated, public;
    37	
    38	insert into public._fj_internal_secrets (key, value)
    39	values ('judge_pin_key', encode(extensions.gen_random_bytes(32), 'hex'))
    40	on conflict (key) do nothing;
    41	
    42	create table if not exists public.judge_secrets (
    43	  judge_id uuid primary key references public.judges(id) on delete cascade,
    44	  org_id text not null references public.organizations(org_id) on delete cascade,
    45	  pin_hash text not null,
    46	  pin_encrypted text not null,
    47	  updated_at timestamptz not null default now()
    48	);
    49	
    50	create index if not exists idx_judge_secrets_org_id on public.judge_secrets(org_id);
    51	alter table public.judge_secrets enable row level security;
    52	revoke all on table public.judge_secrets from anon, authenticated, public;
    53	
    54	create table if not exists public.judge_login_throttle (
    55	  key text primary key,
    56	  fails int not null default 0,
    57	  first_fail_at timestamptz,
    58	  locked_until timestamptz
    59	);
    60	
    61	alter table public.judge_login_throttle enable row level security;
    62	revoke all on table public.judge_login_throttle from anon, authenticated, public;
    63	
    64	-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    65	-- 2. INTERNAL CRYPTO HELPERS (Never exposed to anon/authenticated/public)
    66	-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    67	create or replace function public._fj_get_judge_pin_key()
    68	returns text
    69	language plpgsql
    70	security definer
    71	set search_path = public, extensions
    72	as $$
    73	declare
    74	  v_key text;
    75	begin
    76	  select value into v_key
    77	  from public._fj_internal_secrets
    78	  where key = 'judge_pin_key'
    79	  limit 1;
    80	
    81	  if v_key is null or v_key = '' then
    82	    v_key := encode(extensions.gen_random_bytes(32), 'hex');
    83	    insert into public._fj_internal_secrets (key, value)
    84	    values ('judge_pin_key', v_key)
    85	    on conflict (key) do update set value = public._fj_internal_secrets.value
    86	    returning value into v_key;
    87	  end if;
    88	
    89	  return v_key;
    90	end;
    91	$$;
    92	
    93	revoke all on function public._fj_get_judge_pin_key() from public, anon, authenticated;
    94	
    95	create or replace function public._fj_upsert_judge_secret(
    96	  p_judge_id uuid,
    97	  p_org_id text,
    98	  p_pin text
    99	)
   100	returns void
   101	language plpgsql
   102	security definer
   103	set search_path = public, extensions
   104	as $$
   105	declare
   106	  v_pin text;
   107	  v_key text;
   108	begin
   109	  v_pin := coalesce(nullif(btrim(coalesce(p_pin, '')), ''), '0000');
   110	  v_key := public._fj_get_judge_pin_key();
   111	
   112	  insert into public.judge_secrets (judge_id, org_id, pin_hash, pin_encrypted, updated_at)
   113	  values (
   114	    p_judge_id,
   115	    upper(btrim(p_org_id)),
   116	    extensions.crypt(v_pin, extensions.gen_salt('bf', 10)),
   117	    encode(extensions.pgp_sym_encrypt(v_pin, v_key), 'base64'),
   118	    now()
   119	  )
   120	  on conflict (judge_id) do update set
   121	    org_id = excluded.org_id,
   122	    pin_hash = excluded.pin_hash,
   123	    pin_encrypted = excluded.pin_encrypted,
   124	    updated_at = now();
   125	end;
   126	$$;
   127	
   128	revoke all on function public._fj_upsert_judge_secret(uuid, text, text) from public, anon, authenticated;
   129	
   130	create or replace function public._fj_decrypt_judge_pin(p_pin_encrypted text)
   131	returns text
   132	language plpgsql
   133	security definer
   134	set search_path = public, extensions
   135	as $$
   136	declare
   137	  v_key text;
   138	begin
   139	  if p_pin_encrypted is null or btrim(p_pin_encrypted) = '' then
   140	    return null;
   141	  end if;
   142	  v_key := public._fj_get_judge_pin_key();
   143	  return extensions.pgp_sym_decrypt(decode(p_pin_encrypted, 'base64'), v_key);
   144	exception
   145	  when others then
   146	    return null;
   147	end;
   148	$$;
   149	
   150	revoke all on function public._fj_decrypt_judge_pin(text) from public, anon, authenticated;
   151	
   152	-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   153	-- 3. MIGRATE EXISTING JUDGE PINS -> judge_secrets & CLEAR PLAINTEXT COLUMNS
   154	-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   155	do $$
   156	declare
   157	  v_key text;
   158	  v_missing int;
   159	begin
   160	  v_key := public._fj_get_judge_pin_key();
   161	
   162	  insert into public.judge_secrets (judge_id, org_id, pin_hash, pin_encrypted, updated_at)
   163	  select
   164	    j.id,
   165	    upper(btrim(j.org_id)),
   166	    extensions.crypt(coalesce(nullif(btrim(j.pin), ''), '0000'), extensions.gen_salt('bf', 10)),
   167	    encode(extensions.pgp_sym_encrypt(coalesce(nullif(btrim(j.pin), ''), '0000'), v_key), 'base64'),
   168	    now()
   169	  from public.judges j
   170	  on conflict (judge_id) do nothing;
   171	
   172	  select count(*) into v_missing
   173	  from public.judges j
   174	  where not exists (select 1 from public.judge_secrets js where js.judge_id = j.id);
   175	
   176	  if v_missing > 0 then
   177	    raise exception 'Phase 3 migration aborted: % judge row(s) missing judge_secrets entry.', v_missing;
   178	  end if;
   179	
   180	  -- Wipe plaintext pin & current_session_token on public.judges
   181	  update public.judges
   182	  set pin = '',
   183	      current_session_token = null
   184	  where coalesce(pin, '') <> ''
   185	     or current_session_token is not null;
   186	end $$;
   187	
   188	-- Defense-in-depth: explicit column-level revokes for PII / secret columns
   189	revoke select (mobile_number) on table public.participants from anon, authenticated, public;
   190	revoke select (pin, current_session_token) on table public.judges from anon, authenticated, public;
   191	
   192	-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   193	-- 4. PARTICIPANT READS: NON-PII read_participants + ORGANISER participants_list
   194	-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   195	create or replace function public.read_participants(
   196	  p_token text
   197	)
   198	returns jsonb
   199	language plpgsql
   200	security definer
   201	set search_path = public, extensions
   202	as $$
   203	declare
   204	  v_auth jsonb;
   205	  v_org_id text;
   206	  v_role text;
   207	  v_blind boolean := false;
   208	  v_out jsonb;
   209	begin
   210	  v_auth := public._fj_verify_read_token(p_token, null);
   211	  if not coalesce((v_auth->>'ok')::boolean, false) then
   212	    return jsonb_build_object(
   213	      'ok', false,
   214	      'error', coalesce(v_auth->>'error', 'unauthorized'),
   215	      'message', 'Session expired or invalid. Please sign in again.'
   216	    );
   217	  end if;
   218	
   219	  v_org_id := v_auth->>'org_id';
   220	  v_role := v_auth->>'role';
   221	
   222	  if v_role = 'judge' then
   223	    select coalesce(o.blind_judging_enabled, false)
   224	    into v_blind
   225	    from public.organizations o
   226	    where upper(o.org_id) = upper(v_org_id)
   227	    limit 1;
   228	  end if;
   229	
   230	  -- Explicit safe column list (mobile_number is never read or returned here)
   231	  select coalesce(
   232	    jsonb_agg(
   233	      case
   234	        when v_role = 'judge' and v_blind then
   235	          jsonb_build_object(
   236	            'id', p.id,
   237	            'org_id', p.org_id,
   238	            'identity_id', p.identity_id,
   239	            'sl_no', p.sl_no,
   240	            'name', 'Anonymous',
   241	            'school', '',
   242	            'class_or_age', null,
   243	            'custom_values', '{}'::jsonb,
   244	            'created_at', p.created_at
   245	          )
   246	        else
   247	          jsonb_build_object(
   248	            'id', p.id,
   249	            'org_id', p.org_id,
   250	            'identity_id', p.identity_id,
   251	            'sl_no', p.sl_no,
   252	            'name', p.name,
   253	            'school', p.school,
   254	            'class_or_age', p.class_or_age,
   255	            'custom_values', coalesce(p.custom_values, '{}'::jsonb),
   256	            'created_at', p.created_at
   257	          )
   258	      end
   259	      order by p.created_at asc
   260	    ),
   261	    '[]'::jsonb
   262	  )
   263	  into v_out
   264	  from public.participants p
   265	  where upper(p.org_id) = upper(v_org_id);
   266	
   267	  return jsonb_build_object('ok', true, 'data', v_out);
   268	end;
   269	$$;
   270	
   271	revoke all on function public.read_participants(text) from public;
   272	grant execute on function public.read_participants(text) to anon, authenticated;
   273	
   274	create or replace function public.participants_list(
   275	  p_token text,
   276	  p_org_id text default null
   277	)
   278	returns jsonb
   279	language plpgsql
   280	security definer
   281	set search_path = public, extensions
   282	as $$
   283	declare
   284	  v_org_id text;
   285	  v_out jsonb;
   286	begin
   287	  v_org_id := public._fj_verify_org_token(p_token, p_org_id);
   288	  if v_org_id is null then
   289	    return jsonb_build_object(
   290	      'ok', false,
   291	      'error', 'unauthorized',
   292	      'message', 'Organiser session expired or invalid. Please sign in again.'
   293	    );
   294	  end if;
   295	
   296	  select coalesce(
   297	    jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(p) order by p.created_at asc),
   298	    '[]'::jsonb
   299	  )
   300	  into v_out
   301	  from public.participants p
   302	  where upper(p.org_id) = upper(v_org_id);
   303	
   304	  return jsonb_build_object('ok', true, 'data', v_out);
   305	end;
   306	$$;
   307	
   308	revoke all on function public.participants_list(text, text) from public;
   309	grant execute on function public.participants_list(text, text) to anon, authenticated;
   310	
   311	-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   312	-- 5. JUDGE READS: NON-SECRET read_judges + ORGANISER judges_list_with_pins
   313	-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   314	create or replace function public.read_judges(
   315	  p_token text,
   316	  p_judge_id uuid default null
   317	)
   318	returns jsonb
   319	language plpgsql
   320	security definer
   321	set search_path = public, extensions
   322	as $$
   323	declare
   324	  v_auth jsonb;
   325	  v_org_id text;
   326	  v_out jsonb;
   327	begin
   328	  v_auth := public._fj_verify_read_token(p_token, null);
   329	  if not coalesce((v_auth->>'ok')::boolean, false) then
   330	    return jsonb_build_object(
   331	      'ok', false,
   332	      'error', coalesce(v_auth->>'error', 'unauthorized'),
   333	      'message', 'Session expired or invalid. Please sign in again.'
   334	    );
   335	  end if;
   336	
   337	  v_org_id := v_auth->>'org_id';
   338	
   339	  select coalesce(
   340	    jsonb_agg(
   341	      jsonb_build_object(
   342	        'id', j.id,
   343	        'org_id', j.org_id,
   344	        'name', j.name,
   345	        'judge_id', j.judge_id,
   346	        'assigned_events', coalesce(j.assigned_events, '{}'::text[]),
   347	        'created_at', j.created_at
   348	      )
   349	      order by j.created_at desc
   350	    ),
   351	    '[]'::jsonb
   352	  )
   353	  into v_out
   354	  from public.judges j
   355	  where upper(j.org_id) = upper(v_org_id)
   356	    and (p_judge_id is null or j.id = p_judge_id);
   357	
   358	  return jsonb_build_object('ok', true, 'data', v_out);
   359	end;
   360	$$;
   361	
   362	revoke all on function public.read_judges(text, uuid) from public;
   363	grant execute on function public.read_judges(text, uuid) to anon, authenticated;
   364	
   365	create or replace function public.judges_list_with_pins(
   366	  p_token text,
   367	  p_org_id text default null
   368	)
   369	returns jsonb
   370	language plpgsql
   371	security definer
   372	set search_path = public, extensions
   373	as $$
   374	declare
   375	  v_org_id text;
   376	  v_out jsonb;
   377	begin
   378	  v_org_id := public._fj_verify_org_token(p_token, p_org_id);
   379	  if v_org_id is null then
   380	    return jsonb_build_object(
   381	      'ok', false,
   382	      'error', 'unauthorized',
   383	      'message', 'Organiser session expired or invalid. Please sign in again.'
   384	    );
   385	  end if;
   386	
   387	  select coalesce(
   388	    jsonb_agg(
   389	      (to_jsonb(j) - 'pin' - 'current_session_token') || jsonb_build_object(
   390	        'pin', coalesce(public._fj_decrypt_judge_pin(js.pin_encrypted), nullif(btrim(j.pin), ''), ''),
   391	        'judge_pin', coalesce(public._fj_decrypt_judge_pin(js.pin_encrypted), nullif(btrim(j.pin), ''), '')
   392	      )
   393	      order by j.created_at desc
   394	    ),
   395	    '[]'::jsonb
   396	  )
   397	  into v_out
   398	  from public.judges j
   399	  left join public.judge_secrets js on js.judge_id = j.id
   400	  where upper(j.org_id) = upper(v_org_id);
   401	
   402	  return jsonb_build_object('ok', true, 'data', v_out);
   403	end;
   404	$$;
   405	
   406	revoke all on function public.judges_list_with_pins(text, text) from public;
   407	grant execute on function public.judges_list_with_pins(text, text) to anon, authenticated;
   408	
   409	create or replace function public.read_org_roster_credentials(
   410	  p_token text
   411	)
   412	returns jsonb
   413	language plpgsql
   414	security definer
   415	set search_path = public, extensions
   416	as $$
   417	declare
   418	  v_org_id text;
   419	  v_judges jsonb;
   420	  v_participants jsonb;
   421	begin
   422	  v_org_id := public._fj_verify_org_token(p_token, null);
   423	  if v_org_id is null then
   424	    return jsonb_build_object(
   425	      'ok', false,
   426	      'error', 'unauthorized',
   427	      'message', 'Organiser session expired or invalid. Please sign in again.'
   428	    );
   429	  end if;
   430	
   431	  select coalesce(
   432	    jsonb_agg(
   433	      (to_jsonb(j) - 'pin' - 'current_session_token') || jsonb_build_object(
   434	        'pin', coalesce(public._fj_decrypt_judge_pin(js.pin_encrypted), nullif(btrim(j.pin), ''), ''),
   435	        'judge_pin', coalesce(public._fj_decrypt_judge_pin(js.pin_encrypted), nullif(btrim(j.pin), ''), '')
   436	      )
   437	      order by j.created_at desc
   438	    ),
   439	    '[]'::jsonb
   440	  )
   441	  into v_judges
   442	  from public.judges j
   443	  left join public.judge_secrets js on js.judge_id = j.id
   444	  where upper(j.org_id) = upper(v_org_id);
   445	
   446	  select coalesce(
   447	    jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(p) order by p.created_at asc),
   448	    '[]'::jsonb
   449	  )
   450	  into v_participants
   451	  from public.participants p
   452	  where upper(p.org_id) = upper(v_org_id);
   453	
   454	  return jsonb_build_object(
   455	    'ok', true,
   456	    'judges', v_judges,
   457	    'participants', v_participants
   458	  );
   459	end;
   460	$$;
   461	
   462	revoke all on function public.read_org_roster_credentials(text) from public;
   463	grant execute on function public.read_org_roster_credentials(text) to anon, authenticated;
   464	
   465	-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   466	-- 6. JUDGE WRITE RPCs (Store PIN only in judge_secrets, keep judges clean)
   467	-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   468	create or replace function public.judge_upsert(
   469	  p_token text,
   470	  p_record jsonb
   471	)
   472	returns jsonb
   473	language plpgsql
   474	security definer
   475	set search_path = public, extensions
   476	as $$
   477	declare
   478	  v_org_id text;
   479	  v_id uuid;
   480	  v_existing_org text;
   481	  v_pin text;
   482	  v_row public.judges;
   483	begin
   484	  v_org_id := public._fj_verify_org_token(p_token, p_record->>'org_id');
   485	  if v_org_id is null then
   486	    return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'unauthorized', 'message', 'Session expired or invalid. Please sign in again.');
   487	  end if;
   488	
   489	  v_id := coalesce(nullif(p_record->>'id', '')::uuid, gen_random_uuid());
   490	
   491	  select org_id into v_existing_org from public.judges where id = v_id;
   492	  if v_existing_org is not null and upper(v_existing_org) <> upper(v_org_id) then
   493	    return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'forbidden', 'message', 'Cannot overwrite judge from another organization.');
   494	  end if;
   495	
   496	  v_pin := coalesce(nullif(btrim(coalesce(p_record->>'pin', p_record->>'judge_pin', '')), ''), '0000');
   497	
   498	  insert into public.judges (
   499	    id, org_id, name, judge_id, pin, assigned_events, current_session_token, created_at
   500	  ) values (
   501	    v_id,
   502	    v_org_id,
   503	    coalesce(nullif(btrim(p_record->>'name'), ''), 'Judge'),
   504	    p_record->>'judge_id',
   505	    '',
   506	    case when jsonb_typeof(p_record->'assigned_events') = 'array' then array(select jsonb_array_elements_text(p_record->'assigned_events')) else '{}'::text[] end,
   507	    null,
   508	    coalesce(nullif(p_record->>'created_at', '')::timestamptz, now())
   509	  )
   510	  on conflict (id) do update set
   511	    name = excluded.name,
   512	    judge_id = excluded.judge_id,
   513	    pin = '',
   514	    assigned_events = excluded.assigned_events,
   515	    current_session_token = null
   516	  returning * into v_row;
   517	
   518	  perform public._fj_upsert_judge_secret(v_row.id, v_org_id, v_pin);
   519	
   520	  return jsonb_build_object(
   521	    'ok', true,
   522	    'data', (to_jsonb(v_row) - 'pin' - 'current_session_token') || jsonb_build_object('pin', v_pin, 'judge_pin', v_pin)
   523	  );
   524	end;
   525	$$;
   526	
   527	revoke all on function public.judge_upsert(text, jsonb) from public;
   528	grant execute on function public.judge_upsert(text, jsonb) to anon, authenticated;
   529	
   530	create or replace function public.judge_update(
   531	  p_token text,
   532	  p_judge_id uuid,
   533	  p_updates jsonb
   534	)
   535	returns jsonb
   536	language plpgsql
   537	security definer
   538	set search_path = public, extensions
   539	as $$
   540	declare
   541	  v_org_id text;
   542	  v_row public.judges;
   543	  v_new_pin text;
   544	  v_cur_pin text;
   545	begin
   546	  v_org_id := public._fj_verify_org_token(p_token, null);
   547	  if v_org_id is null then
   548	    return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'unauthorized', 'message', 'Session expired or invalid. Please sign in again.');
   549	  end if;
   550	
   551	  update public.judges
   552	  set
   553	    name = case when p_updates ? 'name' then coalesce(nullif(btrim(p_updates->>'name'), ''), name) else name end,
   554	    judge_id = case when p_updates ? 'judge_id' then p_updates->>'judge_id' else judge_id end,
   555	    pin = '',
   556	    assigned_events = case when p_updates ? 'assigned_events' then (case when jsonb_typeof(p_updates->'assigned_events') = 'array' then array(select jsonb_array_elements_text(p_updates->'assigned_events')) else '{}'::text[] end) else assigned_events end,
   557	    current_session_token = null
   558	  where id = p_judge_id and upper(org_id) = upper(v_org_id)
   559	  returning * into v_row;
   560	
   561	  if v_row.id is null then
   562	    return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'not_found', 'message', 'Judge not found or access denied.');
   563	  end if;
   564	
   565	  v_new_pin := nullif(btrim(coalesce(p_updates->>'pin', p_updates->>'judge_pin', '')), '');
   566	  if v_new_pin is not null then
   567	    perform public._fj_upsert_judge_secret(v_row.id, v_org_id, v_new_pin);
   568	    v_cur_pin := v_new_pin;
   569	  else
   570	    select public._fj_decrypt_judge_pin(js.pin_encrypted)
   571	    into v_cur_pin
   572	    from public.judge_secrets js
   573	    where js.judge_id = v_row.id;
   574	  end if;
   575	
   576	  return jsonb_build_object(
   577	    'ok', true,
   578	    'data', (to_jsonb(v_row) - 'pin' - 'current_session_token') || jsonb_build_object(
   579	      'pin', coalesce(v_cur_pin, ''),
   580	      'judge_pin', coalesce(v_cur_pin, '')
   581	    )
   582	  );
   583	end;
   584	$$;
   585	
   586	revoke all on function public.judge_update(text, uuid, jsonb) from public;
   587	grant execute on function public.judge_update(text, uuid, jsonb) to anon, authenticated;
   588	
   589	-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   590	-- 7. HARDENED judge_login (Verify judge_secrets bcrypt + throttle + zero PIN/token leak)
   591	-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   592	create or replace function public.judge_login(
   593	  p_org_id text default null,
   594	  p_judge_id text default null,
   595	  p_pin text default null,
   596	  p_event_id text default null
   597	)
