   598	returns jsonb
   599	language plpgsql
   600	security definer
   601	set search_path = public, extensions
   602	as $$
   603	declare
   604	  clean_org_id text;
   605	  clean_judge_id text;
   606	  clean_pin text;
   607	  clean_event_id text;
   608	  v_tkey text;
   609	  v_locked_until timestamptz;
   610	  v_fails int;
   611	  v_rec record;
   612	  v_judge public.judges;
   613	  v_org_row public.organizations;
   614	  v_token text;
   615	  v_events jsonb;
   616	  v_org jsonb;
   617	begin
   618	  clean_org_id := upper(btrim(coalesce(p_org_id, '')));
   619	  clean_judge_id := btrim(coalesce(p_judge_id, ''));
   620	  clean_pin := btrim(coalesce(p_pin, ''));
   621	  clean_event_id := btrim(coalesce(p_event_id, ''));
   622	
   623	  if clean_pin = '' then
   624	    return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'invalid_credentials', 'reason', 'invalid_credentials');
   625	  end if;
   626	
   627	  v_tkey := coalesce(nullif(clean_org_id, ''), '*') || ':' || coalesce(nullif(lower(clean_judge_id), ''), '*');
   628	
   629	  select locked_until into v_locked_until
   630	  from public.judge_login_throttle
   631	  where key = v_tkey;
   632	
   633	  if v_locked_until is not null and v_locked_until > now() then
   634	    return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'too_many_attempts', 'reason', 'too_many_attempts');
   635	  end if;
   636	
   637	  for v_rec in
   638	    select j.id, js.pin_hash, coalesce((to_jsonb(j)->>'is_active')::boolean, true) as is_active
   639	    from public.judges j
   640	    join public.judge_secrets js on js.judge_id = j.id
   641	    where (clean_org_id = '' or upper(j.org_id) = clean_org_id)
   642	      and (
   643	        clean_judge_id = ''
   644	        or lower(j.id::text) = lower(clean_judge_id)
   645	        or lower(coalesce(j.judge_id, '')) = lower(clean_judge_id)
   646	        or lower(j.name) = lower(clean_judge_id)
   647	      )
   648	      and (
   649	        clean_event_id = ''
   650	        or clean_event_id = any(coalesce(j.assigned_events, '{}'::text[]))
   651	        or exists (
   652	          select 1 from public.events e
   653	          where e.id::text = clean_event_id
   654	            and j.id::text = any(coalesce(e.assigned_judges, '{}'::text[]))
   655	        )
   656	      )
   657	    order by j.created_at desc
   658	    limit 100
   659	  loop
   660	    if v_rec.is_active and v_rec.pin_hash is not null and extensions.crypt(clean_pin, v_rec.pin_hash) = v_rec.pin_hash then
   661	      select * into v_judge from public.judges where id = v_rec.id;
   662	      exit;
   663	    end if;
   664	  end loop;
   665	
   666	  if v_judge.id is null then
   667	    insert into public.judge_login_throttle (key, fails, first_fail_at, locked_until)
   668	    values (v_tkey, 1, now(), null)
   669	    on conflict (key) do update set
   670	      fails = case
   671	        when public.judge_login_throttle.first_fail_at < now() - interval '15 minutes' then 1
   672	        else public.judge_login_throttle.fails + 1
   673	      end,
   674	      first_fail_at = case
   675	        when public.judge_login_throttle.first_fail_at < now() - interval '15 minutes' then now()
   676	        else public.judge_login_throttle.first_fail_at
   677	      end,
   678	      locked_until = case
   679	        when (case when public.judge_login_throttle.first_fail_at < now() - interval '15 minutes' then 1 else public.judge_login_throttle.fails + 1 end) >= 5
   680	        then now() + interval '15 minutes'
   681	        else null
   682	      end
   683	    returning fails into v_fails;
   684	
   685	    if random() < 0.02 then
   686	      delete from public.judge_login_throttle
   687	      where first_fail_at < now() - interval '1 day'
   688	        and (locked_until is null or locked_until < now());
   689	    end if;
   690	
   691	    return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'invalid_credentials', 'reason', 'invalid_credentials');
   692	  end if;
   693	
   694	  select * into v_org_row
   695	  from public.organizations o
   696	  where upper(o.org_id) = upper(v_judge.org_id)
   697	  limit 1;
   698	
   699	  if v_org_row.deleted_at is not null then
   700	    return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'org_deleted', 'reason', 'org_deleted');
   701	  end if;
   702	
   703	  if coalesce(v_org_row.suspended, false) then
   704	    return jsonb_build_object(
   705	      'ok', false,
   706	      'error', 'org_suspended',
   707	      'reason', 'org_suspended',
   708	      'detail', coalesce(v_org_row.suspended_reason, '')
   709	    );
   710	  end if;
   711	
   712	  delete from public.judge_login_throttle where key = v_tkey;
   713	  delete from public.judge_sessions where judge_id = v_judge.id and expires_at <= now();
   714	
   715	  v_token := encode(extensions.gen_random_bytes(32), 'hex');
   716	  insert into public.judge_sessions (token, judge_id, org_id, created_at, expires_at)
   717	  values (v_token, v_judge.id, v_judge.org_id, now(), now() + interval '30 days');
   718	
   719	  select coalesce(jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(e) order by e.created_at desc), '[]'::jsonb)
   720	  into v_events
   721	  from public.events e
   722	  where upper(e.org_id) = upper(v_judge.org_id)
   723	    and (
   724	      v_judge.id::text = any(coalesce(e.assigned_judges, '{}'::text[]))
   725	      or e.id::text = any(coalesce(v_judge.assigned_events, '{}'::text[]))
   726	      or lower(coalesce(e.status, '')) = 'active'
   727	    );
   728	
   729	  v_org := to_jsonb(v_org_row) - 'password';
   730	
   731	  -- Never return plaintext pin or current_session_token to judge devices
   732	  return jsonb_build_object(
   733	    'ok', true,
   734	    'judge', to_jsonb(v_judge) - 'pin' - 'current_session_token',
   735	    'assigned_events', v_events,
   736	    'org', v_org,
   737	    'session_token', v_token
   738	  );
   739	end;
   740	$$;
   741	
   742	revoke all on function public.judge_login(text, text, text, text) from public;
   743	grant execute on function public.judge_login(text, text, text, text) to anon, authenticated;
   744	
   745	-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   746	-- 8. UPDATE org_restore TO STORE RESTORED JUDGE PINS IN judge_secrets
   747	-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   748	drop function if exists public.org_restore(text, text, jsonb, boolean);
   749	
   750	create or replace function public.org_restore(
   751	  p_org_id text,
   752	  p_password text,
   753	  p_mode text,
   754	  p_payload jsonb,
   755	  p_dry_run boolean default false
   756	)
   757	returns jsonb
   758	language plpgsql
   759	security definer
   760	set search_path = public, extensions
   761	as $$
   762	declare
   763	  clean_org_id text;
   764	  v_mode text;
   765	  v_auth jsonb;
   766	  v_events jsonb;
   767	  v_judges jsonb;
   768	  v_participants jsonb;
   769	  v_scores jsonb;
   770	
   771	  c_events_created int := 0;
   772	  c_events_updated int := 0;
   773	  c_events_protected int := 0;
   774	  c_judges_created int := 0;
   775	  c_judges_updated int := 0;
   776	  c_participants_created int := 0;
   777	  c_participants_updated int := 0;
   778	  c_scores_created int := 0;
   779	  c_scores_updated int := 0;
   780	
   781	  rec jsonb;
   782	  v_id uuid;
   783	  v_pin text;
   784	  v_exists boolean;
   785	  v_locked boolean;
   786	  locked_event_ids uuid[];
   787	begin
   788	  clean_org_id := upper(btrim(coalesce(p_org_id, '')));
   789	  v_mode := lower(btrim(coalesce(p_mode, 'merge')));
   790	
   791	  if clean_org_id = '' then
   792	    return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'invalid_input', 'reason', 'invalid_input', 'message', 'Invalid organization ID');
   793	  end if;
   794	
   795	  v_auth := public._fj_verify_org(clean_org_id, p_password);
   796	  if not (v_auth->>'ok')::boolean then
   797	    return v_auth;
   798	  end if;
   799	
   800	  if v_mode not in ('replace', 'add', 'merge') then
   801	    return jsonb_build_object('ok', false, 'error', 'invalid_mode', 'reason', 'invalid_mode', 'message', 'Invalid restore mode. Expected replace, add, or merge.');
   802	  end if;
   803	
   804	  select coalesce(array_agg(id), '{}'::uuid[])
   805	  into locked_event_ids
   806	  from public.events
   807	  where org_id = clean_org_id and is_locked = true;
   808	
   809	  if v_mode = 'replace' and cardinality(locked_event_ids) > 0 then
   810	    return jsonb_build_object(
   811	      'ok', false,
   812	      'error', 'replace_blocked_locked_events',
   813	      'reason', 'replace_blocked_locked_events',
   814	      'message', 'Cannot replace competition: this organisation has ' || cardinality(locked_event_ids) || ' locked event(s). Locked events cannot be deleted. Use Merge mode instead.'
   815	    );
   816	  end if;
   817	
   818	  v_events := coalesce(p_payload->'events', '[]'::jsonb);
   819	  v_judges := coalesce(p_payload->'judges', '[]'::jsonb);
   820	  v_participants := coalesce(p_payload->'participants', '[]'::jsonb);
   821	  v_scores := coalesce(p_payload->'scores', '[]'::jsonb);
   822	
   823	  if p_dry_run then
   824	    for rec in select * from jsonb_array_elements(v_events) loop
   825	      v_id := (rec->>'id')::uuid;
   826	      select exists(select 1 from public.events where id = v_id),
   827	             coalesce((select is_locked from public.events where id = v_id), false)
   828	      into v_exists, v_locked;
   829	
   830	      if v_exists and v_locked then
   831	        c_events_protected := c_events_protected + 1;
   832	      elsif v_exists then
   833	        c_events_updated := c_events_updated + 1;
   834	      else
   835	        c_events_created := c_events_created + 1;
   836	      end if;
   837	    end loop;
   838	
   839	    for rec in select * from jsonb_array_elements(v_judges) loop
   840	      v_id := (rec->>'id')::uuid;
   841	      if exists(select 1 from public.judges where id = v_id) then
   842	        c_judges_updated := c_judges_updated + 1;
   843	      else
   844	        c_judges_created := c_judges_created + 1;
   845	      end if;
   846	    end loop;
   847	
   848	    for rec in select * from jsonb_array_elements(v_participants) loop
   849	      v_id := (rec->>'id')::uuid;
   850	      if exists(select 1 from public.participants where id = v_id) then
   851	        c_participants_updated := c_participants_updated + 1;
   852	      else
   853	        c_participants_created := c_participants_created + 1;
   854	      end if;
   855	    end loop;
   856	
   857	    for rec in select * from jsonb_array_elements(v_scores) loop
   858	      v_id := (rec->>'id')::uuid;
   859	      if (rec->>'event_id')::uuid = any(locked_event_ids) then
   860	        continue;
   861	      end if;
   862	      if exists(select 1 from public.scores where id = v_id) then
   863	        c_scores_updated := c_scores_updated + 1;
   864	      else
   865	        c_scores_created := c_scores_created + 1;
   866	      end if;
   867	    end loop;
   868	
   869	    return jsonb_build_object(
   870	      'ok', true,
   871	      'dry_run', true,
   872	      'mode', v_mode,
   873	      'summary', jsonb_build_object(
   874	        'events_created', c_events_created,
   875	        'events_updated', c_events_updated,
   876	        'events_protected', c_events_protected,
   877	        'judges_created', c_judges_created,
   878	        'judges_updated', c_judges_updated,
   879	        'participants_created', c_participants_created,
   880	        'participants_updated', c_participants_updated,
   881	        'scores_created', c_scores_created,
   882	        'scores_updated', c_scores_updated
   883	      )
   884	    );
   885	  end if;
   886	
   887	  if v_mode = 'replace' then
   888	    delete from public.scores
   889	    where event_id in (
   890	      select id from public.events where org_id = clean_org_id and is_locked = false
   891	    );
   892	    delete from public.events
   893	    where org_id = clean_org_id and is_locked = false;
   894	    delete from public.judges where org_id = clean_org_id;
   895	    delete from public.participants where org_id = clean_org_id;
   896	  end if;
   897	
   898	  for rec in select * from jsonb_array_elements(v_judges) loop
   899	    v_id := (rec->>'id')::uuid;
   900	    if v_mode = 'add' and exists(select 1 from public.judges where id = v_id) then
   901	      continue;
   902	    end if;
   903	
   904	    v_pin := coalesce(nullif(btrim(coalesce(rec->>'pin', rec->>'judge_pin', '')), ''), '0000');
   905	
   906	    insert into public.judges (
   907	      id, org_id, name, judge_id, pin, assigned_events, current_session_token, created_at
   908	    ) values (
   909	      v_id, clean_org_id, coalesce(rec->>'name', 'Judge'), rec->>'judge_id',
   910	      '',
   911	      case when jsonb_typeof(rec->'assigned_events') = 'array'
   912	           then array(select jsonb_array_elements_text(rec->'assigned_events'))
   913	           else '{}'::text[] end,
   914	      null,
   915	      coalesce((rec->>'created_at')::timestamptz, now())
   916	    )
   917	    on conflict (id) do update set
   918	      name = excluded.name,
   919	      judge_id = excluded.judge_id,
   920	      pin = '',
   921	      assigned_events = excluded.assigned_events,
   922	      current_session_token = null;
   923	
   924	    perform public._fj_upsert_judge_secret(v_id, clean_org_id, v_pin);
   925	    c_judges_created := c_judges_created + 1;
   926	  end loop;
   927	
   928	  for rec in select * from jsonb_array_elements(v_participants) loop
   929	    v_id := (rec->>'id')::uuid;
   930	    if v_mode = 'add' and exists(select 1 from public.participants where id = v_id) then
   931	      continue;
   932	    end if;
   933	
   934	    insert into public.participants (
   935	      id, org_id, identity_id, sl_no, name, school, class_or_age, mobile_number, custom_values, created_at
   936	    ) values (
   937	      v_id, clean_org_id,
   938	      coalesce(rec->>'identity_id', 'P-' || substring(v_id::text from 1 for 6)),
   939	      rec->>'sl_no', coalesce(rec->>'name', 'Participant'),
   940	      rec->>'school', rec->>'class_or_age', rec->>'mobile_number',
   941	      coalesce(rec->'custom_values', '{}'::jsonb),
   942	      coalesce((rec->>'created_at')::timestamptz, now())
   943	    )
   944	    on conflict (id) do update set
   945	      sl_no = excluded.sl_no, name = excluded.name, school = excluded.school,
   946	      class_or_age = excluded.class_or_age, mobile_number = excluded.mobile_number,
   947	      custom_values = excluded.custom_values;
   948	    c_participants_created := c_participants_created + 1;
   949	  end loop;
   950	
   951	  for rec in select * from jsonb_array_elements(v_events) loop
   952	    v_id := (rec->>'id')::uuid;
   953	    if v_id = any(locked_event_ids) then
   954	      c_events_protected := c_events_protected + 1;
   955	      continue;
   956	    end if;
   957	    if v_mode = 'add' and exists(select 1 from public.events where id = v_id) then
   958	      continue;
   959	    end if;
   960	
   961	    insert into public.events (
   962	      id, org_id, name, category, date, status, started_at, elapsed_seconds,
   963	      rubric_schema, assigned_judges, assigned_participants, participant_sl_numbers,
   964	      created_at, updated_at, is_locked
   965	    ) values (
   966	      v_id, clean_org_id, coalesce(rec->>'name', 'Event'), rec->>'category', rec->>'date',
   967	      coalesce(rec->>'status', 'draft'), (rec->>'started_at')::timestamptz,
   968	      coalesce((rec->>'elapsed_seconds')::int, 0), coalesce(rec->'rubric_schema', '[]'::jsonb),
   969	      case when jsonb_typeof(rec->'assigned_judges') = 'array'
   970	           then array(select jsonb_array_elements_text(rec->'assigned_judges'))
   971	           else '{}'::text[] end,
   972	      case when jsonb_typeof(rec->'assigned_participants') = 'array'
   973	           then array(select jsonb_array_elements_text(rec->'assigned_participants'))
   974	           else '{}'::text[] end,
   975	      coalesce(rec->'participant_sl_numbers', '{}'::jsonb),
   976	      coalesce((rec->>'created_at')::timestamptz, now()), now(),
   977	      coalesce((rec->>'is_locked')::boolean, false)
   978	    )
   979	    on conflict (id) do update set
   980	      name = excluded.name, category = excluded.category, date = excluded.date,
   981	      status = excluded.status, rubric_schema = excluded.rubric_schema,
   982	      assigned_judges = excluded.assigned_judges,
   983	      assigned_participants = excluded.assigned_participants,
   984	      participant_sl_numbers = excluded.participant_sl_numbers, updated_at = now();
   985	    c_events_created := c_events_created + 1;
   986	  end loop;
   987	
   988	  for rec in select * from jsonb_array_elements(v_scores) loop
   989	    v_id := (rec->>'id')::uuid;
   990	    if (rec->>'event_id')::uuid = any(locked_event_ids) then
   991	      continue;
   992	    end if;
   993	    if v_mode = 'add' and exists(select 1 from public.scores where id = v_id) then
   994	      continue;
   995	    end if;
   996	
   997	    insert into public.scores (
   998	      id, event_id, judge_id, participant_id, score_payload, total_score, created_at
   999	    ) values (
  1000	      v_id, (rec->>'event_id')::uuid, (rec->>'judge_id')::uuid, (rec->>'participant_id')::uuid,
  1001	      coalesce(rec->'score_payload', '{}'::jsonb), coalesce((rec->>'total_score')::numeric, 0),
  1002	      coalesce((rec->>'created_at')::timestamptz, now())
  1003	    )
  1004	    on conflict (id) do update set
  1005	      score_payload = excluded.score_payload, total_score = excluded.total_score;
  1006	    c_scores_created := c_scores_created + 1;
  1007	  end loop;
  1008	
  1009	  return jsonb_build_object(
  1010	    'ok', true,
  1011	    'dry_run', false,
  1012	    'mode', v_mode,
  1013	    'summary', jsonb_build_object(
  1014	      'events_restored', c_events_created,
  1015	      'events_protected', c_events_protected,
  1016	      'judges_restored', c_judges_created,
  1017	      'participants_restored', c_participants_created,
  1018	      'scores_restored', c_scores_created
  1019	    )
  1020	  );
  1021	end;
  1022	$$;
  1023	
  1024	revoke all on function public.org_restore(text, text, text, jsonb, boolean) from public;
  1025	grant execute on function public.org_restore(text, text, text, jsonb, boolean) to anon, authenticated;
  1026	
  1027	-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  1028	-- 9. SYNC fj_health() WITH PHASE 3 TABLES
  1029	-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  1030	create or replace function public.fj_health()
  1031	returns jsonb
  1032	language plpgsql
  1033	security definer
  1034	set search_path = public
  1035	as $$
  1036	declare
  1037	  v_rls jsonb;
  1038	  v_public_select_count int;
  1039	begin
  1040	  select jsonb_object_agg(relname, relrowsecurity)
  1041	  into v_rls
  1042	  from pg_class
  1043	  join pg_namespace n on n.oid = pg_class.relnamespace
  1044	  where n.nspname = 'public'
  1045	    and relname in (
  1046	      'organizations', 'org_secrets', 'org_login_throttle',
  1047	      'judge_secrets', 'judge_login_throttle', '_fj_internal_secrets',
  1048	      'events', 'judges', 'participants', 'scores'
  1049	    );
  1050	
  1051	  select count(*)
  1052	  into v_public_select_count
  1053	  from pg_policies
  1054	  where schemaname = 'public'
  1055	    and cmd = 'SELECT'
  1056	    and (
  1057	      tablename in ('events', 'judges', 'participants', 'scores', 'judge_secrets', 'judge_login_throttle')
  1058	      or (tablename = 'organizations' and 'anon' = any(roles))
  1059	      or (tablename = 'organizations' and 'public' = any(roles))
  1060	    );
  1061	
  1062	  return jsonb_build_object(
  1063	    'ok', true,
  1064	    'rls_enabled', coalesce(v_rls, '{}'::jsonb),
  1065	    'triggers', jsonb_build_object('roster_guard', true),
  1066	    'public_select_policies', coalesce(v_public_select_count, 0),
  1067	    'pii_locked', true
  1068	  );
  1069	end;
  1070	$$;
  1071	
  1072	revoke all on function public.fj_health() from public;
  1073	grant execute on function public.fj_health() to anon, authenticated;
  1074	
  1075	-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  1076	-- VERIFICATION QUERY (Read-only, returns ONE jsonb cell)
  1077	-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  1078	select jsonb_build_object(
  1079	  'phase3_pii_lock', 'verified',
  1080	  'secret_tables_rls', (
  1081	    select jsonb_object_agg(relname, relrowsecurity)
  1082	    from pg_class
  1083	    join pg_namespace n on n.oid = pg_class.relnamespace
  1084	    where n.nspname = 'public'
  1085	      and relname in ('_fj_internal_secrets', 'judge_secrets', 'judge_login_throttle')
  1086	  ),
  1087	  'judges_total', (select count(*) from public.judges),
  1088	  'judge_secrets_total', (select count(*) from public.judge_secrets),
  1089	  'plaintext_pins_remaining', (select count(*) from public.judges where coalesce(btrim(pin), '') <> ''),
  1090	  'session_tokens_remaining', (select count(*) from public.judges where current_session_token is not null),
  1091	  'anon_pii_column_grants', (
  1092	    select count(*)
  1093	    from information_schema.column_privileges
  1094	    where table_schema = 'public'
  1095	      and grantee in ('anon', 'authenticated')
  1096	      and (
  1097	        (table_name = 'participants' and column_name = 'mobile_number')
  1098	        or (table_name = 'judges' and column_name in ('pin', 'current_session_token'))
  1099	      )
  1100	  ),
  1101	  'rpcs_deployed', (
  1102	    select count(*)
  1103	    from pg_proc p
  1104	    join pg_namespace n on n.oid = p.pronamespace
  1105	    where n.nspname = 'public'
  1106	      and p.proname in (
  1107	        'participants_list', 'judges_list_with_pins', 'read_participants',
  1108	        'read_judges', 'read_org_roster_credentials',
  1109	        'judge_login', 'judge_upsert', 'judge_update', 'org_restore'
  1110	      )
  1111	  ),
  1112	  'invalid_token_checks', jsonb_build_object(
  1113	    'participants_list', public.participants_list('invalid-token', null),
  1114	    'judges_list_with_pins', public.judges_list_with_pins('invalid-token', null)
  1115	  )
  1116	) as phase3_verification;

==================== FILE: sql/15.12-phase4d-simple-sync.sql ====================
     1	-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
     2	-- EPIC 15.12 — PHASE 4D-1: SIMPLE SYNC (MONOTONIC VERSION + DELTAS)
     3	-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
     4	-- Replaces expensive full-table fingerprint hashing (read_org_sync_stamp)
     5	-- and public broadcast channels with an authoritative per-organization
     6	-- monotonic version counter, deletion tombstones, and a single delta RPC:
     7	--   read_org_changes(p_token text, p_since bigint default 0)
     8	--
     9	-- Security & Privacy Guarantees:
    10	--   - Uses _fj_verify_read_token(p_token, null) for organiser & judge auth.
    11	--   - Rejects suspended/deleted orgs and inactive judges automatically.
    12	--   - NEVER returns judges.pin, pin_hash, pin_encrypted, or current_session_token.
    13	--   - NEVER returns participants.mobile_number or organizations.email (for judges).
    14	--   - Enforces blind_judging_enabled & isolated_jury_enabled for judge sessions,
    15	--     and promotes mid-session org-setting flips to full_resync so stale
    16	--     unmasked data is purged from judge caches.
    17	--   - sync_deletions has RLS ON and zero direct anon/authenticated grants.
    18	--
    19	-- IMPORTANT: Does NOT drop read_org_sync_stamp yet inside Supabase until all
    20	-- live clients are on >= 15.12.3 (can be retired in a future cleanup).
    21	-- Ends with a single read-only verification query returning ONE jsonb cell.
    22	-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    23	
    24	-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    25	-- 1. SCHEMA ADDITIONS: VERSION COUNTERS, UPDATED_AT & SCORES.ORG_ID
    26	-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    27	alter table public.organizations
    28	  add column if not exists sync_version bigint not null default 1,
    29	  add column if not exists version bigint not null default 1,
    30	  add column if not exists updated_at timestamptz not null default now();
    31	
    32	alter table public.events
    33	  add column if not exists version bigint not null default 1,
    34	  add column if not exists updated_at timestamptz not null default now();
    35	
    36	alter table public.judges
    37	  add column if not exists version bigint not null default 1,
    38	  add column if not exists updated_at timestamptz not null default now();
    39	
    40	alter table public.participants
    41	  add column if not exists version bigint not null default 1,
    42	  add column if not exists updated_at timestamptz not null default now();
    43	
    44	alter table public.scores
    45	  add column if not exists org_id text,
    46	  add column if not exists version bigint not null default 1,
    47	  add column if not exists updated_at timestamptz not null default now();
    48	
    49	-- Backfill scores.org_id from parent events
    50	update public.scores s
    51	set org_id = e.org_id
    52	from public.events e
    53	where s.event_id = e.id
    54	  and (s.org_id is null or btrim(s.org_id) = '');
    55	
    56	-- Ensure initial versions are valid positive integers
    57	update public.organizations
    58	set sync_version = greatest(coalesce(sync_version, 1), 1),
    59	    version = greatest(coalesce(version, 1), 1),
    60	    updated_at = coalesce(updated_at, created_at, now())
    61	where sync_version is null or sync_version < 1 or version is null or version < 1 or updated_at is null;
    62	
    63	-- Composite org + version indexes for fast O(delta) index scans
    64	create index if not exists idx_events_org_version
    65	  on public.events (upper(org_id), version);
    66	
    67	create index if not exists idx_judges_org_version
    68	  on public.judges (upper(org_id), version);
    69	
    70	create index if not exists idx_participants_org_version
    71	  on public.participants (upper(org_id), version);
    72	
    73	create index if not exists idx_scores_org_version
    74	  on public.scores (upper(org_id), version);
    75	
    76	-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    77	-- 2. DELETION TOMBSTONE LOG (Server-only, RLS ON)
    78	-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    79	create table if not exists public.sync_deletions (
    80	  id bigserial primary key,
    81	  org_id text not null,
    82	  table_name text not null,
    83	  record_id text not null,
    84	  version bigint not null,
    85	  deleted_at timestamptz not null default now()
    86	);
    87	
    88	create index if not exists idx_sync_deletions_org_ver
    89	  on public.sync_deletions (upper(org_id), version);
    90	
    91	create index if not exists idx_sync_deletions_deleted_at
    92	  on public.sync_deletions (deleted_at);
    93	
    94	alter table public.sync_deletions enable row level security;
    95	revoke all on table public.sync_deletions from anon, authenticated, public;
    96	
    97	-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    98	-- 3. MONOTONIC VERSION BUMP HELPER & TRIGGERS
    99	-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   100	create or replace function public._fj_bump_org_sync_version(p_org_id text)
   101	returns bigint
   102	language plpgsql
   103	security definer
   104	set search_path = public, extensions
   105	as $$
   106	declare
   107	  v_clean text := upper(btrim(coalesce(p_org_id, '')));
   108	  v_next bigint;
   109	begin
   110	  if v_clean = '' then
   111	    return 1;
   112	  end if;
   113	
   114	  -- Guard against re-entering _fj_trg_sync_org_upsert on organizations update
   115	  perform set_config('app.fj_bumping_sync', '1', true);
   116	
   117	  update public.organizations
   118	  set sync_version = coalesce(sync_version, 1) + 1
   119	  where upper(org_id) = v_clean
   120	  returning sync_version into v_next;
   121	
   122	  perform set_config('app.fj_bumping_sync', '0', true);
   123	  return coalesce(v_next, 1);
   124	exception
   125	  when others then
   126	    perform set_config('app.fj_bumping_sync', '0', true);
   127	    raise;
   128	end;
   129	$$;
   130	
   131	revoke all on function public._fj_bump_org_sync_version(text) from public, anon, authenticated;
   132	
   133	-- Organization BEFORE INSERT / UPDATE trigger
   134	create or replace function public._fj_trg_sync_org_upsert()
   135	returns trigger
   136	language plpgsql
   137	security definer
   138	set search_path = public, extensions
   139	as $$
   140	begin
   141	  if current_setting('app.fj_bumping_sync', true) = '1' then
   142	    return new;
   143	  end if;
   144	
   145	  if tg_op = 'INSERT' then
   146	    new.sync_version := greatest(coalesce(new.sync_version, 1), 1);
   147	    new.version := new.sync_version;
   148	    new.updated_at := coalesce(new.updated_at, now());
   149	    return new;
   150	  end if;
   151	
   152	  -- Direct / admin / settings update on public.organizations
   153	  if (to_jsonb(new) - 'sync_version' - 'version' - 'updated_at')
   154	     is distinct from
   155	     (to_jsonb(old) - 'sync_version' - 'version' - 'updated_at') then
   156	    new.sync_version := greatest(coalesce(old.sync_version, 1), 1) + 1;
   157	    new.version := new.sync_version;
   158	    new.updated_at := now();
   159	  end if;
   160	
   161	  return new;
   162	end;
   163	$$;
   164	
   165	revoke all on function public._fj_trg_sync_org_upsert() from public, anon, authenticated;
   166	
   167	drop trigger if exists trg_fj_sync_org_version on public.organizations;
   168	create trigger trg_fj_sync_org_version
   169	  before insert or update on public.organizations
   170	  for each row
   171	  execute function public._fj_trg_sync_org_upsert();
   172	
   173	-- Organization AFTER DELETE trigger (cleanup orphaned tombstones)
   174	create or replace function public._fj_trg_sync_org_delete()
   175	returns trigger
   176	language plpgsql
   177	security definer
   178	set search_path = public, extensions
   179	as $$
   180	begin
   181	  delete from public.sync_deletions
   182	  where upper(org_id) = upper(btrim(coalesce(old.org_id, '')));
   183	  return old;
   184	end;
   185	$$;
   186	
   187	revoke all on function public._fj_trg_sync_org_delete() from public, anon, authenticated;
   188	
   189	drop trigger if exists trg_fj_sync_org_delete on public.organizations;
   190	create trigger trg_fj_sync_org_delete
   191	  after delete on public.organizations
   192	  for each row
   193	  execute function public._fj_trg_sync_org_delete();
   194	
   195	-- Child tables (events, judges, participants, scores) BEFORE INSERT / UPDATE trigger
   196	create or replace function public._fj_trg_sync_child_upsert()
   197	returns trigger
   198	language plpgsql
   199	security definer
   200	set search_path = public, extensions
   201	as $$
   202	declare
   203	  v_org_id text;
   204	begin
   205	  if tg_table_name = 'scores' then
   206	    v_org_id := nullif(btrim(coalesce(new.org_id, '')), '');
   207	    if v_org_id is null and new.event_id is not null then
   208	      select e.org_id into v_org_id
   209	      from public.events e
   210	      where e.id = new.event_id
   211	      limit 1;
   212	      new.org_id := v_org_id;
   213	    end if;
   214	  else
   215	    v_org_id := new.org_id;
   216	  end if;
   217	
   218	  new.version := public._fj_bump_org_sync_version(v_org_id);
   219	  new.updated_at := now();
   220	  return new;
   221	end;
   222	$$;
   223	
   224	revoke all on function public._fj_trg_sync_child_upsert() from public, anon, authenticated;
   225	
   226	drop trigger if exists trg_fj_sync_events_version on public.events;
   227	create trigger trg_fj_sync_events_version
   228	  before insert or update on public.events
   229	  for each row
   230	  execute function public._fj_trg_sync_child_upsert();
   231	
   232	drop trigger if exists trg_fj_sync_judges_version on public.judges;
   233	create trigger trg_fj_sync_judges_version
   234	  before insert or update on public.judges
   235	  for each row
   236	  execute function public._fj_trg_sync_child_upsert();
   237	
   238	drop trigger if exists trg_fj_sync_participants_version on public.participants;
   239	create trigger trg_fj_sync_participants_version
   240	  before insert or update on public.participants
   241	  for each row
   242	  execute function public._fj_trg_sync_child_upsert();
   243	
   244	drop trigger if exists trg_fj_sync_scores_version on public.scores;
   245	create trigger trg_fj_sync_scores_version
   246	  before insert or update on public.scores
   247	  for each row
   248	  execute function public._fj_trg_sync_child_upsert();
   249	
   250	-- Child tables AFTER DELETE trigger (bumps org sync_version & logs tombstone)
   251	create or replace function public._fj_trg_sync_child_delete()
   252	returns trigger
   253	language plpgsql
   254	security definer
   255	set search_path = public, extensions
   256	as $$
   257	declare
   258	  v_org_id text;
   259	  v_ver bigint;
   260	begin
   261	  if tg_table_name = 'scores' then
   262	    v_org_id := nullif(btrim(coalesce(old.org_id, '')), '');
   263	    if v_org_id is null and old.event_id is not null then
   264	      select e.org_id into v_org_id
   265	      from public.events e
   266	      where e.id = old.event_id
   267	      limit 1;
   268	    end if;
   269	  else
   270	    v_org_id := old.org_id;
   271	  end if;
   272	
   273	  v_org_id := upper(btrim(coalesce(v_org_id, '')));
   274	  if v_org_id = '' then
   275	    return old;
   276	  end if;
   277	
   278	  -- Skip if parent organization has already been removed
   279	  if not exists (select 1 from public.organizations o where upper(o.org_id) = v_org_id) then
   280	    return old;
   281	  end if;
   282	
   283	  v_ver := public._fj_bump_org_sync_version(v_org_id);
   284	
   285	  insert into public.sync_deletions (org_id, table_name, record_id, version, deleted_at)
   286	  values (v_org_id, tg_table_name, old.id::text, v_ver, now());
   287	
   288	  -- Opportunistic pruning beyond 30-day session horizon
   289	  if random() < 0.02 then
   290	    delete from public.sync_deletions
   291	    where deleted_at < now() - interval '30 days';
   292	  end if;
   293	
   294	  return old;
   295	end;
   296	$$;
   297	
   298	revoke all on function public._fj_trg_sync_child_delete() from public, anon, authenticated;
   299	
   300	drop trigger if exists trg_fj_sync_events_delete on public.events;
   301	create trigger trg_fj_sync_events_delete
   302	  after delete on public.events
   303	  for each row
   304	  execute function public._fj_trg_sync_child_delete();
   305	
   306	drop trigger if exists trg_fj_sync_judges_delete on public.judges;
   307	create trigger trg_fj_sync_judges_delete
   308	  after delete on public.judges
   309	  for each row
   310	  execute function public._fj_trg_sync_child_delete();
   311	
   312	drop trigger if exists trg_fj_sync_participants_delete on public.participants;
   313	create trigger trg_fj_sync_participants_delete
   314	  after delete on public.participants
   315	  for each row
   316	  execute function public._fj_trg_sync_child_delete();
   317	
   318	drop trigger if exists trg_fj_sync_scores_delete on public.scores;
   319	create trigger trg_fj_sync_scores_delete
   320	  after delete on public.scores
   321	  for each row
   322	  execute function public._fj_trg_sync_child_delete();
   323	
   324	-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   325	-- 4. DELTA SYNC RPC: read_org_changes(p_token, p_since)
   326	-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   327	create or replace function public.read_org_changes(
   328	  p_token text,
   329	  p_since bigint default 0
   330	)
   331	returns jsonb
   332	language plpgsql
   333	security definer
   334	set search_path = public, extensions
   335	as $$
   336	declare
   337	  v_auth jsonb;
   338	  v_org_id text;
   339	  v_role text;
   340	  v_judge_id uuid;
   341	  v_org public.organizations;
   342	  v_cur_ver bigint;
   343	  v_req_since bigint;
   344	  v_effective_since bigint;
   345	  v_full_resync boolean := false;
   346	  v_blind boolean := false;
   347	  v_isolated boolean := false;
   348	  v_org_json jsonb := null;
   349	  v_events jsonb := '[]'::jsonb;
   350	  v_judges jsonb := '[]'::jsonb;
   351	  v_participants jsonb := '[]'::jsonb;
   352	  v_scores jsonb := '[]'::jsonb;
   353	  v_deletions jsonb := '[]'::jsonb;
   354	begin
   355	  v_auth := public._fj_verify_read_token(p_token, null);
   356	  if not coalesce((v_auth->>'ok')::boolean, false) then
   357	    return jsonb_build_object(
   358	      'ok', false,
   359	      'error', coalesce(v_auth->>'error', 'unauthorized'),
   360	      'message', 'Session expired or invalid. Please sign in again.'
   361	    );
   362	  end if;
   363	
   364	  v_org_id := v_auth->>'org_id';
   365	  v_role := v_auth->>'role';
   366	  if v_role = 'judge' and nullif(v_auth->>'judge_id', '') is not null then
   367	    v_judge_id := (v_auth->>'judge_id')::uuid;
   368	  end if;
   369	
   370	  select * into v_org
   371	  from public.organizations o
   372	  where upper(o.org_id) = upper(v_org_id)
   373	  limit 1;
   374	
   375	  if v_org.id is null then
   376	    return jsonb_build_object(
   377	      'ok', false,
   378	      'error', 'unauthorized',
   379	      'message', 'Organization not found or unavailable.'
   380	    );
   381	  end if;
   382	
   383	  v_cur_ver := greatest(coalesce(v_org.sync_version, 1), 1);
   384	  v_req_since := greatest(coalesce(p_since, 0), 0);
   385	
   386	  -- Fast unchanged short-circuit when client is already at the latest version
   387	  if v_req_since = v_cur_ver then
   388	    return jsonb_build_object(
   389	      'ok', true,
   390	      'org_id', v_org.org_id,
   391	      'version', v_cur_ver,
   392	      'since', v_req_since,
   393	      'unchanged', true,
   394	      'full_resync', false,
   395	      'organization', null,
   396	      'events', '[]'::jsonb,
   397	      'judges', '[]'::jsonb,
   398	      'participants', '[]'::jsonb,
   399	      'scores', '[]'::jsonb,
   400	      'deletions', '[]'::jsonb
   401	    );
   402	  end if;
   403	
   404	  if v_req_since = 0 or v_req_since > v_cur_ver then
   405	    v_effective_since := 0;
   406	    v_full_resync := true;
   407	  else
   408	    v_effective_since := v_req_since;
   409	  end if;
   410	
   411	  -- If organization row changed for a judge session (e.g. blind_judging_enabled or
   412	  -- isolated_jury_enabled toggled), force full_resync so cached names/scores mask immediately.
   413	  if v_role = 'judge' and v_effective_since > 0 and coalesce(v_org.version, 1) > v_effective_since then
   414	    v_effective_since := 0;
   415	    v_full_resync := true;
   416	  end if;
   417	
   418	  if v_role = 'judge' then
   419	    v_blind := coalesce(v_org.blind_judging_enabled, false);
   420	    v_isolated := coalesce(v_org.isolated_jury_enabled, false);
   421	  end if;
   422	
   423	  -- 1. Organization delta (never expose password; strip organiser email for judges)
   424	  if v_effective_since = 0 or coalesce(v_org.version, 1) > v_effective_since then
   425	    v_org_json := to_jsonb(v_org) - 'password';
   426	    if v_role = 'judge' then
   427	      v_org_json := v_org_json - 'email';
   428	    end if;
   429	  end if;
   430	
   431	  -- 2. Events delta
   432	  select coalesce(jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(e) order by e.created_at desc), '[]'::jsonb)
   433	  into v_events
   434	  from public.events e
   435	  where upper(e.org_id) = upper(v_org_id)
   436	    and (v_effective_since = 0 or coalesce(e.version, 1) > v_effective_since);
   437	
   438	  -- 3. Judges delta (explicit safe columns; NEVER returns pin, pin_hash, pin_encrypted, current_session_token)
   439	  select coalesce(
   440	    jsonb_agg(
   441	      jsonb_build_object(
   442	        'id', j.id,
   443	        'org_id', j.org_id,
   444	        'name', j.name,
   445	        'judge_id', j.judge_id,
   446	        'assigned_events', coalesce(j.assigned_events, '{}'::text[]),
   447	        'created_at', j.created_at,
   448	        'version', j.version,
   449	        'updated_at', j.updated_at
   450	      )
   451	      || case
   452	           when to_jsonb(j) ? 'is_active' then jsonb_build_object('is_active', to_jsonb(j)->'is_active')
   453	           else '{}'::jsonb
   454	         end
   455	      order by j.created_at desc
   456	    ),
   457	    '[]'::jsonb
   458	  )
   459	  into v_judges
   460	  from public.judges j
   461	  where upper(j.org_id) = upper(v_org_id)
   462	    and (v_effective_since = 0 or coalesce(j.version, 1) > v_effective_since);
   463	
   464	  -- 4. Participants delta (explicit safe columns; NEVER returns mobile_number; masks PII in blind mode for judges)
   465	  select coalesce(
   466	    jsonb_agg(
   467	      (
   468	        case
   469	          when v_role = 'judge' and v_blind then
   470	            jsonb_build_object(
   471	              'id', p.id,
   472	              'org_id', p.org_id,
   473	              'identity_id', p.identity_id,
   474	              'sl_no', p.sl_no,
   475	              'name', 'Anonymous',
   476	              'school', '',
   477	              'class_or_age', null,
   478	              'custom_values', '{}'::jsonb,
   479	              'created_at', p.created_at,
   480	              'version', p.version,
   481	              'updated_at', p.updated_at
   482	            )
   483	          else
   484	            jsonb_build_object(
   485	              'id', p.id,
   486	              'org_id', p.org_id,
   487	              'identity_id', p.identity_id,
   488	              'sl_no', p.sl_no,
   489	              'name', p.name,
   490	              'school', p.school,
   491	              'class_or_age', p.class_or_age,
   492	              'custom_values', coalesce(p.custom_values, '{}'::jsonb),
   493	              'created_at', p.created_at,
   494	              'version', p.version,
   495	              'updated_at', p.updated_at
   496	            )
   497	        end
   498	      )
   499	      || case
   500	           when to_jsonb(p) ? 'is_active' then jsonb_build_object('is_active', to_jsonb(p)->'is_active')
   501	           else '{}'::jsonb
   502	         end
   503	      order by p.created_at asc
   504	    ),
   505	    '[]'::jsonb
   506	  )
   507	  into v_participants
   508	  from public.participants p
   509	  where upper(p.org_id) = upper(v_org_id)
   510	    and (v_effective_since = 0 or coalesce(p.version, 1) > v_effective_since);
   511	
   512	  -- 5. Scores delta (organiser sees all org scores; judge sees own scores when blind or isolated)
   513	  select coalesce(jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(s) order by s.created_at asc), '[]'::jsonb)
   514	  into v_scores
   515	  from public.scores s
   516	  where upper(s.org_id) = upper(v_org_id)
   517	    and (v_effective_since = 0 or coalesce(s.version, 1) > v_effective_since)
   518	    and (
   519	      v_role = 'organiser'
   520	      or (not v_blind and not v_isolated)
   521	      or s.judge_id = v_judge_id
   522	    );
   523	
   524	  -- 6. Deletions delta (omitted on full_resync since full snapshot already excludes deleted rows)
   525	  if not v_full_resync and v_effective_since > 0 then
   526	    select coalesce(
   527	      jsonb_agg(
   528	        jsonb_build_object(
   529	          'id', d.id,
   530	          'table_name', d.table_name,
   531	          'record_id', d.record_id,
   532	          'version', d.version,
   533	          'deleted_at', d.deleted_at
   534	        )
   535	        order by d.version asc, d.id asc
   536	      ),
   537	      '[]'::jsonb
   538	    )
   539	    into v_deletions
   540	    from public.sync_deletions d
   541	    where upper(d.org_id) = upper(v_org_id)
   542	      and d.version > v_effective_since;
   543	  end if;
   544	
   545	  return jsonb_build_object(
   546	    'ok', true,
   547	    'org_id', v_org.org_id,
   548	    'version', v_cur_ver,
   549	    'since', v_req_since,
   550	    'unchanged', false,
   551	    'full_resync', v_full_resync,
   552	    'organization', v_org_json,
   553	    'events', v_events,
   554	    'judges', v_judges,
   555	    'participants', v_participants,
   556	    'scores', v_scores,
   557	    'deletions', v_deletions
   558	  );
   559	end;
   560	$$;
   561	
   562	revoke all on function public.read_org_changes(text, bigint) from public;
   563	grant execute on function public.read_org_changes(text, bigint) to anon, authenticated;
   564	
   565	-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   566	-- 5. UPDATE fj_health() TO REPORT PHASE 4D SYNC METADATA
   567	-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   568	create or replace function public.fj_health()
   569	returns jsonb
   570	language plpgsql
   571	security definer
   572	set search_path = public
   573	as $$
   574	declare
   575	  v_rls jsonb;
   576	  v_public_select_count int;
   577	begin
   578	  select jsonb_object_agg(relname, relrowsecurity)
   579	  into v_rls
   580	  from pg_class
   581	  join pg_namespace n on n.oid = pg_class.relnamespace
   582	  where n.nspname = 'public'
   583	    and relname in (
   584	      'organizations', 'org_secrets', 'org_login_throttle',
   585	      'judge_secrets', 'judge_login_throttle', '_fj_internal_secrets',
   586	      'events', 'judges', 'participants', 'scores', 'sync_deletions'
   587	    );
   588	
   589	  select count(*)
   590	  into v_public_select_count
   591	  from pg_policies
   592	  where schemaname = 'public'
   593	    and cmd = 'SELECT'
   594	    and (
   595	      tablename in ('events', 'judges', 'participants', 'scores', 'judge_secrets', 'judge_login_throttle', 'sync_deletions')
   596	      or (tablename = 'organizations' and 'anon' = any(roles))
   597	      or (tablename = 'organizations' and 'public' = any(roles))
   598	    );
   599	
   600	  return jsonb_build_object(
   601	    'ok', true,
   602	    'rls_enabled', coalesce(v_rls, '{}'::jsonb),
   603	    'triggers', jsonb_build_object('roster_guard', true, 'sync_version', true),
   604	    'public_select_policies', coalesce(v_public_select_count, 0),
   605	    'pii_locked', true,
   606	    'delta_sync', true
   607	  );
   608	end;
   609	$$;
   610	
   611	revoke all on function public.fj_health() from public;
   612	grant execute on function public.fj_health() to anon, authenticated;
   613	
   614	-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   615	-- VERIFICATION QUERY (Read-only, returns ONE jsonb cell)
   616	-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   617	select jsonb_build_object(
   618	  'phase4d_simple_sync', 'verified',
   619	  'version_columns_present', (
   620	    select count(*)
   621	    from information_schema.columns
   622	    where table_schema = 'public'
   623	      and (
   624	        (table_name = 'organizations' and column_name in ('sync_version', 'version', 'updated_at'))
   625	        or (table_name in ('events', 'judges', 'participants') and column_name in ('version', 'updated_at'))
   626	        or (table_name = 'scores' and column_name in ('org_id', 'version', 'updated_at'))
   627	      )
   628	  ),
   629	  'scores_missing_org_id', (
   630	    select count(*) from public.scores where org_id is null or btrim(org_id) = ''
   631	  ),
   632	  'sync_deletions_rls', (
   633	    select relrowsecurity
   634	    from pg_class c
   635	    join pg_namespace n on n.oid = c.relnamespace
   636	    where n.nspname = 'public' and c.relname = 'sync_deletions'
   637	  ),
   638	  'sync_deletions_anon_grants', (
   639	    select count(*)
   640	    from information_schema.table_privileges
   641	    where table_schema = 'public'
   642	      and table_name = 'sync_deletions'
   643	      and grantee in ('anon', 'authenticated')
   644	  ),
   645	  'indexes_present', (
   646	    select count(*)
   647	    from pg_indexes
   648	    where schemaname = 'public'
   649	      and indexname in (
   650	        'idx_events_org_version',
   651	        'idx_judges_org_version',
   652	        'idx_participants_org_version',
   653	        'idx_scores_org_version',
   654	        'idx_sync_deletions_org_ver'
   655	      )
   656	  ),
   657	  'triggers_present', (
   658	    select count(*)
   659	    from pg_trigger t
   660	    join pg_class c on c.oid = t.tgrelid
   661	    join pg_namespace n on n.oid = c.relnamespace
   662	    where n.nspname = 'public'
   663	      and not t.tgisinternal
   664	      and t.tgname in (
   665	        'trg_fj_sync_org_version',
   666	        'trg_fj_sync_org_delete',
   667	        'trg_fj_sync_events_version',
   668	        'trg_fj_sync_events_delete',
   669	        'trg_fj_sync_judges_version',
   670	        'trg_fj_sync_judges_delete',
   671	        'trg_fj_sync_participants_version',
   672	        'trg_fj_sync_participants_delete',
   673	        'trg_fj_sync_scores_version',
   674	        'trg_fj_sync_scores_delete'
   675	      )
   676	  ),
   677	  'read_org_changes_security_definer', (
   678	    select p.prosecdef
   679	    from pg_proc p
   680	    join pg_namespace n on n.oid = p.pronamespace
   681	    where n.nspname = 'public' and p.proname = 'read_org_changes'
   682	  ),
   683	  'invalid_token_check', public.read_org_changes('invalid-token', 0),
   684	  'fj_health', public.fj_health()
   685	) as phase4d_verification;
